## Optional experimental tools

### `collaboration.build_portable_research_episode` · 可移植研究 Episode 清单构建器 / Portable Research Episode Manifest Builder

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `d729df5987b1d9cf7f39ad296c6fee9a0565ccd9`.
- Can do: Build one caller-declared portable research episode manifest from explicitly listed local artifacts, computing bounded SHA-256 and byte-count observations without copying files or authorizing work.
- Good for: Constructing a caller-declared portable episode manifest when artifact-version, dependency, execution, review, omission, or fork-mapping records must be assembled from explicit inputs.; Computing SHA-256 and byte-count observations for up to 64 explicitly selected regular files under a caller-chosen root within ignition/.; Preparing a local manifest for the separate existing portable-episode validator while keeping the original artifact files in place.
- Not for: Authenticating files, sources, actors, executions, reviews, timestamps, permissions, or scientific claims.; Copying or importing a package, executing work, resolving authorization, publishing results, or changing repository files.; Replacing the existing manifest validator or the separate FederatedResultReceipt byte verifier.
- Use only if: The caller explicitly supplies a builder request and a workspace root under ignition/, with each selected artifact declared by a safe relative POSIX path.; The task needs manifest construction and current local byte digests; run the existing portable-episode validator separately for schema and cross-reference consistency.; The caller accepts limits of 64 files, 8 MiB per file, 32 MiB total file bytes, and an unauthenticated local read observation.
- Do not use when: Artifacts must be read outside ignition/, require following symlinks, are not regular files, or exceed the declared byte limits.; The task needs source authenticity, a stable snapshot, execution or review authentication, semantic/scientific review, importing, or external actions.; A completed manifest already exists and only its declared relationships need checking; use the existing portable-episode validator.
- Inputs → outputs: One UTF-8 JSON object conforming to toolbox-portable-research-episode-builder-input-r0 on stdin, capped at 262144 bytes, plus a separately supplied explicit workspace-root CLI argument under ignition/. → One bounded deterministic JSON envelope containing a portable-research-episode-r0 manifest, computed SHA-256 and byte-count receipts, typed findings, total artifact bytes read, and explicit non-authorization/non-write/non-authenticity fields., On invalid input or unsafe reads, a typed failure result with no manifest; successful assembly alone does not assert manifest validity.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_BOUNDED_LOCAL_ARTIFACT_HASHING_AND_MANIFEST_ASSEMBLY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads only caller-selected regular files beneath the explicit root using descriptor-based no-follow opens; prints one JSON result to stdout and does not copy or write files..
- Dependencies: ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-portable-research-episode-builder-input-r0.schema.json; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9, jsonschema >=4.18 with Draft 2020-12 support., POSIX O_NOFOLLOW, O_DIRECTORY, and dir_fd support; unsupported platforms fail closed..
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No measured latency, CPU, memory, or analyst-time comparison exists; local file reads are bounded to 32 MiB per invocation.
- Alternatives: `collaboration.validate_portable_research_episode` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The validator consumes an already-built manifest and checks schema/cross-reference consistency without reading artifact paths; this builder assembles the manifest and hashes selected files.; `collaboration.verify_handoff_artifact_files` (SPECIALIZED_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The byte verifier checks files against a FederatedResultReceipt; it does not construct portable-episode dependency, execution, review, omission, or fork-map relations..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Assemble a portable episode manifest and digest receipts from caller-declared local artifact files before handing the result to the separate manifest validator.; Build version/dependency/run/review/omission/fork-map relationships from stable artifact ID and version declarations without copying the selected files..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · Contract inspection and one real task package build distinguish three different input/output contracts. They do not provide matched quality, broad reliability, user-benefit, or cost evidence. Comparators: collaboration.validate_portable_research_episode, collaboration.verify_handoff_artifact_files; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_portable_research_episode.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_verify_handoff_artifact_files.py; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-portable-research-episode-builder-c1-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-portable-research-episode-builder-real-task-evidence-r0.json, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_portable_research_episode.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_portable_research_episode_builder.py; findings: The new tool constructs and hashes a local-file-backed manifest; the existing validator consumes that manifest without file reads, so they form separate producer and validator steps.; The handoff byte verifier uses a different receipt contract and does not assemble portable episode relationships.; No same-task reliability or resource-cost advantage is established; retain uncertainty and keep all three tools within their distinct scopes..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · The real-task manifest passed the separate existing validator, while contract comparison shows distinct input/output responsibilities. No replacement, retirement, or comparative reliability/cost conclusion is supported.
- Migration/rollback: No Current registry operation or existing candidate is migrated. Preserve the manifest validator and FederatedResultReceipt byte verifier for their own input contracts; invoke this tool only when manifest assembly and local file hashing are separately required. / Remove this candidate card, builder, schemas, task data, tests, docs, C1/route evidence, and regenerated identity projections from the isolated branch; preserve committed task and comparison evidence if the executable is retired..

### `collaboration.preflight_receiver_handoff` · 接收端交接预检器 / Receiver Handoff Preflight

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `9dd3c2c9eaac9cfe955c760d5aa2107dbed5e64d`.
- Can do: Check an incoming serialized Federation handoff against its public source receipt, then evaluate receiver capability and workspace-reobservation preconditions without accepting or executing the handoff.
- Good for: Checking that an incoming typed handoff preserves the task, executor, validated completed work, artifacts, external-session pointers, and unresolved items bound by its canonical public result receipt.; Running the existing receiver capability and re-observation preconditions only after source-receipt/bundle projection equality passes.; Returning a machine-readable, read-only preflight before a human or separately authorized runtime decides whether to continue.
- Not for: Authenticating the person, executor, or system that created a receipt; the receipt digest is unkeyed.; Independently observing a workspace, verifying live receiver capabilities, authorizing a takeover, or dispatching/resuming an executor.; Proving that prior actions happened, artifacts are true, work is correct, or any scientific claim is valid.
- Use only if: The task has a serialized public FederatedResultReceipt and FederatedHandoffBundle using the existing strict contract shapes.; The caller treats workspace_reobserved and target_capabilities as caller declarations and preserves execution_authorized=false.; The input is a single UTF-8 JSON object no larger than 262144 bytes on stdin.
- Do not use when: The source receipt authenticity must be cryptographically authenticated or the sender identity is unknown and cannot be separately checked.; A live handoff, workspace read, network call, permission grant, provider action, or automatic takeover is expected.; A current runtime receiver protocol/version negotiation is required; this tool only uses the existing local Federation contract.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON object with schema_version receiver-handoff-preflight-r0, strict FederatedResultReceipt and FederatedHandoffBundle objects, target executor ID/capabilities, caller workspace_reobserved boolean, and observed artifact refs; maximum 262144 bytes. → Bounded JSON status for receipt digest, receipt-to-bundle projection, capability/re-observation preconditions, and typed mismatch findings., Explicit receipt_authenticity=NOT_ESTABLISHED_UNKEYED_DIGEST, execution_authorized=false, and runtime_enforcement_status=NOT_EVALUATED.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_SINGLE_HANDOFF_AND_LOCAL_FEDERATION_CONTRACTS` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/agent_federation/approval_handoff.py, ignition/agent_federation/contracts.py` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads one bounded stdin object and local Federation code; writes one JSON result to stdout only..
- Dependencies: ignition/agent_federation/approval_handoff.py, ignition/agent_federation/contracts.py; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9, Local Ignition agent_federation package; standard library only..
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No measured CPU, memory, stable latency, or matched cost profile has been collected.
- Alternatives: `collaboration.validate_agent_episode_binding` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): That tool checks caller-declared multi-agent role, context, channel, permission, write-domain, and work-unit relationships. This tool checks serialized Federation receipt-to-handoff projection and receiver preconditions..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Before receiver takeover, reject a validly shaped handoff whose source-bound artifact, task/executor identity, validated work, session pointer, or unresolved items no longer match its public result receipt..

### `collaboration.select_handoff_advisories` · 交接提醒筛选器 / Handoff Advisory Selector

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `02419af73fd71611ba505e0c00145ed0bd2905b7`.
- Can do: Create a review-only suggestion for omitting a unique optional handoff advisory only when exact text, ordered source refs, task scope, and caller-declared receiver-state revision match caller-declared receiver context; never transform or send a handoff.

- Not for: Deciding semantic necessity, compressing prose, ranking messages, choosing source material, refreshing shared state, or escalating a receiver.; Authenticating sender, receiver, receiver state, message classification, or caller-declared context digests.; Editing or transmitting a handoff, authorizing actions, proving task completion, or claiming token, latency, quality, or cost savings.

- Inputs → outputs: One UTF-8 JSON object matching handoff-advisory-selection-r0; max 262144 stdin bytes, 64 messages, 128 context digests, 8192 characters per message, and 16 source refs per message. → One JSON result with retained message IDs and suggested optional-advisory omission IDs, exact digest basis, and a narrow omitted-content UTF-8 byte count., Explicit caller-context-not-authenticated, semantic_necessity=NOT_EVALUATED, handoff_transformed=false, send_authorized=false, and execution_authorized=false.

- Route default: `false` · conditions: Remain excluded unless this exact candidate ID is explicitly allowlisted for a caller-supplied portable research episode manifest preflight task.; Use only when artifact-version, execution, review, omission or read-only fork-map relationships are required; do not add it to unrelated tasks.; A route proposal never invokes the validator, authenticates manifest fields or authorizes package import, execution, research or publication..
- Claim ceiling: Checks only schema and caller-declared episode, artifact-version, execution, review, omission, and fork-map relations. It does not authenticate any source, actor, digest, execution, review, or permission; read package bytes; import or fork files; execute code; validate scientific meaning; or authorize research, publication, or external action.

### `collaboration.verify_handoff_artifact_files` · 交接产物字节核验器 / Handoff Artifact Byte Verifier

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `bd8b7062be93e311734143df4611e943eccee1f8`.
- Can do: Compare bounded bytes from local files under an explicit ignition/ workspace root with the SHA-256 values in a typed FederatedResultReceipt, without returning artifact contents or authorizing a handoff.
- Good for: Checking whether the current local bytes of receipt-referenced artifacts under a caller-selected ignition/ workspace root match the receipt's declared SHA-256 values.; Hashing bounded regular files, including non-UTF-8 and binary artifacts, without returning their contents.; Adding a local byte observation before a separate receiver or reviewer decision when an existing handoff preflight only checks declarations and receipt-to-bundle binding.
- Not for: Authenticating the receipt, its author, executor, or source; its self-digest is unkeyed.; Proving artifact provenance, stable state after the read, task completion, semantic correctness, scientific validity, or receiver authorization.; Reading paths outside ignition/, contacting a provider, dispatching or accepting a handoff, writing files, or enforcing runtime isolation.
- Use only if: The caller supplies one valid FederatedResultReceipt with 1 to 64 artifact references and an explicit workspace root under ignition/.; Every artifact reference is a safe relative POSIX path under that selected root, and each file is expected to be a regular file no larger than 8 MiB within the 32 MiB total byte budget.; The caller accepts an unkeyed digest comparison and preserves execution_authorized=false as well as runtime_enforcement_status=NOT_EVALUATED.
- Do not use when: Receipt provenance or sender identity must be authenticated, or the task needs a trusted source or stable snapshot guarantee.; The artifacts are outside ignition/, exceed the declared count/byte limits, or require following symlinks or reading non-regular files.; The requested outcome requires semantic review, task correctness, a handoff decision, permission grant, network action, or scientific acceptance.
- Inputs → outputs: One UTF-8 JSON object with schema_version handoff-artifact-byte-check-r0 and a valid FederatedResultReceipt containing artifact refs, plus a separately supplied workspace-root CLI argument under ignition/; stdin is capped at 262144 bytes. → Per-artifact expected and observed SHA-256 statuses, byte counts, and typed read/path/size failures for bounded regular-file bytes; no file contents are returned., Explicit receipt_authenticity=NOT_ESTABLISHED_UNKEYED_DIGEST, execution_authorized=false, and runtime_enforcement_status=NOT_EVALUATED.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_BOUNDED_LOCAL_ARTIFACT_HASHING` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads only caller-referenced regular-file bytes under the explicit workspace root with bounded descriptor-based opens; prints one bounded JSON result to stdout..
- Dependencies: ignition/agent_federation/contracts.py; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9, Local Ignition agent_federation.contracts types; otherwise standard library only., POSIX O_NOFOLLOW, O_DIRECTORY, and dir_fd support; unsupported platforms fail closed..
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No measured CPU, memory, latency, or matched alternative cost profile has been collected; per-invocation file reads are capped at 32 MiB.
- Alternatives: `collaboration.preflight_receiver_handoff` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The receiver preflight checks receipt-to-bundle projection and caller-declared receiver preconditions without reading referenced file bytes; this candidate checks current bounded local bytes without deciding takeover readiness.; `knowledge.assemble_context_packet` (SPECIALIZED_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The context packet also checks current file digests but requires UTF-8 text context, line ranges, and obligation mapping and emits excerpts; this candidate is receipt-native, hashes binary artifacts, and emits no file contents..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Verify receipt-bound bytes of binary or non-UTF-8 artifacts under a caller-selected ignition/ workspace without returning artifact contents.; Add current local-byte digest comparison to a Federation handoff review while leaving authenticity, provenance, and takeover decisions to separate authority..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · Matched synthetic controls show that the receiver preflight accepts caller-declared artifact refs without checking changed local bytes, while the context packet hashes UTF-8 context files but classifies the same binary artifact as SOURCE_NOT_UTF8. The candidate returns DIGEST_MISMATCH for the first case and DIGEST_MATCH for the binary case without emitting file contents. No real-task quality, broad reliability, cost, authenticity, provenance, or stable-snapshot conclusion is established. Comparators: collaboration.preflight_receiver_handoff, knowledge.assemble_context_packet; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-handoff-artifact-byte-check-c1-evidence-r0.json, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_context_packet.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_handoff_artifact_file_verifier.py, ignition/tests/test_approval_handoff.py; findings: The receiver preflight and byte verifier serve consecutive, distinct handoff stages: declaration consistency first, current local byte comparison only when separately requested.; The context packet has a distinct text-context and obligation-mapping output; its UTF-8 excerpt behavior does not cover binary artifacts or digest-only handling without content output.; Retain both existing tools and add this optional specialization; evidence does not support replacing or retiring any current or candidate tool, and cost remains unknown..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · A synthetic matched comparison supports a separate binary-capable, no-content artifact-byte check; it does not establish that the receiver preflight or context packet should be retired or replaced.
- Migration/rollback: No Current registry operation or existing candidate is migrated. Keep the receiver preflight for receipt-to-bundle and receiver-declaration checks; add this tool only when actual local artifact-byte comparison is required.; Use the context packet when a task needs UTF-8 excerpts and structural obligation linkage; do not route those tasks here merely to obtain a digest. / Remove this candidate card, entrypoint, docs, fixtures, tests, comparison/route evidence, and regenerate toolbox identity projections on the isolated branch; preserve prior evidence commits for history..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_verify_handoff_artifact_files.py --stdin --workspace-root <path-under-ignition>`
- Fallback: If any ref, root, size, or digest check fails, stop before receiver takeover and reconcile the receipt, workspace, and artifact bytes. Use a separate provenance/authenticity process when trust is required; never infer authorization from a digest match.

### `foundation.batch_resolve_current_asset_references` · Current 资产身份批量解析 / Batch Current asset identity resolver

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `983c0a43603bf7e6e786718266c51cd89a8c27ec`.
- Can do: Resolve an ordered batch of exact references against the checked-out Current canonical identity, alias, correction, claim, and legacy-mapping authorities; emit a source-hashed receipt to stdout.

- Not for: Fuzzy, semantic, natural-language, web, or cross-repository identity search.; Changing identities, repairing source data, or writing results into the repository.; Establishing scientific truth, proof, external validity, or evidence quality.

- Do not use when: The task requires fresh external sources, fuzzy matching, or resolution beyond the checked-out repository snapshot.; An ambiguous or unresolved result must be automatically selected or corrected.; The task asks for scientific adjudication, proof, or evidence validation.
- Inputs → outputs: JSON stdin object with an ordered nonempty array of exact reference strings → input-ordered canonical identity resolution rows, authority and resolver SHA-256 receipt
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_REPOSITORY_SNAPSHOT` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/foundation/resolve_current_canonical_asset.py, ignition/data/foundation/function-assets/identity-cards.jsonl, ignition/data/foundation/nonfunction-claims/claim-registry.jsonl, ignition/data/governance/knowledge-experience/alias-index.jsonl, ignition/data/foundation/function-assets/corrections.jsonl, ignition/data/foundation/mappings/legacy-mappings.jsonl` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Writes the result only to stdout; malformed input and unavailable authority are reported to stderr with nonzero status; no repository files are created or changed..
- Dependencies: ignition/tools/foundation/resolve_current_canonical_asset.py, ignition/data/foundation/function-assets/identity-cards.jsonl, ignition/data/foundation/nonfunction-claims/claim-registry.jsonl, ignition/data/governance/knowledge-experience/alias-index.jsonl, ignition/data/foundation/function-assets/corrections.jsonl, ignition/data/foundation/mappings/legacy-mappings.jsonl; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · One warm-cache two-reference task observed 1009.003 ms and 86738175 declared authority bytes for batch, versus 1676.789 ms and 173476350 bytes for two single-reference calls; this is not a stable benchmark or billed cost.
- Alternatives: `foundation.resolve_current_asset` (COMPLEMENTARY_SINGLE_REFERENCE_TOOL): The Current operation exposes the single-reference resolver contract; this experimental CLI adds ordered multi-reference input and a batch receipt while reusing that resolver algorithm..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Resolve multiple ordered references against one Current repository snapshot in one invocation with one resolver and authority-hash receipt; the single-reference resolver remains appropriate for a one-reference task..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · One controlled local two-reference identity-resolution task comparing two Current single-reference calls, one optional batch call, and a forced full-record read after the task was already complete. These are not stable latency, physical I/O, or billed-cost estimates.; The router selected only the batch tool when it was explicitly allowlisted; the full-record reader was not selected because it added no required output. No replacement or retirement is supported by this task.; In one exact-T2 task requiring the full stored record, the batch resolver returned the same verified record digest but only a resolution row; the full-card reader alone met the complete-record output contract. This supports complementary scope, not batch retirement or general comparative quality..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · Matched evidence supports an optional ordered multi-reference batch use case without replacing the existing Current single-reference resolver; the candidate remains experimental and default-off.
- Migration/rollback: No automatic migration or default-routing change is inferred; use the batch candidate only when one ordered multi-reference snapshot and its receipt are useful, and retain the Current single-reference resolver for one-reference tasks. / Remove the candidate entry and wrapper from the isolated toolbox branch, regenerate the identity and human-directory projections, and preserve prior commits and evidence..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_batch_resolve_current_assets.py --stdin (stdin JSON: {"references":[...]})`
- Fallback: For one reference, use the existing foundation.resolve_current_asset operation or ignition/tools/foundation/resolve_current_canonical_asset.py --reference; if this batch tool is unavailable, process references individually and preserve each unresolved or ambiguous status.

### `foundation.read_current_asset_card` · Current Foundation 身份卡读取器 / Current Foundation record reader

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `80b1d6ebd20ec7107ff158d8f75d76f8cfb7dbd6`.
- Can do: Read one exact canonical ID from the checked-out Current function-identity or non-function-claim registry; return the complete stored record and verify its record SHA-256.
- Good for: Read the complete current record after the caller already has an exact canonical ID.; Inspect the record kind, authority fields, evidence references, use boundaries, and verified record digest in one deterministic JSON result.
- Not for: Discovering IDs, resolving aliases or historical references, fuzzy search, or selecting between ambiguous records.; Checking that linked source files still support the record, validating scientific claims, or establishing proof or external validity.; Changing registries or writing a dossier into the repository.
- Use only if: The caller supplies one exact canonical ID already listed in a Current registry.; The function identity and non-function claim registries are available in the same checked-out snapshot.; The caller accepts a full record payload on stdout and record-level SHA-256 verification only.
- Do not use when: The input is a title, alias, historical path, natural-language query, or uncertain identifier.; The task needs fresh external evidence, independent source-anchor checking, scientific adjudication, or an authority upgrade.; The caller needs only a compact yes/no identity resolution; use the existing resolver instead.
- Inputs → outputs: JSON stdin object containing exactly one exact canonical_id string → complete Current function-identity card or non-function claim record, verified record digest and hashes of both registry snapshots
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_REPOSITORY_SNAPSHOT` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/data/foundation/function-assets/identity-cards.jsonl, ignition/data/foundation/nonfunction-claims/claim-registry.jsonl` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Writes the record result only to stdout; invalid input or unavailable/corrupt authorities return a nonzero status and no partial stdout..
- Dependencies: ignition/data/foundation/function-assets/identity-cards.jsonl, ignition/data/foundation/nonfunction-claims/claim-registry.jsonl; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · Both checked-out JSONL registries are scanned per call; latency and memory have not been benchmarked.
- Alternatives: `foundation.batch_resolve_current_asset_references` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The batch resolver accepts aliases and historical references but returns bounded resolution rows; this reader accepts one canonical ID and returns its full current registry record..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Read the full exact Current registry row and verify its stored digest when a task explicitly requires the record's complete attributes or claim boundaries..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · One exact T2 task required the complete stored identity card plus verified record digest; compared the current single-reference resolver, the batch resolver, and this full-card reader under the same checkout and read-only conditions. Comparators: foundation.batch_resolve_current_asset_references, foundation.resolve_current_asset; implementation refs: ignition/tools/foundation/resolve_current_canonical_asset.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_batch_resolve_current_assets.py; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-current-asset-full-record-c1-comparison-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-current-asset-t2-real-task-outcome-r1.json, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_read_current_asset_card.py; findings: All three tools resolved exact canonical ID T2 and returned the same record digest; only the full-card reader returned the complete stored record and verified its digest, satisfying this task's output contract.; The prior T2 identity-resolution task left the reader unselected because full record content was not required; use resolution tools for that narrower contract.; Single-run wall times were 917.807 ms, 995.574 ms, and 970.179 ms, with stdout sizes of 961, 3035, and 34727 bytes respectively. These are one-shot observations, not stable cost or performance estimates..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · The matched full-record task required output that the identity resolvers omit; retain only as a narrow optional/default-off reader and do not route it for identity-only tasks.
- Migration/rollback: Use this reader only when the task needs the full exact Current row and its verified stored digest; use single or batch resolution when only identity resolution is required.; Do not replace the current single-reference or batch-resolution contracts with this reader; its complete output is unnecessary and much larger for resolution-only tasks. / Remove the candidate entry and wrapper from the isolated toolbox branch, regenerate the identity and human-directory projections, and preserve the comparison record and prior source history.; If full-record content or digest verification regresses, restore the previous wrapper and candidate card and rerun its focused checks..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_read_current_asset_card.py --stdin (stdin JSON: {"canonical_id":"<exact Current ID>"})`
- Fallback: Use foundation.batch_resolve_current_asset_references to confirm exact Current identity resolution; for full record content, read the authority row through a separately approved repository reader and verify its record_sha256.

### `function_os.run_bounded_symbolic_spec` · 有界符号函数规格执行器 / Bounded symbolic FunctionSpec runner

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `toolbox-wrapper-0.1.0; Function OS 0.2.1rc0 candidate` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `bcb1537700d14acff651aabb5ddbe7f311561c27`.
- Can do: Run one explicitly supplied, single-output pure symbolic FunctionSpec through Function OS N1-N7 with bounded expression size, arithmetic intermediates, and numeric input magnitude.
- Good for: Run one declared deterministic symbolic function with one output and 1 to 16 explicitly supplied numeric or boolean inputs.; Inspect the N1-N7 execution status, in-memory artifact integrity report, trace, and N7 internal consistency checks for a bounded specification.
- Not for: Executing untrusted or malicious code, acting as an isolated security sandbox, or processing arbitrary Python expressions.; Scientific adjudication, proof, empirical validation, external truth claims, or recognition that a function models reality.; Stateful or I/O effects, multiple outputs, exponentiation, calls, attributes, N8 routing, N9 registry writes, or persisted artifacts.
- Use only if: Python 3.10 or newer is available and the checked-out Function OS v0.2 source and wrapper are intact.; The caller supplies a single symbolic FunctionSpec with exactly one output, exactly pure effects, 1 to 16 declared inputs, and one output equality postcondition.; The UTF-8 JSON request is at most 16 KiB; expressions are at most 512 characters, 64 AST nodes, and depth 16; numeric inputs/constants are at most 10^12 in magnitude and arithmetic intermediates/results at most 10^100.
- Do not use when: Inputs or expressions are adversarial and require operating-system isolation or a security boundary.; The specification needs exponentiation, calls, attributes, strings, collections, multiple outputs, non-pure effects, or values beyond the declared bounds.; The task requires persistence, registry admission, source-backed evidence, scientific truth, proof, or external validation.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object containing exactly one bounded FunctionSpec object and one exact input-value object → N1-N7 execution status and single declared output when successful, in-memory artifact integrity report, execution trace, and N7 internal consistency result, source-tree and wrapper source digests
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_REPOSITORY_SOURCE_AND_STDIN` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_function_os_evaluator.py, ignition/function-os-candidate/v0.2/function_os/, ignition/function-os-candidate/v0.2/v0.2-scope-contract.json, ignition/function-os-candidate/v0.2/pyproject.toml` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Writes the bounded JSON result to stdout; execution artifacts and traces remain in process memory and N9 is not invoked., The wrapper disables Python bytecode writes before loading Function OS modules..
- Dependencies: ignition/function-os-candidate/v0.2/function_os/, ignition/function-os-candidate/v0.2/v0.2-scope-contract.json, ignition/function-os-candidate/v0.2/pyproject.toml; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.10 standard library; no third-party runtime packages.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · Execution is local and bounded by request and AST limits, but no latency or memory benchmark has been established.
- Alternatives: none mapped.
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: not established.
- Comparison evidence: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · Existing evidence tests this candidate in isolation; no paired same-task comparison against its mapped alternatives has been recorded for C1. Comparators: none registered; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_function_os_evaluator.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_function_os_evaluator.py; findings: No replacement, unique-use, or retirement conclusion is established; preserve the current optional/default-off disposition pending a task-paired comparison where overlap matters..
- Deprecation/retirement: `ACTIVE` · The optional experimental entry remains available and default-off; comparative retirement status is unknown.
- Migration/rollback: No automatic migration is inferred; preserve this exact input/output contract until a same-task comparison supports specialization or replacement. / Remove the candidate entry and wrapper from the isolated toolbox branch, regenerate the identity and human-directory projections, and preserve prior commits and evidence..

- Fallback: Use the checked-in Function OS N1-N7 tests on Python 3.10 or newer and inspect the exact specification and expected outputs manually; do not substitute eval, a general Python runner, or a scientific-validity claim.
- Evidence: `REAL_TOOL_TEST` · source refs `ignition/tools/toolbox_function_os_evaluator.py` (BOUNDED_READ_ONLY_TOOL_ENTRYPOINT), `ignition/function-os-candidate/v0.2/function_os/` (FUNCTION_OS_N1_TO_N7_IMPLEMENTATION), `ignition/function-os-candidate/v0.2/v0.2-scope-contract.json` (UPSTREAM_SCOPE_AND_NON_GOAL_CONTRACT), `ignition/function-os-candidate/v0.2/pyproject.toml` (UPSTREAM_VERSION_LICENSE_AND_RUNTIME_METADATA), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_function_os_evaluator.py` (C1_FUNCTIONAL_EVIDENCE_REFERENCE); focused checks [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_function_os_evaluator.py](tests/test_toolbox_function_os_evaluator.py); negative controls: Calls, attributes, exponentiation, multiple outputs, non-pure effects, oversized inputs, and oversized intermediate arithmetic are rejected.; Duplicate JSON keys and requests over 16 KiB return nonzero with no partial stdout; division by zero is rejected by the bounded arithmetic preflight.; Precondition failure is preserved; N9 registry writes are never invoked; a passing N7 status is explicitly limited to internal consistency..
- Route default: `false` · conditions: Include only through an explicit experimental-tool allowlist when the task supplies a bounded pure symbolic FunctionSpec and asks for its local N1-N7 execution report.; A route proposal never runs the function; the caller separately supplies the complete FunctionSpec and inputs to the CLI.; Never select for arbitrary code, scientific proof, truth, registry admission, or effectful execution..
- Claim ceiling: A bounded local execution report for one pure symbolic FunctionSpec through Function OS N1-N7, with internal artifact/trace consistency checks only; it does not establish specification truth, empirical or scientific correctness, proof, external validation, security isolation, registry admission, or publication status.

### `governance.preflight_declared_permission_delta` · 声明权限差分预检器 / Declared Permission Delta Preflight

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field grants no Owner or security authority.` · source blob `b09cae867ca21b24478c0bd0bb53488ebb02a707`.
- Can do: Compare two caller-supplied declared permission snapshots for exact (capability, scope) string additions and removals, without interpreting or applying policy semantics.
- Good for: Listing exact declared (capability, scope) grant strings added to or removed from a candidate snapshot when both snapshots name the same policy and subject.; Failing closed on unsupported capability labels, duplicate grants, malformed declarations, unknown fields, oversized input, or mismatched comparison bindings.; Producing a structural reviewer aid without reading a policy file, inspecting a runtime, applying a policy, or granting permission.
- Not for: Installing or enforcing OpenShell or any other sandbox, kernel, syscall, network, or filesystem policy.; Authenticating snapshots, resolving inherited or effective permissions, wildcard meanings, path containment, host reachability, or policy precedence.; Granting or revoking permission, approving an external action, or establishing a security finding or security improvement.
- Use only if: The caller explicitly selects this optional tool for two non-sensitive caller-supplied declarations.; Both declarations use the exact versioned schema and have equal policy_id and subject_id values.; The caller treats scopes as exact opaque labels and reviews any reported change independently.
- Do not use when: The task needs actual sandbox state, effective permission calculation, policy enforcement, or an authorization decision.; The input contains secrets, sensitive paths or identifiers, or raw policy material that must not be echoed in output.; The caller expects wildcard, hierarchy, filesystem, DNS, or network semantics to be inferred from scope strings.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin request matching ignition/schemas/operations/declared-permission-delta-r0.schema.json: two declared grant snapshots with the same policy_id and subject_id. → Exact string-set differences listing caller-declared (capability, scope) grants added to and removed from the candidate snapshot., Review-required status and explicit source-authenticity, effective-policy, enforcement, permission-grant, and Owner-authorization ceilings.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_BOUNDED_CALLER_SUPPLIED_STDIN` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `none` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads at most 131073 bytes from stdin and emits one deterministic JSON report to stdout., Does not open repository, temporary, policy, provider, or output files; it makes no network requests and changes no policy..
- Dependencies: ignition/schemas/operations/declared-permission-delta-r0.schema.json; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · Input is capped at 128 KiB and 256 grants per snapshot; no latency or memory profile has been calibrated.
- Alternatives: `ignition.adaptive_toolbox_route_planner` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The route planner proposes operations from task input/output types and validates candidate permission-card shape; it does not compare before/after declared policy snapshots.; `maintenance.compare_audit_findings` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The audit comparator reconciles two finding sets under a shared target; it does not calculate exact capability/scope grant set differences..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Preflight the literal declared-grant delta between two caller-provided policy snapshots for the same policy and subject..
- Comparison evidence: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · Low-cost contract review against the route planner and audit-finding comparator only; no same-task outcome or security-benefit comparison was run. Comparators: ignition.adaptive_toolbox_route_planner, maintenance.compare_audit_findings; implementation refs: ignition/tools/operations/plan_ignition_operation_run.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_audit_finding_comparator.py; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-declared-permission-delta-c1-evidence-r0.json; findings: The route planner proposes task operations and checks declared candidate-card permissions; it does not report exact grant-set changes between caller snapshots.; The audit comparator compares declared review findings rather than policy grants.; No evidence supports replacement, security effectiveness, lower review cost, or retirement of either neighboring tool; retain comparison uncertainty..
- Deprecation/retirement: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · New experimental specialization with no paired outcome comparison; retain no replacement or retirement claim.
- Migration/rollback: No Current operation, registry, route, policy, or sandbox is changed; callers can use this optional comparator without changing their policy format. / Remove the optional card, comparator, schema, docs, fixture, tests, and evidence from the isolated branch; retain commit history and negative-control evidence..

### `ignition.adaptive_toolbox_route_planner` · 点火工具组合路由预览 / Ignition toolbox route preview

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `d7881daae28a3d82612ceadb78d77bcd879bfe83`.
- Can do: Select a sufficient set of registered operations for exact input and output requirements, optionally considering explicitly allowlisted experimental cards and exact caller-supplied network hosts.
- Good for: Planning routes from explicit input types and required output types.; Showing when a task needs one operation, complementary operations, or remains uncovered.; Keeping experimental tools opt-in and returning an explicit incomplete result when no declared route covers a requirement.; Keeping fixed-host network candidates excluded unless the typed profile explicitly lists every exact required DNS host.
- Not for: Inferring the task contract from unstructured natural language.; Calling, dispatching, or executing selected operations.; Granting permissions, Owner approval, or scientific authority.; Claiming measured cost optimality or a globally minimal set.; Authenticating the caller authority behind an allowed_network_hosts value or authorizing a network request.
- Use only if: The caller supplies exact input types, required outputs, allowed modes, and a nonblank Current reference string; the planner echoes that string without resolving it.; The operation registry and pinned experimental candidate-card source are present.; Optional allowed_network_hosts entries are caller-supplied route context only and never grant authority; actual invocation and permissions are checked separately.
- Do not use when: Task requirements cannot be expressed with the declared input/output types.; The caller needs actual tool execution or a scientific conclusion rather than a route proposal.; The current operation registry no longer matches the candidate-card source pin.
- Inputs → outputs: typed toolbox route profile on stdin, optional allowed_network_hosts exact DNS-name list; omitted means no network hosts allowed → typed route proposal, covered and unmet output list, candidate rejection reasons, caller-supplied network-host context and its unverified binding status
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_REPOSITORY_SNAPSHOT` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/data/operations/ignition-operation-capability-registry-r1.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-candidate-cards-r0.json` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: stdout only.
- Dependencies: ignition/data/operations/ignition-operation-capability-registry-r1.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-candidate-cards-r0.json, ignition/tools/operations/classify_ignition_run_mode.py; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · The selection run is local, but no calibrated latency, memory, or compute-cost comparison exists.
- Alternatives: `repository.apply_iteration_method` (NOT_EQUIVALENT): The existing single-operation lifecycle planner resolves one declared operation; this preview selects a typed set and still does not execute it..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: not established.
- Comparison evidence: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · Existing evidence tests this candidate in isolation; no paired same-task comparison against its mapped alternatives has been recorded for C1. Comparators: none registered; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/tools/operations/plan_ignition_operation_run.py, ignition/tests/test_ignition_operation_lifecycle.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py; findings: No replacement, unique-use, or retirement conclusion is established; preserve the current optional/default-off disposition pending a task-paired comparison where overlap matters..
- Deprecation/retirement: `ACTIVE` · The optional experimental entry remains available and default-off; comparative retirement status is unknown.
- Migration/rollback: No automatic migration is inferred; preserve this exact input/output contract until a same-task comparison supports specialization or replacement. / Remove the candidate entry and wrapper from the isolated toolbox branch, regenerate the identity and human-directory projections, and preserve prior commits and evidence..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/operations/plan_ignition_operation_run.py --route-request`
- Fallback: If exact typed coverage is unavailable, return INCOMPLETE and use the existing single-operation planner or report the missing capability; never guess an operation from natural-language similarity.

### `ignition.select_bounded_thinking_operation` · 有界思考操作选择器 / Bounded Thinking Operation Selector

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field grants no Owner, executor, or scientific authority.` · source blob `04d0b7e8036e77b8419650d845125d571f772029`.
- Can do: From caller-declared state of an already bounded run, recommend one next thinking-operation class or a terminal STOP; it neither chooses a Goal nor selects or executes concrete tools.
- Good for: Proposing one next thinking-operation class from explicit authority, frame, capability, freshness, verification, decomposition, obligation, failure-history and budget state inside an already bounded run.; Returning explicit STOP dispositions when a required operation is unavailable, the declared budget is insufficient, expected value is not positive, or a required obligation remains unresolved.; Re-evaluating after the caller records an observable operation result, including a new verification obligation revealed by a freshness read.
- Not for: Selecting or changing Intent, Goal, authority, the task frame, the toolbox route, or the executor.; Executing READ_MORE, VERIFY, escalation, owner contact, or any other operation; a proposal is not an invocation or permission grant.; Reading or exposing hidden chain-of-thought, establishing source truth, completing a Goal, or upgrading scientific evidence.; Replacing the adaptive toolbox route planner, which selects concrete tools for typed input/output requirements.
- Use only if: The caller has already admitted a bounded run and supplies a schema-valid, current caller-declared state with explicit references and required outputs.; Each non-STOP candidate has caller-declared positive expected value, abstract cost, required capabilities, and a return condition; cost units are planning inputs, not measured resource costs.; The caller separately authorizes and performs any suggested operation, records its observable outcome, and submits a new state for reconsideration.
- Do not use when: Goal/authority/frame state is unknown, stale, untrusted, or cannot be declared without inventing facts.; The request expects the selector to execute a tool, grant permission, contact an Owner, decide truth, or complete an obligation automatically.; The task requires calibrated cost estimates, live-model metacognition, or production-level effectiveness claims.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object matching toolbox-bounded-thinking-operation-input-r0; one bounded run with explicit task refs, state/obligations, available operation/capability lists, and candidate assessments; maximum 262144 bytes. → One thinking-operation proposal READ_MORE, VERIFY, DECOMPOSE, REFRAME_OR_CLARIFY, CAPACITY_OR_EXECUTOR_ESCALATE, REQUEST_OWNER_DECISION, or STOP with a bounded terminal disposition., Structured reasons and refs, expected state delta/value/cost declarations when present, preserved first failure, and fixed no-authority/no-execution flags.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_LOCAL_SCHEMA_READ` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-bounded-thinking-operation-input-r0.schema.json, ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-bounded-thinking-operation-output-r0.schema.json` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads stdin and the two local schemas; writes one JSON result to stdout only..
- Dependencies: ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-bounded-thinking-operation-input-r0.schema.json, ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-bounded-thinking-operation-output-r0.schema.json; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9, jsonschema with Draft 2020-12 support.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · One local process timing is recorded but is not a calibrated latency, compute, memory, or cost benchmark; the input cost bands are caller-declared.
- Alternatives: `ignition.adaptive_toolbox_route_planner` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The existing planner selects a sufficient set of concrete tools for typed inputs and outputs; this selector chooses the next thinking-operation class inside an already bounded run. Neither performs the other’s job..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Select one next thinking-operation class from caller-declared within-run state without selecting a Goal or concrete tool set..
- Comparison evidence: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · One bounded tool-admission task was observed, but the compared systems emit different output types and operate at different layers; broad quality, cost, reliability, and maintenance comparisons remain unknown. ### `knowledge.assemble_context_packet` · 有界上下文包组装器 / Bounded Context Packet Assembler

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `1c6e6f5fdf4ceb2ef0f75d17dd0354c9b8daa301`.
- Can do: Assemble caller-selected repository excerpts into one digest-checked ContextPacket with structural obligation links and explicit gaps; never evaluate semantic support or sufficiency.
- Good for: Packaging multiple caller-selected local source excerpts under one task/question ID with exact file digests and line ranges.; Showing which declared context obligations have current byte-matching inputs and which remain missing, stale, generated-only, unreadable, partial, or over budget.; Preparing a structured context handoff where semantic support and sufficiency must remain an explicit later decision.
- Not for: Discovering sources, resolving a Current ref, authenticating source authority, or deciding semantic relevance.; Proving that a source supports an obligation, that the packet is complete, or that a conclusion is true.; Turning a generated summary into source evidence or authorizing retrieval, network access, publication, or repository writes.
- Use only if: The caller provides exact source paths, SHA-256 digests, line ranges, obligation IDs, and an explicit aggregate byte budget.; All requested files are under the authorized checked-out ignition/ tree and excerpt output is permitted for its intended audience.; The expected result is structural packet assembly only; a separate reviewer will assess source meaning and sufficiency.
- Do not use when: The task requires external or newly discoverable sources, authoritative Current-ref resolution, semantic coverage, or scientific judgment.; A source digest is unknown, a path is outside ignition/, or the requested range cannot be bounded.; Selected local text may be sensitive and the caller or output audience is not authorized to receive it; the tool does not redact excerpts.
- Inputs → outputs: Exact JSON request for task-scoped bounded context assembly with declared obligations, exact source digests/ranges, and a total source-byte budget. → Digest-checked line excerpts, structural obligation linkage, explicit gaps, and semantic sufficiency left NOT_EVALUATED.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_BOUNDED_LOCAL_SOURCE_RANGES` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Returns caller-selected excerpt text on stdout; no file, network, credential, billing, or external-action effects..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · Reads full selected files to verify SHA-256 before emitting bounded line ranges; aggregate source-read budget is at most 1,000,000 bytes. Latency and memory are not calibrated.
- Alternatives: `knowledge.navigate_raw_evidence` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The navigator finds query-conditioned index candidates and literal matches; this assembler requires explicit paths/ranges and groups them by declared task obligations.; `knowledge.cluster_source_brief` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The source-brief tool renders a declared article cluster; this tool assembles arbitrary exact local ranges across obligations and reports digest/gap structure.; `foundation.read_current_asset_card` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The asset-card reader accepts one exact canonical ID; this tool accepts multiple caller-selected repository paths and line ranges..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: A single local packet binding multiple explicit source excerpts, task obligations, current byte digests, and unresolved gaps.; Preventing a caller-declared generated summary from structurally satisfying an obligation marked source_required..
- Comparison evidence: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · One local task compared exact digests and selected lines against direct SHA-256 plus line-range reads; downstream answer quality, semantic coverage, reliability, and cost were not compared. Comparators: none registered; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-context-packet-c1-comparison-r0.json, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_context_packet.py, ignition/OPERATING-METHOD.md, ignition/AI-START-HERE.md; findings: The direct baseline and packet excerpted the same exact source bytes and line numbers.; The packet added a grouped structural obligation map and explicit gaps without deciding semantic support or sufficiency.; Current cluster-brief, raw-navigation, and exact-asset tools use different input/output contracts; no same-task superiority or retirement evidence exists. Preserve COMPARISON_UNKNOWN..
- Deprecation/retirement: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · Optional default-off candidate; one smoke and a synthetic AB-01 pilot do not establish replacement value or justify retiring another tool.
- Migration/rollback: No Current operation, source registry, or Knowledge asset is migrated or changed. Existing source-brief, raw-navigation, and exact-ID contracts remain available. / Remove this candidate entry and wrapper from the isolated branch, regenerate identity-card and human-directory projections, and preserve the source/test/evidence commit history..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_context_packet.py --stdin`
- Fallback: If a requested source is stale, missing, generated-only for a source-required obligation, unreadable, partial, or over budget, preserve the gap and use a separately authorized retrieval or exact-source tool; do not mark the packet sufficient.
- Evidence: `REAL_TOOL_TEST` · source refs `ignition/tools/toolbox_context_packet.py` (BOUNDED_PACKET_ASSEMBLER_ENTRYPOINT), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_context_packet.py` (C1_STALE_GAP_GENERATED_AND_PATH_NEGATIVE_CONTROLS), `ignition/AI-START-HERE.md` (CURRENT_TASK_AND_PERMISSION_BOUNDARY), `ignition/OPERATING-METHOD.md` (CURRENT_DEFAULT_READ_ONLY_BOUNDARY), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-context-packet-route-evidence-r0.json` (EXPLICIT_ALLOWLIST_ROUTE_PLAN), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-context-packet-c1-comparison-r0.json` (MATCHED_LOCAL_CONTEXT_ASSEMBLY_EVIDENCE); focused checks [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_context_packet.py](tests/test_toolbox_context_packet.py); negative controls: Stale digests withhold excerpts and remain explicit gaps.; Generated summaries cannot satisfy source-required obligations by caller declaration.; Missing, unreadable, partial-range, and over-budget inputs never become structural presence.; Path traversal, symlink targets, unknown IDs, duplicate JSON keys, and invalid request shapes fail closed.; The tool does not alter source bytes and never sets execution_authorized or sufficiency to true..
- Route default: `false` · conditions: Explicit allowlisting is required.; The task supplies exact source refs, expected digests, line ranges and required output contract.; No semantic source support, sufficiency, Current-ref resolution or external action is required..
- Claim ceiling: A bounded packet of caller-selected local excerpts with current byte-digest checks and structural obligation linkage only. Current-ref resolution, source-kind declarations, authority, freshness meaning, semantic relevance, evidential support, sufficiency, truth, proof, scientific acceptance, and publication readiness are not evaluated.

### `knowledge.cluster_source_brief` · 文章簇来源简报 / Article-cluster source brief

Relative operator-error risk, maintenance cost, and broader task outcomes remain unmeasured. ### `knowledge.navigate_raw_evidence` · 原始证据导航器 / Raw evidence navigator

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `c8ef67442dcf90a9ef657e725b7562f63c392988`.
- Can do: Use the checked-out Knowledge search index as a navigation prior, reopen bounded current raw files, and return exact line matches with source hashes.
- Good for: Finding repository-local source candidates from explicit literal query terms when the caller does not know an asset ID or article-cluster ID.; Reopening indexed source files from the current checkout and returning line numbers, current SHA-256 values, and whether the indexed digest differs.; Keeping incomplete or budget-limited navigation visible as an unresolved gap.
- Not for: Semantic retrieval, query expansion, or discovery of newly added sources absent from the checked-out search index.; Deciding that any source supports a proposition, that a requirement is satisfied, or that independent source-family support exists.; Replacing a live web search, scientific review, source-authenticity check, proof, or publication decision.
- Use only if: The requested task is local repository navigation and supplies 1 to 12 literal query terms for each of 1 to 8 named requirements.; The checked-out Knowledge search index and current source files are the intended scope.; The caller accepts an explicit total raw-source byte budget, a per-requirement source limit, and a result ceiling of navigation hints only.
- Do not use when: The task requires current external facts or sources outside the checked-out Formal repository.; The relevant source may be new or unindexed and the caller needs a completeness guarantee.; A semantic, causal, mathematical, or scientific judgment is required rather than literal source navigation.; The selected repository excerpts may contain sensitive text; use only when the current caller and output audience are authorized to read those files because the tool does not redact source content.
- Inputs → outputs: Bounded UTF-8 JSON stdin with requirement IDs, literal query terms, maximum sources per requirement, total raw-source byte budget, and maximum excerpt length → Ranked Knowledge-index candidates with exact repository source paths and indexed/current SHA-256 comparison., Current raw line matches with line numbers, matched and missing query terms, current/indexed SHA-256 comparison, at most 8 excerpts per source, matched-line count, truncation flag, and explicit partial/unopened/missing statuses., Requirement-level navigation hints and unresolved gaps; sufficiency_decision is always NOT_EVALUATED.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_SEARCH_INDEX_AND_INDEX_SELECTED_SOURCES_UNDER_IGNITION` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads the fixed checked-out Knowledge index and bounded indexed source files; emits one JSON result to stdout and writes no repository or temporary files..
- Dependencies: ignition/data/governance/knowledge-experience/search-index.jsonl; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · The current fixed index is about 20.5 MB and 24695 rows; raw-source reads are caller-bounded to at most 2 MB, but latency and memory profiles are not calibrated.
- Alternatives: `knowledge.cluster_source_brief` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): That tool renders a declared cluster by ID; this tool accepts explicit query terms and reopens indexed raw sources.; `foundation.read_current_asset_card` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): That tool reads one exact canonical record ID; this tool navigates from literal terms without requiring an ID..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Offline query-conditioned navigation from the checked-out Knowledge index to current raw source lines when the caller does not know a canonical asset or cluster ID.; Detecting that a currently reopened source digest differs from the digest recorded in the navigation index while preserving the current raw bytes as the returned source..
- Comparison evidence: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · A same-corpus local task compared this implementation's reopened source lines with ripgrep on the same selected source. It shows output overlap and the additional index/digest/budget receipt, not end-to-end search superiority, reliability, or cost. The current registered cluster-ID and exact-asset-ID tools have different input contracts. Comparators: none registered; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_raw_evidence_navigator.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_raw_evidence_navigator.py, ignition/KNOWLEDGE/SEARCH.md, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-raw-evidence-navigator-c1-comparison-r0.json; findings: On one bounded repository task, the navigator's exact raw line numbers matched ripgrep on the same reopened source file.; The navigator additionally recorded 24,695 index rows, an index digest, prioritized candidate paths, current-versus-indexed source digests, and the 300000-byte budget gap.; The baseline was given a raw path already selected by the navigator; this does not measure full source-discovery quality or runtime/cost.; No same-task comparison with a registered equivalent tool, calibrated reliability evidence, or cost benchmark supports replacement, retirement, or default routing; preserve COMPARISON_UNKNOWN..
- Deprecation/retirement: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · Optional default-off candidate; the synthetic AB-02 result and local tool tests do not establish replacement value or justify retirement of another tool.
- Migration/rollback: No Current operation is migrated or changed; existing cluster-ID and exact-record-ID tool contracts remain available. / Remove this candidate entry and wrapper from the isolated toolbox branch, regenerate the identity-card projections, and preserve the implementation/test/evidence commit history..

- Fallback: Use the current Knowledge search surface or direct repository search, then inspect the exact current source manually. If no indexed candidate is returned, report that the index did not locate one; do not infer that no source exists.
- Evidence: `REAL_TOOL_TEST` · source refs `ignition/tools/toolbox_raw_evidence_navigator.py` (BOUNDED_LOCAL_NAVIGATION_ENTRYPOINT), `ignition/data/governance/knowledge-experience/search-index.jsonl` (NONAUTHORITATIVE_NAVIGATION_PRIOR), `ignition/KNOWLEDGE/SEARCH.md` (CURRENT_KNOWLEDGE_SEARCH_CONTRACT), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_raw_evidence_navigator.py` (C1_BUDGET_FRESHNESS_AND_PATH_NEGATIVE_CONTROLS), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-raw-evidence-navigator-route-evidence-r0.json` (OPT_IN_LOCAL_ROUTE_PLAN), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-raw-evidence-navigator-c1-comparison-r0.json` (MATCHED_LOCAL_SEARCH_BASELINE_EVIDENCE); focused checks [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_raw_evidence_navigator.py](tests/test_toolbox_raw_evidence_navigator.py); negative controls: Stale indexed digests are reported separately from the hash of reopened current raw bytes.; A partial raw-term match reports missing terms; no term match or budget exhaustion never becomes a sufficiency pass.; Missing files, path traversal, symlink targets, invalid request fields, and excessive budgets fail closed without reading outside ignition/.; More than 8 matching lines report the full match count and an explicit line_matches_truncated flag..

### `knowledge.preflight_reusable_context` · 跨任务上下文复用预检器 / Cross-task reusable context preflight

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `156963db67ff6c8bbc309a77868ed93eaeb8577d`.
- Can do: Preflight caller-selected derived context cards against current local source bytes, declared versions, and caller-attested access scope before cross-task reuse; stale or inaccessible cards are withheld.
- Good for: Checking whether caller-selected task-facing derived context cards still match the exact local source bytes, declared versions, and access-scope identifiers before reuse.; Withholding stale, revoked, unreadable, unauthorized, mismatched-scope, or over-budget cached context and listing eligible refresh candidates.; Returning explicit raw-source reopen requirements when the task needs current, freshness-sensitive, full-text, or raw evidence.
- Not for: Discovering sources, creating or persisting derived cards, or ranking semantic relevance.; Authenticating caller-supplied access grants or source authority.; Treating derived context as raw evidence or deciding semantic support, completeness, sufficiency, truth, or task completion.; Reading external sources, accessing a network, modifying repository files, or invoking other tools.
- Use only if: The caller selects the exact cached cards, current source records, local source paths, versions, SHA-256 digests, and access-scope identifiers.; Every source is local under the checked-out ignition/ tree and the caller declares access ACTIVE and authorized; these access fields remain caller-attested.; The task permits DERIVED_CONTEXT_ONLY and supplies explicit context and source-byte budgets.
- Do not use when: The task requires raw, current, freshness-sensitive, or full-text evidence; use the listed authorized raw sources instead.; A source is revoked, unreadable, missing, outside ignition/, symlinked, changed, or over the declared budget.; The caller cannot attest current access scope, or the cached summary needs semantic validation.
- Inputs → outputs: Exact JSON request with caller-selected derived cache cards, current local source metadata/access state, and explicit context/source-byte budgets. → Byte-checked DERIVED_CONTEXT_ONLY cards, per-card reuse/skip/invalidation decisions, accessible refresh candidates, and raw-source reopen requirements.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_BOUNDED_LOCAL_SOURCE_DIGEST_CHECK` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads only caller-declared local source files after the caller-declared access state is ACTIVE and authorized; returns selected derived text on stdout; writes no files and makes no external calls..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · Source reads are capped at 1,000,000 bytes per file and 5,000,000 bytes per request; context output is capped at 16,384 UTF-8 bytes. Latency and comparative savings are not calibrated.
- Alternatives: `knowledge.assemble_context_packet` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The packet assembler emits exact caller-selected excerpts and structural obligation gaps for one task; this candidate checks whether prior derived cards remain eligible for cross-task reuse.; `knowledge.navigate_raw_evidence` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The navigator finds candidates and reopens current raw source ranges; this candidate validates a caller-selected derived cache card and directs raw reopen when required.; `knowledge.cluster_source_brief` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The cluster brief inventories declared article-cluster cards; this candidate checks version and access-scope bindings on task-facing derived context..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Preflight caller-selected cross-task derived context cards against current local source bytes, declared versions, and access-scope identifiers before reuse..
- Comparison evidence: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · Contract and implementation boundary review plus local negative controls; no same-task quality comparison or measured relative cost was available. Comparators: knowledge.assemble_context_packet, knowledge.navigate_raw_evidence, knowledge.cluster_source_brief; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_context_packet.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_raw_evidence_navigator.py, ignition/tools/extract_cluster_source_brief.py; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-reusable-context-preflight-c1-comparison-r0.json; findings: The candidate preflights reuse of an explicitly supplied derived card; the existing packet assembler, raw navigator, and cluster brief have different input/output contracts and remain useful for their declared tasks.; Workbench AB-20 R0 reported one synthetic 24-task workload with baseline-sensitive RBEU results; this is not live quality, permission, or production evidence.; Relative quality, reliability, and measured cost remain COMPARISON_UNKNOWN. No replacement or retirement is supported..

- Route default: `false` · conditions: Only caller-selected cards may be checked; explicit experimental allowlisting is required.; Task input and output contracts match the exact versioned request/result schemas.; Task permits derived context, current caller-attested access is ACTIVE and authorized, and source/context budgets are explicit.; Any raw, freshness-sensitive, current-source, full-text, or source-evidence requirement is handled by separately authorized raw-source tools..
- Claim ceiling: Exact local byte-digest and caller-declared version/access-scope preflight for caller-selected derived context cards only. No source authority, permission grant, semantic relevance, raw evidence, evidential sufficiency, truth, proof, completion, or scientific acceptance is established.

### `maintenance.attribute_actions_validation_evidence` · Actions 验证计划/工件归因器 / Actions validation plan/artifact attribution

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `4a37bd62415975999c31f07d83cffeb612b35533`.
- Can do: Align one caller-supplied component validation plan and its selected targets with a GitHub Actions run/jobs snapshot, artifact file list, and optional target receipt.
- Good for: Joining the digest-bound selected-target list in one supplied component-validation plan to the same run ID, attempt, and head SHA in an Actions jobs snapshot.; Distinguishing a target receipt absent from a caller-supplied complete artifact file list from unknown receipt presence or failed target results.; Reviewing observed job intervals together with plan and artifact identity while retaining exact workflow conclusions.
- Not for: Fetching or authenticating GitHub runs, artifacts, plans, or receipts.; Certifying component validation, selected-target success, required-check satisfaction, test equivalence, or official Foundation status.; Estimating billable minutes, cash cost, waste, runtime savings, or analyst-time savings.; Granting CI sufficiency, merge/release authorization, or scientific validity.

- Do not use when: The task requires live GitHub acquisition, authenticated source provenance, or a CI policy decision.; The requested answer is billable time, cash cost, avoidable work, required-check satisfaction, merge permission, or release permission.; The caller cannot process the supplied CI and artifact metadata within the current task authority.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin containing one component validation plan, one workflow run/jobs snapshot, one artifact inventory with extracted basenames and completeness flag, and an optional component validation receipt. → Deterministic JSON receipt with plan/run/artifact identity bindings, selected-target IDs, receipt presence or binding status, observed job intervals, source/tool digests, and explicit non-claims.

- Side effects: Reads one bounded stdin JSON envelope and the two local tool sources for digests; emits one JSON receipt to stdout or a validation error to stderr; creates or modifies no files and makes no network request..

- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No representative analyst-time, latency, CPU, memory, hosted-runner or cash-cost comparison has been measured; the tool is offline.

Their useful input scopes differ..

### `maintenance.audit_execution_state_lineage` · 执行状态谱系审计器 / Execution State Lineage Auditor

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field grants no Owner, execution, recovery, or scientific authority.` · source blob `5daf78fac42bcffcc59af7e1b1ab65ed972daca6`.
- Can do: Audit a bounded caller-supplied checkpoint DAG, selected active tip, accounting for every inactive leaf, resume-capsule bindings and strict rollback ancestry. Read-only, offline and default off.
