### `maintenance.compare_actions_repeated_step_sequences` · GitHub Actions 重复步骤序列分析器 / GitHub Actions repeated step sequence analyzer

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0; reuses toolbox_actions_step_timeline summarize_snapshot contract` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field grants no CI, Owner, or release authority.` · source blob `7eaaa9f5f8161ae0f590c059875567fb42c1c421`.
- Can do: Compare repeated job/step names and observed intervals across supplied runs of one workflow, event, pull request, and base commit; keep changed head revisions and source anomalies visible.
- Good for: Finding repeated job/step labels across two to twenty caller-supplied workflow runs for the same workflow, event, pull request, and base SHA.; Reviewing each run and exact head SHA beside repeated step outcomes and observed intervals, including failed and successful runs.; Preserving source timestamp anomalies and partial coverage rather than silently converting them to zero duration.
- Not for: Deciding repeated work was avoidable, wasted, caused by a workflow defect, or safe to remove.; Calculating billable runner minutes, cash cost, user wait, globally elapsed time, or causal value of CI.; Assessing required-check satisfaction, test equivalence, branch protection, merge/release permission, or Foundation validity.; Fetching or authenticating GitHub data; the tool only processes caller-supplied JSON snapshots.; Treating an equal-timestamp skipped step in a cancelled run as a successful execution or as proof that the check ran.
- Use only if: The caller supplies two to twenty complete enough workflow-run/jobs snapshots with one shared workflow ID/path, event, PR number, and base SHA.; Each snapshot can be bound to its run ID, attempt, head SHA, and matching job IDs/attempts/head SHA.; The caller accepts that head SHAs may differ, timestamps may be partial, and input provenance is unauthenticated by this tool.
- Do not use when: Snapshots mix workflows, events, pull requests, or base commits; the analyzer fails closed on mixed contexts.; The task requires a causal waste estimate, exact GitHub billing, a CI policy decision, or an authoritative source signature.; The caller cannot retain or process the supplied workflow metadata within the current task authority.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object containing 2–20 workflow_run/jobs_response snapshots for one workflow/event/PR/base context → Per-run source-bound-to-input timeline projections retaining each head SHA and run conclusion, Repeated step groups with per-run identifiers, outcomes, timestamps, and observed interval seconds, Explicit timestamp-anomaly list, input/tool digests, and non-claim markers
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_CALLER_SUPPLIED_ACTIONS_SNAPSHOT_BATCH` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_repeated_step_analysis.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_step_timeline.py` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads bounded UTF-8 JSON from stdin and the analyzer/helper source files for their digests; emits one JSON receipt to stdout or a validation error to stderr; makes no network request and creates or modifies no files., For a completed skipped job with reversed timestamps only, preserves the original times in timestamp_anomalies and nulls both interval endpoints in the derived analysis; all other reversed intervals remain invalid..
- Dependencies: ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_step_timeline.py; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No representative CPU, memory, latency, GitHub hosted-runner, or cash-cost profile has been measured; the analyzer itself is offline.
- Alternatives: `maintenance.summarize_actions_step_timeline` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The existing tool analyzes one supplied run. This analyzer reuses its per-run contract and adds cross-run repeated-step grouping for a single PR/base history; neither proves avoidable work..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Compare repeated named steps and their per-head outcomes across two to twenty snapshots from one PR/base context in one call, preserving changed heads and partial-time anomalies..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · Paired offline analysis of four same-workflow/event/PR/base #264 run snapshots and an isolated single-run smoke. The batch output has 70 repeated-step groups and preserves two timestamp anomalies; no latency, resource, analyst-effort, or causal-waste comparison was performed. ### `maintenance.compare_audit_findings` · 审计发现对照器 / Audit Finding Comparator

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `74a8f94a31eb196d6f815b986306059fa7262673`.
- Can do: Compare two caller-declared audit records for finding coverage, disposition disagreements, and evidence-reference differences when scope and target revision match; it does not adjudicate findings or establish review independence.
- Good for: Comparing caller-supplied primary and follow-up audit records for one identical scope, target, and target revision.; Showing finding IDs present in only one review, caller-declared disposition disagreements, and differences in evidence-reference sets.; Preparing a bounded comparison for human follow-up while preserving the two original records and their separate evidence references.
- Not for: Performing an audit, reading evidence references, authenticating review records or reviewers, or proving reviewer independence.; Adjudicating claim truth, evidence quality, security severity, scientific validity, or whether a finding is correctly confirmed or rejected.; Comparing records for different scopes, targets, or target revisions; authorizing code changes, releases, or external actions.
- Use only if: Both audit records are caller supplied and bind the same exact scope_ref, target_ref, and target_revision.; The caller needs a structural difference report and accepts that reviewer identity and evidence authenticity remain unverified.; Input contains bounded references and hashes rather than secret, hidden-reasoning, or raw sensitive audit text.
- Do not use when: The records refer to different targets or revisions or require content-level evidence verification.; A result would be treated as independent approval, security certification, scientific validation, or permission to act.; The input includes credentials, hidden reasoning, or raw material that should not enter the local CLI process.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON request matching toolbox-audit-finding-comparison-r0.schema.json with two audit records bound to the same scope_ref, target_ref, and target_revision; each finding includes an ID, caller-declared disposition, and bounded source references with caller-declared SHA-256 values. → Caller-declared finding coverage and disposition relations, source-reference lists, target binding, and explicit non-adjudication / non-independence status in JSON.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_LOCAL_STDIN_AUDIT_RECORD_COMPARISON` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `none` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads at most 256000 stdin bytes and writes one comparison JSON result to stdout; it does not read evidence files, write files, contact a network, access secrets, bill, or authorize an action..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · Input is capped at 256000 bytes and 100 findings per record; runtime, memory, and any human follow-up cost have not been calibrated.
- Alternatives: `research.validate_evidence_program_snapshot` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Runs the fixed repository evidence-program validator and returns a source-tree digest; it does not compare two review finding sets.; `maintenance.attribute_actions_validation_evidence` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Maps a validation plan and its targets to one Actions run snapshot; it does not reconcile audit disposition or evidence-reference differences.; `knowledge.navigate_raw_evidence` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Finds bounded raw source lines in the checked-out Knowledge index; it does not compare caller-supplied review records.; `maintenance.compare_context_packet_variants` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Compares same-task ContextPacket excerpt and obligation structures, not audit finding records..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Compare two caller-provided audit records bound to identical scope, target, and revision and list finding and evidence-reference differences without adjudicating either record..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · R0 is limited to same-target caller-record comparison for finding coverage, declared dispositions, and evidence-reference sets; it does not perform independent verification. Comparators: research.validate_evidence_program_snapshot, maintenance.attribute_actions_validation_evidence, knowledge.navigate_raw_evidence, maintenance.compare_context_packet_variants; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-audit-finding-comparator-c1-evidence-r0.json; findings: The existing validator checks one fixed evidence program, the attribution tool binds run data to planned targets, the navigator locates raw repository lines, and the ContextPacket comparator measures a different pair of structural packets.; No compared tool consumes two audit records and reports finding coverage, caller-declared disposition differences, and evidence-reference differences under an exact shared target binding.; This bounded function has no evidence of replacing any compared tool; no existing tool is deprecated or retired..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · The candidate has a narrow record-comparison input/output contract; comparison evidence does not support replacing another tool.
- Migration/rollback: Optional default-off candidate only; no Current operation, report authority, or permission changes.; Callers may supply immutable audit records by piping them to stdin; the tool does not migrate or rewrite those records. / Remove this candidate card, implementation, schemas, tests, generated identity projection, and its route/C1/task evidence from the isolated candidate.; Keep existing audit records and all compared tools unchanged..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_audit_finding_comparator.py --stdin`
- Fallback: Manually compare two same-target records by stable finding ID and evidence-reference identity, then have a human review uncovered or conflicting rows.
- Evidence: `REAL_TASK_OUTCOME` · source refs `ignition/tools/toolbox_audit_finding_comparator.py` (AUDIT_RECORD_COMPARISON_IMPLEMENTATION), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_audit_finding_comparator.py` (MATCHING_DIFFERENCE_AND_NEGATIVE_CONTROLS), `ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-audit-finding-comparison-r0.schema.json` (STRICT_INPUT_SHAPE), `ignition/docs/operations/toolbox-audit-finding-comparator.md` (LIMITS_AND_INVOCATION_CONTRACT), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-audit-finding-comparator-c1-evidence-r0.json` (C1_FUNCTIONAL_SCOPE_COMPARISON), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-audit-finding-comparator-real-task-input-r0.json` (REAL_PROJECT_RECORD_INPUT), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-audit-finding-comparator-real-task-r0.json` (BOUNDED_REAL_PROJECT_INVOCATION), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-audit-finding-comparator-route-profile-r0.json` (EXACT_DEMAND_ROUTE_PROFILE), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-audit-finding-comparator-route-evidence-r0.json` (DEFAULT_OFF_AND_EXPLICIT_ROUTE_EXPERIMENT), `ignition/tools/toolbox_validate_evidence_program.py` (C1_EXISTING_FIXED_SCOPE_VALIDATOR), `ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_validation_attribution.py` (C1_EXISTING_RUN_TO_TARGET_ATTRIBUTION), `ignition/tools/toolbox_raw_evidence_navigator.py` (C1_EXISTING_RAW_SOURCE_NAVIGATOR), `ignition/tools/toolbox_context_packet_variant_comparator.py` (C1_EXISTING_STRUCTURAL_CONTEXT_COMPARATOR); focused checks [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_audit_finding_comparator.py](tests/test_toolbox_audit_finding_comparator.py); negative controls: Reject different scope, target, or target revision rather than compare incomparable records.; Reject duplicate findings, malformed or duplicate evidence references, invalid dispositions, duplicate JSON keys, unknown fields, and oversized stdin without partial output.; Report same reviewer references as not independent; distinct caller-declared reviewer references remain unauthenticated..
- Route default: `false` · conditions: Only for an explicit request to compare two caller-provided audit records with identical scope_ref, target_ref, and target_revision.; The caller explicitly allows this candidate; default routing leaves it unselected.; Never use it as independent review, truth adjudication, evidence authentication, authorization, or release approval..
- Claim ceiling: Repository-local deterministic comparison of caller-declared audit records only; it establishes no reviewer independence, evidence authenticity, finding truth, task success, or authority.

### `maintenance.compare_context_packet_variants` · 上下文包变体对照器 / ContextPacket Variant Comparator

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `1487adecbd8be43c2ecbbdec9dd43047a097cbe4`.
- Can do: Compare two matched ContextPacket outputs by exact excerpt text bytes and caller-declared structural obligation deltas without echoing excerpts or judging sufficiency.
- Good for: Comparing two ContextPacket R0 outputs for the same caller-declared question, current_ref, and ordered obligation list.; Counting excerpt text UTF-8 bytes, lines, and characters without returning excerpt content.; Showing which caller-declared obligations are present or missing in each packet while preserving semantic_coverage and sufficiency_decision as NOT_EVALUATED.
- Not for: Authenticating either packet, resolving a Current ref, or verifying source authority or source bytes against the checkout.; Judging semantic relevance, evidence support, important-error recall, task outcome, source completeness, or packet sufficiency.; Estimating provider token use, latency, or billing cost; exact local UTF-8 text bytes are not provider tokens.; Comparing packets from different declared questions, current_ref values, or obligation lists.
- Use only if: Both inputs are outputs from knowledge.assemble_context_packet R0 and the caller wants a structural, same-task comparison.; Both packets declare the exact same operation_or_question_id, current_ref, and ordered required obligation IDs.; The caller accepts that packet identity, source status, and obligation labels remain caller-declared and are not authenticated.
- Do not use when: Either packet contains confidential text that should not be passed to a local stdin tool or retained in process memory.; The task requires a judgment about semantic coverage, actual answer quality, scientific support, important-error recall, or provider cost.; The two variants do not share the same caller-declared task, current reference, or ordered obligations.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON object with exactly comparison_id, baseline_packet, candidate_packet; each packet is a knowledge.assemble_context_packet R0 result. → Matched caller-declared task binding, exact UTF-8 excerpt-text byte/character/line counts, source identity counts, and declared obligation set differences without echoing excerpts., Explicit NOT_EVALUATED semantic coverage and sufficiency, NOT_MEASURED provider tokens/cost, and execution_authorized=false.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_LOCAL_STDIN_PACKET_COMPARISON` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `none` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads two bounded packet objects from stdin and emits a compact structural comparison to stdout; it does not read files, echo excerpts, write files, contact a network, or authorize execution..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · Input is capped at 256000 bytes and at most 16 context items per packet. Runtime and memory are not calibrated; UTF-8 excerpt bytes are not provider token or billing costs.
- Alternatives: `knowledge.assemble_context_packet` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The assembler reads caller-selected local sources and creates one digest-checked packet; this comparator consumes two packet outputs and reports structural deltas without re-reading sources.; `knowledge.navigate_raw_evidence` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The navigator searches a checked-out index and opens bounded raw matches; it does not compare two assembled task contexts.; `maintenance.compare_actions_repeated_step_sequences` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): That comparator measures repeated workflow job/step timing sequences, not excerpt bytes or declared context obligations.; `research.compare_publication_metadata_snapshots` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): That comparator checks selected title/year metadata fields for the same DOI, not task context packets..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Compare two caller-selected ContextPacket R0 variants for the same declared task and show exact text-byte and structural obligation deltas without emitting context text..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · Contract-level comparison against the ContextPacket assembler, local raw-evidence navigator, repeated Actions sequence comparator, and publication metadata comparator. Same-task semantic quality, important-error recall, provider token cost, and retrieval-maintenance savings are not measured. Comparators: knowledge.assemble_context_packet, knowledge.navigate_raw_evidence, maintenance.compare_actions_repeated_step_sequences, research.compare_publication_metadata_snapshots; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_context_packet.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_context_packet.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_raw_evidence_navigator.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_repeated_step_analysis.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_compare_publication_metadata.py, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-context-packet-variant-c1-evidence-r0.json; findings: The existing assembler creates and hashes one packet but does not compare two matched packet outputs or report their exact excerpt-byte delta.; The raw-evidence navigator searches one local corpus; the Actions comparator compares job/step intervals; the publication comparator compares DOI metadata fields. Their inputs and outputs differ from matched context-packet comparison.; No comparison establishes better semantic coverage, answer quality, important-error recall, provider token use, billing, or retrieval cost. No existing tool is retired or replaced..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · Retain as a narrowly specialized comparator for two matched ContextPacket outputs. It does not assemble packets, search sources, or measure semantic quality or provider cost.

Continue using the ContextPacket assembler to build each packet; select this candidate only when two outputs need a matched structural comparison. / Remove the optional candidate card, wrapper, tests, and generated tool-directory entry; regenerate identity projections and retain historical comparison and route receipts..

### `maintenance.compare_toolbox_component_coverage` · 工具组件覆盖比较器 / Toolbox Component Coverage Comparator

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field grants no Owner, lifecycle, or release authority.` · source blob `0be9fb3ccbcb87e66d6c17578777e91099a22290`.
- Can do: Compare an incumbent toolbox component with a proposed successor across declared valuable use cases, matched results, cost, permission rank, and adverse effects; emit replacement, coexistence, retention, or uncertainty guidance without changing lifecycle state.
- Good for: Comparing declared incumbent and successor task ranges before changing an optional tool card or route.; Detecting incomplete same-task evidence, missing cost, new permission exposure, and declared adverse effects.; Keeping complementary tools together when each retains distinct valuable tasks.
- Not for: Authenticating evidence or independently deciding whether a caller-declared use case is valuable.; Automatically removing runtime code, editing cards or registries, changing routes, or granting permission.; Scientific quality assessment, external truth, production approval, merge, or release decisions.
- Use only if: The caller supplies both exact tool identities and explicit valuable-use-case inventories with input/output contract digests.; Any claimed replacement is backed by paired results with matching contracts, input identity, evaluator, and environment.; Interpret every result as caller-declared evidence and keep incomplete cost, risk, or outcome data unknown.
- Do not use when: The incumbent valuable-use-case inventory is missing or inferred only from similar names.; The task requires a route invocation, registry migration, code removal, scientific adjudication, or Owner authorization.; The supplied trials use different task contracts, evaluators, environments, permission inventories, or resource units.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin matching toolbox-component-comparison-input-r0; exactly one incumbent, one proposed successor, 1-100 declared use cases per tool, and up to 100 paired results; maximum 512 KiB. → Deterministic JSON with shared and distinct task ranges, matched-result completeness, caller-declared permission/risk/cost deltas, a bounded replacement/coexistence/retention/unknown disposition, and a next low-cost comparison suggestion.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_BOUNDED_STDIN_AND_SOURCE_HASH` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_component_comparison.py` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.

- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · Local input size and case count are bounded; no calibrated latency, memory, or resource-cost benchmark is recorded.
- Alternatives: `maintenance.summarize_execution_candidate_pareto_frontier` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The Pareto analyzer computes a repeated-trial outcome/resource frontier; this tool compares incumbent/successor use-case coverage and permission/risk constraints. Neither produces the other output..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Compare declared incumbent-versus-successor valuable-use-case coverage and identify evidence required before a lifecycle decision..
- Comparison evidence: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · The new tool evaluates whole-tool use-case coverage and lifecycle evidence; the existing Pareto analyzer summarizes matched candidate pass rates and resource costs. Their inputs and outputs differ, and no same-task comparative quality/cost trial has been recorded. ### `maintenance.preflight_execution_effect_receipt` · 工具运行效果回执预检器 / Execution Effect Receipt Preflight

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field grants no Owner, lifecycle, or release authority.` · source blob `d48c7a57148dd50baaedbaee2e709f9a5332766b`.
- Can do: Checks caller-declared run, tool/version/source/input/output, environment and later-observation bindings; optionally rereads selected bounded regular files under ignition/ and returns digest-only comparisons to a declared baseline. It never invokes the subject tool.
- Good for: Binding a declared completed run to a later observation by exact run ID, tool ID/version/source SHA-256, input/output SHA-256, environment reference and observation time.; Rereading up to 32 selected regular files under ignition/ without returning file contents, then comparing current SHA-256 or absence with a caller-declared baseline.; Keeping no-run, started, completed, unknown, contradictory observation and unpreserved nonzero failure states distinct.
- Not for: Authenticating the caller, source, run, environment, actor, observation, or a supplied baseline digest.; Observing paths outside ignition/, cloud systems, APIs, physical systems, or effects not represented by selected local files.; Proving that the declared action caused a filesystem difference or that no other effect occurred.; Executing, retrying, authorizing, publishing, merging, or establishing scientific or production truth.
- Use only if: The task needs a bounded consistency check of one execution record and caller-supplied later observation.; Any requested local state observation is limited to explicit regular files under ignition/ and the caller accepts digest-only output.; The baseline is preserved separately and is treated as caller-declared unless its provenance is independently checked.
- Do not use when: The required effect is outside the local ignition/ checkout or requires an authenticated remote or physical observer.; The caller expects source hashes, run identity, baseline provenance, actor independence, or action causality to be authenticated.; A run is missing or incomplete but the task would treat the output as success.
- Inputs → outputs: One UTF-8 JSON object conforming to toolbox-execution-effect-receipt-input-r0, maximum 131072 bytes. → Deterministic JSON with caller-declared binding findings, execution/outcome states, failure preservation, fixed non-authority fields, and optional digest-only observations of selected ignition/ files.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `BOUNDED_STDIN_AND_READ_ONLY_RECHECK_OF_EXPLICIT_IGNITION_PATHS` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_execution_effect_receipt.py, ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-execution-effect-receipt-input-r0.schema.json, ignition/` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads one bounded stdin JSON object, the local schema and source file, and optionally selected ignition/ file bytes for SHA-256 only; emits JSON to stdout or an error to stderr. It writes no files and returns no observed file contents..
- Dependencies: ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-execution-effect-receipt-input-r0.schema.json; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library, jsonschema >=4.18 with Draft 2020-12 support.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · Input is capped at 131072 bytes; local reads are capped at 32 paths, 1 MiB per file and 4 MiB total. No matched latency, operator-time, or monetary-cost comparison was conducted.

### `maintenance.preflight_failure_anchored_recovery` · 失败锚定恢复预检器 / Failure-Anchored Recovery Preflight

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field grants no Owner, lifecycle, or release authority.` · source blob `dc681c831ab8c765e705c857de334d5d5aa503d1`.
- Can do: Gate one caller-authored recovery proposal against a preserved failed-run record, evidence excerpts, labeled diagnosis, repeated-attempt history, bounded permission/budget deltas, verification, and stop conditions; never executes or authorizes a retry.
- Good for: Checking that one proposed recovery refers to preserved evidence excerpts bound to a caller-declared failed run and exact failure signature.; Keeping observations distinct from hypotheses and requiring each cited diagnosis to carry evidence and a falsifier.; Blocking an unchanged action after the same failure signature, permission expansion, unknown/unbounded budget, missing verifier, or missing stop conditions.; Producing a bounded human-review packet with a precise next step of HUMAN_REVIEW or STOP_NO_RETRY.
- Not for: Authenticating a CI run, source URL, caller, excerpt provenance, digest origin, root cause, diagnosis, or recovery safety.; Generating a diagnosis, editing files, calling validators, changing permissions or budgets, executing a recovery, or authorizing any retry.; Adjudicating a reproduced implementation defect; use the existing failure-case evidence gate for that claim.; Aggregate predecessor/successor improvement evaluation or portable research episode artifact/version validation.
- Use only if: The caller supplies one bounded failure receipt, evidence excerpts with matching caller-supplied hashes, labeled observations/hypotheses, one recovery proposal, a verifier, and stop conditions.; The result is treated only as internal consistency and a human-review aid; no source, actor, diagnosis, or action is authenticated.; The proposed work remains within the declared permissions and budget and preserves the first failure and evidence append-only.
- Do not use when: The caller needs independent source/log authentication, root-cause diagnosis, or assurance that a recovery command is safe or effective.; The recovery requires permission expansion, unknown or unbounded budget, relaxing stop conditions, or rewriting the original failure record.; The caller expects automatic execution, retry, file changes, network access, CI-policy changes, scientific acceptance, or publication.
- Inputs → outputs: One UTF-8 JSON object conforming to toolbox-failure-anchored-recovery-input-r0, maximum 131072 bytes, supplied on stdin. → Deterministic JSON with READY_FOR_HUMAN_REVIEW or BLOCKED, exact failure/action fingerprints, diagnosis and evidence references, verifier and stop conditions, plus fixed execution_authorized=false and retry_authorized=false.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_BOUNDED_STDIN_SINGLE_REQUEST` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-failure-anchored-recovery-input-r0.schema.json` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads one bounded JSON request from stdin and the local input schema; writes one JSON result to stdout; performs no network calls, subprocesses, repository changes, or retries..
- Dependencies: ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-failure-anchored-recovery-input-r0.schema.json; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9, jsonschema >=4.18 with Draft 2020-12 support.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No matched runtime, operator-effort, reliability, or monetary-cost comparison has been measured; input is capped at 131072 bytes and evaluation is deterministic and local.
- Alternatives: `maintenance.preflight_governed_improvement_transition` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): It checks declared system-level predecessor/successor evaluator, quality, cost, failure, authority, rollback, and next-round deltas; it does not bind one task failure to evidence-linked recovery steps or gate an unchanged retry.; `collaboration.validate_portable_research_episode` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): It checks portable artifact versions, run relations, reviews, omissions, and fork mappings; it does not assess failure diagnosis evidence, recovery verification, permission/budget changes, or repeated actions..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Preflight one proposed recovery against preserved failure evidence, an exact signature, labeled diagnosis, a verifier, retry history, and stop rules without executing the proposal..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · One caller-declared failed task run and one proposed recovery. Contract inspection plus one real #264 CI failure preflight show distinct outputs from defect-claim evidence gating, portable-episode manifest validation, and system-level improvement transition review. Relative quality, reliability, latency, operator effort, and monetary cost remain unknown. Comparators: maintenance.preflight_governed_improvement_transition, collaboration.validate_portable_research_episode; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_governed_improvement_transition.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_portable_research_episode.py, ignition/tools/failure_case_evidence_gate.py; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-failure-anchored-recovery-c1-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-failure-anchored-recovery-real-task-r0.json, ignition/tools/failure_case_evidence_gate.py; findings: The compared tools have adjacent inputs but no recovery-plan evidence linkage, unchanged-retry gate, exact-signature verifier binding, or STOP_NO_RETRY output.; The new tool produced READY_FOR_HUMAN_REVIEW on one real task-derived failure; it did not execute the recovery or authorize a retry.; No matched output-quality, reliability, operator-time, latency, or cash-cost study supports a performance or cost superiority claim..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · A distinct recovery-preflight use case is evidenced on one task-derived failure; no existing component is replaced or retired, and performance/cost superiority remains unknown.
- Migration/rollback: This is an optional candidate card and does not modify Current registry operations or activate any route.; Existing failure-case, episode-manifest, and governed-improvement tools remain available for their distinct inputs and outputs. / Remove this candidate card, implementation, schemas, docs, fixtures, tests, evidence, route profile/receipt, and generated identity-card/TOOLBOX projections from the isolated candidate.; Retain the source failure and historical evaluation record; do not rewrite the failure evidence..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_failure_anchored_recovery.py --stdin`
- Fallback: If input evidence links, hashes, action history, verification or stop rules are incomplete, retain the original failure, stop retry planning, and ask a human to inspect the exact run. Use the failure-case evidence gate only for defect-claim adjudication and use an independent reviewer for source authentication or root-cause analysis.

### `maintenance.preflight_producer_consumer_semantics` · 生产者—消费者语义预检器 / Producer-Consumer Semantic Preflight

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `4ecc9f39dd0c6bcfdc6844685ccac61f7024e4da`.
- Can do: Statically inspect one bounded caller-supplied Python consumer function for direct constant re.search checks and compare them with caller-supplied producer text; never import or execute the consumer.
- Good for: Checking whether a bounded producer text contains the direct constant regex records required by one supported consumer function.; Distinguishing missing, case-variant, malformed or reordered, duplicate, and contradictory required records without returning raw captured field values.; Producing a local diagnostic before a separately authorized runtime acceptance check when the consumer uses the narrowly supported direct re.search subset.
- Not for: Authenticating the caller, source repository, Git revision, expected digest, or producer provenance.; Proving the complete consumer accepts the producer, that producer and consumer are semantically equivalent, or that any task or scientific claim is correct.; Evaluating dynamic regex construction, arbitrary transformations, nested parser logic, arbitrary Python semantics, or runtime side effects.; Editing a producer, changing a frozen handoff, importing or executing an old consumer, or restarting an experiment.
- Use only if: The caller provides one UTF-8 JSON input object within the 262144-byte cap, with consumer source up to 131072 UTF-8 bytes and producer text up to 65536 UTF-8 bytes.; The caller-supplied expected SHA-256 values bind the exact strings supplied in the same input; they are digests, not authenticated provenance.; The selected function uses only direct constant re.search(pattern, text_name) calls over a direct string argument or a single UTF-8 decode, with all result variables jointly guarded by a simple fail-closed raise.
- Do not use when: Actual runtime acceptance, full parser behavior, arbitrary control flow, or semantic interpretation must be tested.; Patterns are dynamic, transformed, nested, unsupported by the bounded regex subset, or not jointly fail-closed.; The decision requires authenticated provenance, permission enforcement, scientific review, external truth, or publication approval.
- Inputs → outputs: One UTF-8 JSON object containing a Python consumer source excerpt, function name, expected SHA-256, producer text, and its expected SHA-256; stdin is capped at 262144 bytes. → One bounded deterministic JSON result with source digest bindings, per-pattern and field match statuses/counts/digests, redacted captures, and all non-execution/non-authentication claim flags false.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_BOUNDED_STDIN_AND_LOCAL_INPUT_SCHEMA` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-producer-consumer-semantic-input-r0.schema.json` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads bounded JSON from stdin and the local input schema; writes one bounded JSON result to stdout only..
- Dependencies: ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-producer-consumer-semantic-input-r0.schema.json; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9, jsonschema >=4.18 with Draft 2020-12 support..
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No CPU, memory, latency, or cash-cost measurements against an equivalent implementation have been collected.
- Alternatives: `collaboration.validate_agent_episode_binding` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): That tool checks caller-declared multi-agent plan roles, context, feedback, write domains, and dependency DAGs; this tool checks static producer text against a source-level consumer pattern contract.; `collaboration.verify_handoff_artifact_files` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): That tool hashes current local receipt-referenced artifact bytes; this tool analyzes caller-supplied consumer and producer text and does not authenticate or read those source artifacts..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: not established.
- Comparison evidence: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · The frozen real task produced a repeatable static mismatch observation, but the inspected adjacent tools accept different typed inputs and outputs. No matched same-task implementation, comparative quality score, broad reliability result, or cost measurement exists. Comparators: collaboration.validate_agent_episode_binding, collaboration.verify_handoff_artifact_files; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-producer-consumer-semantic-c1-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-producer-consumer-semantic-real-task-r0.json, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_agent_episode_binding.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_handoff_artifact_file_verifier.py; findings: The agent-plan validator checks a different declared object and does not evaluate a producer-consumer text contract.; The receipt byte verifier compares local artifact bytes to receipt hashes and does not evaluate parser patterns or field relationships.; Two invocations on the same pinned task input returned the same mismatch classification; this establishes repeatability for that input only.; No evidence supports replacement, retirement, globally unique scope, comparative quality, or lower cost; keep this tool optional and preserve COMPARISON_UNKNOWN..
- Deprecation/retirement: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · New optional default-off candidate; inspected neighboring tools have different input contracts and no matched task evidence supports retiring or replacing any component.
- Migration/rollback: No Current registry operation or existing tool is migrated. Keep runtime consumer checks and receipt-byte verification in their existing scopes; use this candidate only for a static preflight when its supported source pattern applies. / Remove this candidate card, source wrapper, schemas, documentation, tests, and task evidence from the isolated branch; regenerate the identity-card and toolbox projections while retaining the prior source commit and external evidence references..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_producer_consumer_semantic_preflight.py --stdin`
- Fallback: If source shape or regex syntax is unsupported, report NOT_EVALUABLE and inspect the pinned consumer and producer manually. If a required pattern mismatches, preserve the mismatch and seek a separately authorized reviewed runtime test; do not edit or rerun frozen artifacts.
- Evidence: `REAL_TASK_OUTCOME` · source refs `ignition/LICENSES/active/BUSL-1.1.md` (CURRENT_EXECUTABLE_CODE_LICENSE), `ignition/LICENSES/README.md` (CURRENT_LICENSE_SCOPE_AUTHORITY), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-producer-consumer-semantic-c1-evidence-r0.json` (C1_COMPARISON_AND_RETIREMENT_DISPOSITION), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-producer-consumer-semantic-real-task-r0.json` (PINNED_REAL_TASK_SOURCE_BINDINGS_AND_STATIC_OUTCOME), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-producer-consumer-semantic-route-profile-r0.json` (FROZEN_TASK_ROUTE_PROFILE), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-producer-consumer-semantic-route-evidence-r0.json` (DEFAULT_OFF_AND_EXPLICIT_ALLOWLIST_ROUTE_EXPERIMENT), `ignition/docs/operations/producer-consumer-semantic-preflight.md` (USER_SCOPE_LIMITS_AND_INVOCATION_GUIDE), `ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-producer-consumer-semantic-input-r0.schema.json` (STRICT_BOUNDED_INPUT_SCHEMA), `ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-producer-consumer-semantic-output-r0.schema.json` (BOUNDED_OUTPUT_SCHEMA_AND_CLAIM_FLAGS), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_identity_cards.py` (MACHINE_CARD_AND_C1_ASSERTIONS), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_producer_consumer_semantic_preflight.py` (FOCUSED_FUNCTIONAL_AND_NEGATIVE_CONTROLS), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py` (DEFAULT_OFF_AND_EXPLICIT_ALLOWLIST_ROUTE_TESTS), `ignition/tools/operations/plan_ignition_operation_run.py` (EXISTING_COVERAGE_FIRST_READ_ONLY_ROUTE_PLANNER), `ignition/tools/toolbox_agent_episode_binding.py` (ADJACENT_DIFFERENT_SCOPE_C1_COMPARATOR), `ignition/tools/toolbox_producer_consumer_semantic_preflight.py` (BOUNDED_STATIC_ANALYZER_ENTRYPOINT), `ignition/tools/toolbox_verify_handoff_artifact_files.py` (ADJACENT_DIFFERENT_SCOPE_C1_COMPARATOR), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_agent_episode_binding.py` (ADJACENT_AGENT_PLAN_TOOL_TEST_REFERENCE), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_handoff_artifact_file_verifier.py` (ADJACENT_RECEIPT_BYTE_TOOL_TEST_REFERENCE); The consumer source is parsed as AST but never imported or executed; route selection remains default-off and never sets execution_authorized=true..

### `maintenance.summarize_actions_interval_union` · GitHub Actions 多运行区间并集汇总器 / GitHub Actions multi-run interval union summarizer

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field grants no execution, CI, Owner, or release authority.` · source blob `abeb5ef5665bb57c9fbf5b3c8a87cb9013603e68`.
- Can do: Compute the observed union, sum, excess overlap, and peak concurrency of job wall intervals across two to twenty context-matched caller-supplied GitHub Actions snapshots, preserving incomplete coverage without estimating billing.
- Good for: Summarize the union of observed job intervals and excess overlap across two to twenty snapshots from one workflow, event, pull request, and base SHA.; Separate elapsed wall-clock coverage from the arithmetic sum of overlapping job intervals, while reporting missing timestamps and incomplete job lists.
- Not for: Estimating billable runner minutes, cash cost, resource use, or savings.; Deciding whether repeated CI work was avoidable, a required check is sufficient, or a merge or release is authorized.; Authenticating snapshots or querying GitHub.
- Use only if: The task explicitly needs aggregate wall-clock interval coverage over two to twenty runs.; The caller provides same-workflow, same-event, same-PR, same-base snapshots with job interval timestamps.
- Do not use when: The task concerns one isolated run; use the single-run step timeline tool.; The task only needs repeated step-name grouping and does not need interval union or overlap.; The user needs authenticated billing, causal savings, runner utilization, or gate sufficiency.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON with 2–20 caller-supplied workflow_run/jobs_response snapshots sharing workflow ID, path, event, pull request, and base SHA; each job interval is timezone-aware ISO-8601 or explicitly missing. → Per-run and aggregate observed job interval sum, interval union, excess overlap seconds, maximum concurrent observed jobs, completeness flags, anomaly list, and input/tool digests.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_CALLER_SUPPLIED_UNAUTHENTICATED_ACTIONS_SNAPSHOTS` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_interval_union.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_repeated_step_analysis.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_step_timeline.py` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads at most two MiB from stdin plus imported source files for digests, then writes one JSON receipt to stdout; it creates or modifies no files..
- Dependencies: ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_repeated_step_analysis.py; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No comparable latency, CPU, memory, or analyst-effort measurement has been collected; network and billing are disabled.
- Alternatives: `maintenance.compare_actions_repeated_step_sequences` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The batch analyzer groups repeated job and step names and preserves per-run intervals, but explicitly does not compute a multi-run interval union or overlap aggregate.; `maintenance.summarize_actions_step_timeline` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The single-run timeline exposes one run's job and step intervals; it cannot summarize a multi-run history..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: For an explicitly requested multi-run Actions timing task, calculate the union of observed job time intervals and the extra job-seconds contributed by overlap while keeping missing intervals visible..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · Paired offline analysis of the same four-run PR #264 snapshot fixture; the existing batch output exposes 14 jobs, 275 steps, repeated-step groups, and no elapsed-time aggregation, while this tool additionally calculates interval union and overlap. No performance or cost comparison was performed. Comparators: maintenance.compare_actions_repeated_step_sequences, maintenance.summarize_actions_step_timeline; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_repeated_step_analysis.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_step_timeline.py; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-actions-interval-union-c1-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-actions-interval-union-real-task-r0.json, ignition/docs/operations/actions-interval-union.md; findings: On the same four-run #264 input, the repeated-step analyzer reports elapsed_time_aggregated_across_runs=false and groups step occurrences; it does not provide the union of all observed job intervals.; The single-run timeline retains isolated per-step detail and is not a multi-run replacement; both existing tools keep distinct useful tasks.; The new tool reports 26,972 seconds of observed interval sum and union, zero excess overlap among observed job intervals, and peak concurrency one; two of fourteen rows lack an interval, so this does not prove no other interval overlapped.; No latency, resource-cost, billed-minute, causal-saving, or analyst-effort comparison is available; retain comparison uncertainty outside this narrow capability distinction..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · The same-input comparison establishes a multi-run interval-union output that the existing single-run and repeated-step tools do not provide; those tools retain their separate per-run and repeated-step outputs.
- Migration/rollback: No existing tool is removed or modified; call this optional analyzer only when a multi-run interval union is explicitly requested.; It adds no Current operation, required check, workflow, permission, route-default, or component-release authority. / Remove this optional card, implementation, schema, documentation, fixture references, tests, source/C1/task/route evidence, regenerate identity cards and TOOLBOX.md, and retain the prior commit plus historical input/output receipts..
- Invocation example: `PYTHONPATH=ignition python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_interval_union.py --stdin`
- Fallback: If snapshots fail shared-context validation, report the exact rejection. If job times or job-list completeness are missing, retain observed-only metrics with PARTIAL status; do not impute zero or claim the absent jobs were idle.
- Evidence: `REAL_TASK_OUTCOME` · source refs `ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_interval_union.py` (BOUNDED_INTERVAL_UNION_ANALYZER), `ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_repeated_step_analysis.py` (INPUT_CONTEXT_AND_TIMESTAMP_VALIDATOR), `ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_step_timeline.py` (PER_RUN_TIMESTAMP_NORMALIZER), `ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-actions-interval-union-output-r0.schema.json` (OUTPUT_CONTRACT), `ignition/docs/operations/actions-interval-union.md` (SCOPE_AND_LIMITS), `ignition/tests/fixtures/toolbox-actions-repeated-step-analysis/pr264-four-runs-r0.json` (REAL_TASK_INPUT_SNAPSHOT_FIXTURE), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_actions_interval_union.py` (FUNCTIONAL_AND_INTERVAL_NEGATIVE_CONTROLS), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_actions_interval_union_routing.py` (DEFAULT_OFF_AND_MINIMAL_ROUTE_EXPERIMENT), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-actions-interval-union-source-binding-r0.json` (BLUEPRINT_SOURCE_SCOPE_BINDING), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-actions-interval-union-real-task-r0.json` (PR264_FOUR_RUN_OUTPUT_RECEIPT), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-actions-interval-union-c1-evidence-r0.json` (SAME_INPUT_FUNCTIONAL_COMPARISON), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-actions-interval-union-route-profile-r0.json` (BOUND_REAL_TASK_ROUTE_PROFILE), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-actions-interval-union-route-evidence-r0.json` (DEFAULT_LIGHT_ADEQUATE_REDUNDANT_ROUTE_TRIALS); - Route default: `false` · conditions: Only when the caller explicitly needs multi-run job interval union, overlap excess, or observed peak concurrency for same-context snapshots.; Default routing must leave this candidate excluded.; A route proposal does not authenticate the snapshots, invoke the analyzer, or authorize a merge, billing action, or release..

### `maintenance.summarize_actions_step_timeline` · GitHub Actions 作业/步骤时间线摘要器 / GitHub Actions job/step timeline summarizer

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `9c9981755b58dcfff8dd830d6581c330ea02e191`.
- Can do: Summarize observed job and step timestamp intervals from a caller-supplied GitHub Actions snapshot without network access.
- Good for: Summarizing job and step elapsed intervals from a supplied workflow run/jobs API JSON snapshot bound to one run ID, attempt, and head SHA.; Preserving missing timestamps and distinguishing a complete job list from complete timestamp coverage.; Reviewing observed run/step intervals while keeping overlapping intervals separate from billable minutes or cash cost.
- Not for: Querying GitHub, authenticating the source of a supplied snapshot, or fetching workflow/job records.; Calculating billable runner minutes, service charges, cash cost, user wait, or elapsed workflow duration from overlapping job sums.; Determining required-check satisfaction, test equivalence, CI sufficiency, merge/release authorization, or scientific validity.; Treating equal timestamps on skipped workflow steps as proof that the step executed or consumed measured runtime.
- Use only if: The caller supplies one UTF-8 JSON envelope containing the workflow run and corresponding jobs API snapshot.; Each supplied job binds to the same run ID, attempt, and head SHA declared in workflow_run.; The caller treats snapshot provenance as unauthenticated and accepts that incomplete timestamps produce a partial receipt.
- Do not use when: The task requires live GitHub acquisition or authenticated source provenance.; The requested value is billable time, cash cost, test equivalence, required-check satisfaction, merge permission, or release permission.; The snapshot is confidential and the caller is not authorized to process it in the local repository context.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin envelope with one workflow_run object and a jobs_response containing job snapshots for the same run ID, attempt, and head SHA → Deterministic JSON receipt with observed job and step interval seconds, missing-time markers, snapshot completeness, and input/tool digests
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_CALLER_SUPPLIED_SNAPSHOT` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_step_timeline.py` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads bounded JSON from stdin and the tool source for its digest; emits a single JSON receipt to stdout or validation errors to stderr; creates or modifies no files and makes no network request..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No comparable local latency, memory, or compute-cost profile has been measured; network and billing are disabled.
- Alternatives: `maintenance.inspect_repository` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Generic repository inspection does not parse a caller-supplied Actions jobs snapshot into per-job and per-step intervals; manual API/UI review remains an alternative but has not been compared on the same task.; `maintenance.compare_actions_repeated_step_sequences` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The paired #264 comparison confirms this tool accepts a single run while the batch analyzer requires 2-20 runs and adds cross-run repeated-step grouping; retain the single-run path for isolated snapshots..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Analyze one supplied GitHub Actions run when there is no multi-run comparison set, including its per-job and per-step observed intervals and source-snapshot digest..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · Paired offline analysis of the four-run #264 workflow/event/PR/base history plus the existing one-run API snapshot smoke; confirms distinct cardinalities and output scope, but does not compare latency, CPU, memory, or analyst effort. Comparators: maintenance.compare_actions_repeated_step_sequences; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_step_timeline.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_actions_repeated_step_analysis.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_actions_repeated_step_analysis.py, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-actions-repeated-step-evidence-r0.json; findings: The single-run tool handles one supplied run; the batch tool requires two to twenty runs and emits repeated-step groups. The batch tool cannot replace isolated single-run analysis. Retain both as opt-in specialized tools; broader cost and performance comparisons remain unknown..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · Four-run paired scope evidence shows the single-run input contract remains useful for isolated Actions snapshots, while the batch analyzer covers same-PR run histories.
- Migration/rollback: Do not migrate manual Actions review or registered repository operations based on this single snapshot smoke; preserve the exact caller-supplied input and timestamp-only output contract pending same-task comparison. / Remove this candidate card, wrapper, fixture, test, and scope note from the isolated toolbox branch; regenerate identity cards and TOOLBOX.md; retain the previous commit and evidence..

### `maintenance.summarize_component_path_residue` · 组件路径残留分组器 / Component path residue grouper

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field grants no canonical mapping or release authority.` · source blob `1951a5281689e0c342da9bd8a1fdedd587fc74a7`.

- Not for: Choosing or authorizing a component ID, path pattern, validation target, CI gate, or release action.; Proving that paths in one cohort share semantic ownership, risk, or validation scope.; Replacing compute_change_propagation.resolve_paths or route_component_validation.

### `maintenance.summarize_execution_candidate_pareto_frontier` · 执行候选帕累托前沿汇总器 / Execution candidate Pareto frontier summarizer

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field grants no execution, CI, Owner, or release authority.` · source blob `e0ddbf5fa445fb7d03bd3676a97930f1dec9394a`.
- Can do: Summarize matched caller-supplied candidate outcomes and one shared resource metric above a baseline pass-rate floor; report observed dominated and nondominated candidates without selecting a deployment.
- Good for: Compare two to twenty caller-declared candidate implementations measured on exactly the same case/trial set, evaluator digest, environment reference, and one resource metric.; Show an empirical pass-rate floor from a named baseline and identify candidates that are below the floor, dominated, or observed on a nondominated frontier.; Preserve unknown resource measurements and exact input/tool digests rather than treating missing cost as zero.
- Not for: Authenticating test results, resource telemetry, task-set digests, evaluator identity, or candidate revisions.; Establishing statistical significance, generalization, causal improvement, billable savings, or reliability.; Selecting a deployment, editing a harness, changing a route, CI workflow, required check, permission, or release decision.; Comparing different task sets, evaluators, environments, resource metrics, or unpaired case/trial observations.

Missing resource cost stays null and suppresses the frontier.; The task explicitly needs an observed outcome/resource tradeoff summary and accepts caller-declared, unauthenticated measurements.

### `reasoning.search_structural_analogies` · 结构类比搜索 / Structural Analogy Search

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1 (code); Apache-2.0 (schemas); CC-BY-NC-SA-4.0 (documentation); see LICENSE.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner, truth, or scientific authority.` · source blob `7a2425cdc6dfcebef266a18f249a8c26547ef65a`.
- Can do: Enumerate and rank bounded injective node mappings between caller-supplied typed relation graphs under explicit role classes and one-to-one relation-type mappings; emit transfer hypotheses that require separate target-domain validation.
- Good for: Find exact directed structural overlaps among one bounded source graph and one to eight caller-supplied typed target graphs.; Check role-compatible injective node mappings, caller-declared relation correspondences, extra mapped edges, and direction-sensitive negative controls.; Produce a ranked list of candidate transfer hypotheses for later source and target-domain review.
- Not for: Discovering or validating target-domain facts, semantic equivalence, causal transfer, scientific truth, solution effectiveness, or authority.; Searching free text, inferring role classes or relation-type mappings, or generating target candidate graphs.; Replacing evidence appraisal, domain experts, experiments, or a target-domain validation process.
- Use only if: Python 3.10 or newer is available; runtime uses the Python standard library only.; The caller supplies typed nodes, directed relation triples, one to eight candidate graphs, and an explicit one-to-one mapping for every source relation type.; Each graph has two to eight nodes and one to 24 relations; the aggregate estimated mapping count stays at or below 200,000.; The UTF-8 JSON request is at most 256 KiB.
- Do not use when: Role classes or relation correspondences are disputed, unknown, or cannot be declared without semantic interpretation.; The task requires open-ended retrieval, textual similarity, target-domain validation, or claims about actual-world evidence.; Graph size, candidate count, or mapping budget exceeds the declared bounds.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object conforming to toolbox-structural-analogy-search-input-r0 with one typed source graph, explicit source-to-target relation-type mappings, and one to eight typed target candidates. → One bounded JSON result conforming to toolbox-structural-analogy-search-output-r0 with structural ranks, exact directed relation coverage, mapping ties, extra and unmatched edges, and claim ceilings., Transfer-hypothesis pairs explicitly marked REQUIRES_TARGET_DOMAIN_VALIDATION; no source authority, target validity, evidence, execution, or authority transfer.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_STDIN_AND_LOCAL_COMPUTATION` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_structural_analogy_search.py` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads one bounded request from stdin and writes one bounded JSON result to stdout; malformed requests produce a diagnostic on stderr., Does not write files, alter repositories or source evidence, access network or secrets, invoke providers, or perform external actions..
- Dependencies: ignition/tools/toolbox_structural_analogy_search.py; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.10 standard library; no third-party runtime packages..
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · One bounded exhaustive enumeration over at most 200,000 role-compatible mappings; no portable latency or memory benchmark has been collected.
- Alternatives: `knowledge.collide_object` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Checks exact/canonical identity collisions among registered objects; it does not enumerate mappings across caller-supplied relation graphs.; `research.preflight_grounded_ideation` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Checks a caller-supplied sourced idea and relation map for counterevidence and falsifiers; it does not enumerate or rank candidate graph correspondences..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Enumerate and rank exact directed structural correspondences across finite caller-supplied graphs before separate source and target-domain validation..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · Same requested graph-search task compared against the declared input/output interfaces of the nearest Current identity-collision operation and optional grounded-ideation preflight. Existing tools cannot accept the same typed candidate-graph set and emit ranked node mappings; therefore their unlike outputs were not assigned misleading numeric scores. Comparators: knowledge.collide_object, research.preflight_grounded_ideation; implementation refs: ignition/tools/operations/evaluate_object_collision_run.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_grounded_ideation_preflight.py, ignition/schemas/operations/grounded-ideation-preflight-r0.schema.json; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-structural-analogy-search-c1-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-structural-analogy-search-real-task-output-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-structural-analogy-search-route-evidence-r0.json; findings: The in-task bounded example ranks the exact role- and direction-preserving structure first and discriminates both an extra mapped edge and reversed relations.; The Current collision checker and grounded-ideation preflight keep their distinct identity and source-evidence use cases; neither currently duplicates the graph enumeration output.; The evidence supports coexistence and does not establish broad superiority, general usefulness, or lower cost; no existing tool is replaced or retired..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · This optional candidate has a distinct typed graph-enumeration use. The two nearest existing tools retain their identity-collision and source-evidence validation roles; no replacement is established.
- Migration/rollback: No automatic migration is inferred. Use this candidate only when the task already has typed relation graphs and explicit role/relation mappings; keep existing collision and grounded-ideation tools for their separate tasks. / Remove the optional candidate card, implementation, schemas, fixture, focused tests, documentation, and candidate-specific route/C1/task evidence from the isolated branch; regenerate toolbox and identity-card projections while preserving prior commits and historical evidence..
- Invocation example: `python3.10 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_structural_analogy_search.py --stdin < request.json`
- Fallback: Retain the typed graphs and review candidate mappings manually with a domain reviewer; do not read no-match as proof that no useful analogy exists.
