A missing provider field or explicit acquisition failure remains INSUFFICIENT and is never represented as agreement.; A changed source title or publication year yields DISAGREE without choosing which provider is correct.; The comparator has no network or file access and cannot fetch missing source data..
- Route default: `false` · conditions: Remain excluded unless explicitly allowlisted for a task that already has both supported source-result objects and specifically requires title/year comparison.; The route planner only proposes this local comparison; it does not authorize the prior public Crossref or OpenAlex calls.; Do not route for a single-provider lookup, source authenticity, article-content, retraction, or scientific-claim adjudication..
- Claim ceiling: Agreement or disagreement in title/year fields returned by caller-supplied Crossref and OpenAlex result objects only. The comparator does not authenticate inputs or providers, establish publication identity, verify article contents, decide scientific truth, or reconcile retraction status.

### `research.inspect_pubmed_update_links` · PubMed 更新关联读取器 / PubMed update-link reader

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.2.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1; NCBI E-utilities usage terms apply, and no license for underlying PubMed record data is asserted. API documentation: https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/books/NBK25499/.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `fea4231a4879c609b02c16547174aadcbd403024`.
- Can do: Read raw Comments/Corrections relations for exact PubMed PMIDs from fixed NCBI EFetch; an optional expected DOI is checked against the same returned record before relations are emitted.
- Good for: Reading raw PubMed Comments/Corrections RefType, citation text, and linked PMID fields for one or more already-known PubMed records.; Recording whether a specific PubMed response contains relation rows without interpreting the relation as a full scientific or editorial adjudication.; Reviewing a known PubMed record after a caller has obtained its PMID directly or through a separately selected identifier resolver.
- Not for: Retrieving, displaying, or reviewing article titles, abstracts, full text, or arbitrary publisher content; the upstream XML may contain an abstract but this wrapper discards it.; Proving that a paper is or is not retracted, corrected, valid, authentic, or scientifically supported.; Treating an empty or missing Comments/Corrections list as evidence that no update exists.; Searching for PMIDs from arbitrary text or changing a repository research record.
- Use only if: The caller supplies one to ten exact numeric PubMed IDs and explicitly permits sending those identifiers to NCBI for this invocation.; The caller invokes the CLI with --allow-public-network.; The exact host eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov is permitted by the actual invocation environment; a route proposal alone is not authority.; The caller understands that only the update links present in the returned record are reported.
- Do not use when: The PMID or its presence in task context is confidential or must not be sent to a public service.; Network access is unavailable, disallowed, or not explicitly approved for this invocation.; The task requires complete retraction status, article-content review, source-authenticity review, or scientific assessment.
- Inputs → outputs: JSON stdin object with one to ten unique numeric PubMed IDs (PMIDs); optional expected_doi is accepted only for exactly one PMID and must be a bare DOI. → input-ordered PubMed Comments/Corrections relation rows with raw RefType, related PMID, and reference source, when expected_doi is supplied: expected DOI, same-response DOI binding status, and relations only on EXACT_MATCH, per-response SHA-256 and explicit HTTP or network outcome summary
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: When explicitly invoked with --allow-public-network, sends at most ten HTTPS GET requests to https://eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/entrez/eutils/efetch.fcgi with the supplied PMID and fixed PubMed/XML/tool parameters; optional expected_doi is used only for local response validation and is not sent to NCBI., The upstream XML response may contain a full PubMed record including an abstract; the wrapper parses only the exact PMID, DOI identifiers when requested, and CommentsCorrectionsList, discards the rest, emits no abstract/title, writes only JSON to stdout, and creates or modifies no repository files., Uses no redirects, secrets, API key, billing, retry, or arbitrary URL; applies a five-second socket timeout, seven-second response-body budget with bounded 8 KiB reads, 256 KiB response cap, and 0.34-second serial inter-request delay..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No comparable latency, bandwidth, service-availability, or compute-cost profile has been measured; the wrapper allows at most ten serial bounded PubMed XML requests.
- Alternatives: `research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The identifier resolver maps DOI values to returned PMID/PMCID values; this tool requires an exact PMID and reads only the record's raw comments/corrections relations.; `research.audit_pubmed_updates_from_doi` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The composition starts from one DOI and performs lookup conditionally; this standalone reader remains useful when the caller already has an exact PMID and needs only its raw relation fields..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Read only the raw Comments/Corrections fields for exact caller-supplied PMIDs, including PMIDs obtained outside the DOI resolver.; Avoid an unnecessary DOI-conversion request when the task already has an authorized exact PMID..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · Historical R0 evidence compares a known-PMID read as a separate stage and inside DOI composition. R1 adds a same-response expected-DOI identity gate; synthetic negative controls withhold relations for mismatch, absence, multiple identifiers, and malformed identifier values. No replacement or broad quality superiority is established. Comparators: research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers, research.audit_pubmed_updates_from_doi; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_doi_update_audit.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-c1-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-c1-evidence-r1.json, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-route-evidence-r1.json; findings: The standalone reader retains exact-PMID tasks, including caller-supplied PMIDs that do not originate from a DOI resolver.; When a composing task supplies expected_doi, the reader returns relations only after the same matching PubMed response contains exactly that DOI; negative cases return no relations.; Identity binding improves attribution of the returned record to the requested DOI but does not authenticate the source or establish comprehensive update detection.; The exact-PMID contract remains a unique standalone use case; no reader retirement or replacement is supported..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · Keep the default-off exact-PMID reader for caller-supplied PMID tasks; the DOI composition extends the chain but does not cover this standalone input scope.
- Migration/rollback: Version 0.2.0 remains backward-compatible for PMID-only inputs; an optional expected_doi adds a same-response identity gate and is restricted to one PMID.; Existing PMID-only calls remain on this adapter and incur no identifier-resolution request or DOI validation. / Remove the candidate entry and wrapper from the isolated toolbox branch, regenerate the identity and human-directory projections, and preserve prior commits and evidence.; If the composition is removed, keep this standalone exact-PMID reader and its prior evidence untouched..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py --stdin --allow-public-network`
- Fallback: If live network use is not approved or fails, preserve NOT_RUN or the acquisition failure and open the official PubMed record manually only when the caller separately authorizes that lookup; never infer no update from an empty/unavailable response.
- Evidence: `REAL_TASK_OUTCOME` · source refs `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py` (BOUNDED_FIXED_HOST_CLI), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py` (FOCUSED_MOCKED_NETWORK_AND_NEGATIVE_CONTROLS), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-c1-evidence-r0.json` (C1_MATCHED_COMPOSITION_COMPARISON), `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_doi_update_audit.py` (C1_COMPOSED_ROUTE_IMPLEMENTATION_COMPARATOR), `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py` (C1_DOI_IDENTIFIER_STAGE_IMPLEMENTATION_COMPARATOR), `ignition/schemas/operations/pubmed-update-link-inspection-input-r0.schema.json` (TYPED_PMID_AND_OPTIONAL_EXPECTED_DOI_INPUT_CONTRACT), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-route-evidence-r1.json` (CANDIDATE_VERSION_0_2_0_DEFAULT_OFF_ROUTE_EXPERIMENT), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-c1-evidence-r1.json` (C1_SAME_TASK_IDENTITY_BINDING_COMPARISON), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-route-evidence-r2.json` (CURRENT_CATALOG_ROUTE_REPLAY_R2); focused checks [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py](tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py); negative controls: Missing --allow-public-network makes no request and emits no result.; Reject malformed, duplicate, nonnumeric, URL-like, and over-limit PMIDs before network access.; An optional expected_doi requires exactly one PMID and a bare DOI; absent, malformed, mismatched, multiple, or invalid DOI identifiers in that same EFetch record return no relations.; Only eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov is contacted; redirects, non-XML, oversized responses, HTTP errors, timeout, malformed XML, entity declarations, and mismatched PMID records remain explicit outcomes.; Return raw relation fields without article titles/abstracts; empty relation lists remain limited observations and no repository files are written..
- Route default: `false` · conditions: Remain excluded unless the candidate ID is explicitly allowlisted and the typed route profile explicitly lists eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov in allowed_network_hosts.; A route proposal has execution_authorized=false and does not validate the caller authority behind that host list.; Every actual CLI invocation separately requires --allow-public-network and a task-specific decision to transmit the supplied PMID values..
- Claim ceiling: Raw PubMed Comments/Corrections fields for exact records at retrieval time only. When an optional expected DOI is supplied, relations are emitted only if that same EFetch record reports exactly one valid matching DOI; this binds response identity but does not authenticate the provider source. The response may omit relationships that exist elsewhere or have not been added, so no completeness, retraction-status, content, authenticity, scientific-validity, or absence claim is established.

### `research.lookup_arxiv_paper_metadata` · arXiv 论文元数据查询器 / arXiv paper metadata lookup

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1; arXiv metadata source and reuse terms remain provider-controlled.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; no Owner, source-authentication, or scientific authority.` · source blob `fb932d94028e4a75c2e2e97eba2391d9d4ebd78d`.
- Can do: Look up selected current arXiv metadata for one caller-supplied modern arXiv identifier using one bounded fixed-host HTTPS request.
- Good for: Resolving one modern arXiv identifier to its provider-reported current entry/version, title, authors, dates, categories, journal reference, and DOI when supplied.; Checking an arXiv citation identifier before literature work while keeping source metadata retrieval separate from claim support and scientific judgment.; Retaining the request URL, retrieval timestamp, response digest, and explicit acquisition or parse status without storing the provider response.
- Not for: Retrieving abstracts, article files, full text, or arbitrary URLs.; Establishing that an article is authentic, supports a claim, is scientifically valid, peer reviewed, or accepted by a venue.; Resolving DOI identifiers, legacy slash-form arXiv identifiers, or provider records outside the modern arXiv identifier format.; Sending confidential task identifiers or metadata to a public service without caller approval.
- Use only if: The caller explicitly approves sending one modern arXiv identifier to the public arXiv API and passes --allow-public-network.; A route proposal includes this exact candidate ID and export.arxiv.org in allowed_network_hosts; a proposal does not authorize execution.; The identifier matches YYMM.NNNN[N] with an optional lowercase vN suffix and the caller accepts unauthenticated provider metadata.; The fixed HTTPS arXiv API is reachable from the declared environment.
- Do not use when: The identifier or task context must remain private and cannot be transmitted to arXiv.; Network access is disallowed, unavailable, or not approved for this invocation.; The task needs full text, an abstract, source authenticity, claim entailment, scientific assessment, or a DOI-based provider comparison.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object with exactly one modern arXiv ID (YYMM.NNNN[N], optionally vN) → Provider-reported arXiv entry ID/version, title, author names, dates, categories, journal reference, and DOI when present, One request URL, retrieval timestamp, response SHA-256, byte count, and claim-ceiling receipt
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `export.arxiv.org` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: When explicitly invoked with --allow-public-network, sends one fixed-host HTTPS GET to export.arxiv.org containing only the caller-supplied arXiv ID; it does not send title text or arbitrary query parameters., Reads bounded UTF-8 JSON and emits one selected metadata receipt to stdout or a validation error to stderr; it creates or modifies no files and retains no raw response., Rejects redirects, non-Atom content, oversized or slow responses, malformed XML, DTD/entity payloads, error entries, and mismatched provider IDs as explicit statuses..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · One bounded public metadata request was observed; comparable latency, reliability, bandwidth, or service-cost profiling has not been done.
- Alternatives: `research.verify_crossref_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Crossref compares caller-provided expected title/year by bare DOI; the arXiv adapter accepts a modern arXiv ID and returns the arXiv provider entry/version. The identifiers and output contracts differ.; `research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): OpenAlex requires a DOI and returns OpenAlex Work fields; the arXiv adapter directly resolves the source arXiv ID and provider entry/version without requiring a DOI..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Resolve a cited modern arXiv ID directly, including the provider entry/version when no DOI is supplied; DOI-based Crossref and OpenAlex candidates cannot consume that identifier as input..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · One real arXiv API lookup for the arXiv ID cited by Workbench #81 returned an arXiv entry/version and no DOI. Current Crossref and OpenAlex adapters require bare DOI inputs. This establishes a distinct identifier contract for that task, not comparative accuracy, coverage beyond this sample, performance, effort, or cost. Comparators: research.verify_crossref_metadata, research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py, ignition/docs/operations/arxiv-metadata-lookup.md, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-arxiv-metadata-live-task-evidence-r0.json, ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_metadata_verifier.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_openalex_work_metadata.py; findings: For the source-bound Workbench #81 cited ID 2610.04074, the live arXiv API returned entry 2610.04074v2 and no DOI. Existing Crossref/OpenAlex adapters accept bare DOI inputs, so they cannot directly resolve this input. This demonstrates specialized identifier coverage; it does not establish a broader provider or performance advantage..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · The live source-bound task returned an arXiv entry/version without a DOI; direct modern arXiv-ID lookup is outside the input contracts of the current DOI-based metadata tools.
- Migration/rollback: Keep this tool opt-in for direct arXiv identifiers. When an arXiv response includes a DOI and DOI-provider fields are specifically required, route that separate task to Crossref or OpenAlex as well. No Current operation or default route changes. / Remove this optional candidate, tool, focused tests, evidence, and scope note from the isolated branch; regenerate the candidate identity cards and TOOLBOX.md; retain the source API URL and recorded evidence if useful..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py --stdin --allow-public-network`
- Fallback: If live lookup is not approved or fails, retain the explicit acquisition outcome and use the official arXiv abstract page only when separately authorized; do not infer that a failed API lookup means the paper does not exist.
- Evidence: `REAL_TASK_OUTCOME` · source refs `ignition/tools/toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py` (BOUNDED_FIXED_HOST_METADATA_LOOKUP), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py` (MOCKED_NETWORK_AND_NEGATIVE_CONTROLS), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py` (DEFAULT_OFF_AND_EXACT_HOST_ROUTING_TEST), `ignition/docs/operations/arxiv-metadata-lookup.md` (API_SCOPE_AND_BLUEPRINT_PROVENANCE), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-arxiv-metadata-live-task-evidence-r0.json` (LIVE_API_TASK_AND_ROUTE_EVIDENCE), `ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_metadata_verifier.py` (DOI_INPUT_COMPARATOR), `ignition/tools/toolbox_openalex_work_metadata.py` (DOI_INPUT_COMPARATOR); focused checks [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py](tests/test_toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py), [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py](tests/test_toolbox_routing.py), [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_identity_cards.py](tests/test_toolbox_identity_cards.py); negative controls: Missing --allow-public-network performs no request and emits no result.; Reject DOI/URL inputs, legacy slash-form IDs, invalid months or versions, duplicate JSON keys, and requests above 4 KiB before network access.; Only export.arxiv.org receives a request; redirects, non-Atom content, oversized or slow bodies, DTD/entities, malformed XML, provider errors, and mismatched record IDs return explicit non-success outcomes.; The receipt excludes abstracts/full text and does not authenticate source identity or scientific claims; invocation creates no files..
- Route default: `false` · conditions: Remain excluded unless this exact candidate ID is explicitly allowlisted for a direct modern arXiv-ID metadata task.; Require the route profile to list export.arxiv.org explicitly; the route planner does not verify caller authority or grant network permission.; Every actual invocation also requires --allow-public-network and a task-specific decision to transmit the identifier.; Do not select this tool only to repeat DOI metadata or infer article support..
- Claim ceiling: Provider-reported selected arXiv metadata for one caller-supplied modern ID only; the result does not authenticate a source, retrieve article content, establish claim support, or imply scientific validity or acceptance.

### `research.lookup_datacite_doi_metadata` · DataCite DOI 元数据查询器 / DataCite DOI metadata lookup

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1. Provider-returned metadata and rights fields are not a grant or legal reuse determination; honor the record and applicable terms.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field grants no Owner, provider, scientific, or legal authority.` · source blob `348b85df7ff2cbaac52b2bc66fb2b3b13a484f55`.
- Can do: Retrieve one exact-DOI-matched DataCite Public API record projection with provider-specific resource types, rights, related identifiers, schema version, and state.
- Good for: Retrieving one caller-known DOI’s DataCite state, schema version, publisher, year, resourceTypeGeneral, and resourceType from the DataCite Public API.; Returning bounded DataCite titles/creators, rights fields, related identifiers, alternate identifiers, and dates while distinguishing unreported, reported-empty, and malformed fields.; Recording one response SHA-256, byte count, HTTP status, retrieval time, and an explicit no-findable-record or acquisition failure outcome.; Adding DataCite-source provenance fields when the task requires the DataCite registration schema alongside OpenAlex, Crossref, or another provider.
- Not for: Discovering DOIs from a topic, searching or harvesting multiple records, authenticated access to Registered or Draft records, or bulk metadata export.; Retrieving landing pages, dataset contents, article text, content URLs, or arbitrary URLs; returned URLs are not emitted or requested.; Establishing DOI nonexistence from a public API 404, complete provider coverage, source authenticity, scientific truth, data availability, or legal reuse permission.; Treating DataCite resource types, rights fields, dates, or related identifiers as a quality score, access grant, or verified relationship.
- Use only if: The caller has exactly one known bare DOI and specifically needs DataCite provider fields not supplied by a local snapshot.; The caller explicitly permits transmission of that DOI to the unauthenticated public DataCite API for this invocation and passes --allow-public-network.; The invocation uses only api.datacite.org, one HTTPS GET, and a response of at most 128 KiB; redirects are blocked.; A route proposal explicitly includes this candidate and allowed_network_hosts contains api.datacite.org; routing itself does not authorize execution.
- Do not use when: No DOI is known, a local source snapshot is sufficient, or the task asks for systematic discovery or bulk collection.; The task requires private Registered/Draft metadata, content retrieval, licensing advice, scientific appraisal, or proof that a DOI does not exist.; The caller has not separately approved the public network request or the exact host is not allowed.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object with exactly one bare DOI identifier string → One exact DOI-matched DataCite Public API metadata projection with state, schema version, resourceTypeGeneral/resourceType, publisher/year, bounded rights/related-identifier fields, per-field presence/counts, response hash, and explicit acquisition outcome.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `ROUTER_GATED_EXPLICIT_SELECTION_AND_PER_CALL_PUBLIC_NETWORK_OPT_IN` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_datacite_doi_metadata.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `api.datacite.org` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: When explicitly invoked, sends exactly one HTTPS GET to https://api.datacite.org/dois/{percent-encoded-doi}, transmitting only the supplied DOI; it uses no credential, API key, query string, arbitrary host, or secondary URL., Keeps at most 128 KiB of response bytes in memory long enough to parse/hash, then emits only the bounded projection and digest to stdout; it writes no repository or provider state., Blocks redirects; uses a 5-second request timeout, a 7-second bounded body read, 8 KiB read chunks, and explicit oversized, timeout, malformed, HTTP, 404, and network outcomes., Does not request the record URL, any content URL, or any related identifier; no data or article content is retrieved..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python standard library only; no third-party runtime dependency.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · The single observed public GET used no authentication or billed account. Per-request provider fees and environment egress costs were not established.
- Alternatives: `research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): OpenAlex supplies its own Work identity, metadata, retraction signal, and location fields. A same-DOI direct endpoint returned an OpenAlex dataset record, but the existing DOI-filter wrapper returned no result on one retry; neither supplies the DataCite schema projection.; `research.verify_crossref_metadata` (ALTERNATE_PROVIDER_DIFFERENT_PROVENANCE): Crossref compares caller-supplied title/year against a Crossref Work record and can expose Crossref relations/references; its same-DOI 404 does not establish DOI nonexistence and does not replace DataCite registration fields..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Return DataCite-registered state, schemaVersion, resourceTypeGeneral/resourceType, and bounded rightsList/relatedIdentifiers fields for one caller-known DOI..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · One purposively selected live dataset DOI comparison across the new DataCite adapter, the existing OpenAlex DOI-filter wrapper plus direct DOI endpoint, and the Crossref verifier. It supports provider-specific scope only; broader performance and quality remain unknown. Comparators: research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata, research.verify_crossref_metadata; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_openalex_work_metadata.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_metadata_verifier.py; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-datacite-doi-metadata-c1-comparison-r0.json; findings: DataCite and the OpenAlex direct DOI endpoint returned the same dataset title and year for DOI 10.25491/3v3x-tj13 using provider-specific projections.; The DataCite adapter returned DataCite schema version, state, resourceTypeGeneral/resourceType, and rights/related-identifier field status; OpenAlex supplies its own Work ID and OpenAlex-specific fields.; The existing OpenAlex DOI-filter wrapper first returned NETWORK_ERROR and then HTTP 200 with NO_RESULT_FOR_FILTER, while a direct OpenAlex DOI endpoint request returned the exact Work; record the route-specific difference without inferring absence.; Crossref returned HTTP 404 for this one DOI. This does not establish that the DOI or work does not exist.; One DOI does not establish provider ranking, coverage, quality, reliability, latency, cost, or a broad replacement case; retain provider-specific tools and preserve uncertainty outside this task..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · Retain as a default-off adapter for the DataCite DOI registration schema and provider-specific fields; the compared tools keep distinct useful outputs and no replacement is supported.
- Migration/rollback: No Current operation is changed and no provider is selected by default. Use DataCite only when its registration fields are explicitly requested; keep OpenAlex Work/location and Crossref title/relation outputs for their separate tasks. / Remove this optional candidate and wrapper from the isolated branch, regenerate identity cards and ignition/TOOLBOX.md, and rerun focused tests. Preserve the matched DOI comparison and route receipts as historical evidence..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_datacite_doi_metadata.py --stdin --allow-public-network`
- Fallback: If the Public API returns no findable record or an explicit acquisition failure, report that outcome without inferring DOI nonexistence; use another provider only when its own fields satisfy the requested task.

- Route default: `false` · conditions: Remain excluded unless the candidate ID is explicitly allowlisted and the caller-supplied typed route profile explicitly allows api.datacite.org.; Only select when the task needs DataCite registration-specific fields for one caller-known DOI and local captured data do not satisfy the request.; Actual invocation separately requires task-specific caller consent and --allow-public-network; a route plan never grants execution authorization..
- Claim ceiling: One exact DOI-matched record returned by the DataCite Public API at retrieval time. The public API exposes Findable records only; no record does not establish DOI nonexistence. Returned metadata does not establish scientific truth, source authenticity, complete coverage, content availability, or legal reuse permission.

### `research.lookup_noaa_global_sf6_annual_growth` · NOAA 全球六氟化硫年度增量读取器 / NOAA global sulfur hexafluoride annual growth lookup

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1; NOAA GML source-use page requests fair credit for its measurements and data.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field confers no NOAA, Owner, emissions-accounting, or scientific authority.` · source blob `34e5d057fdc6fab3afb5a33c69121ab7cd07a198`.
- Can do: Retrieve a bounded year range from NOAA GML’s fixed raw global annual atmospheric SF6 mole-fraction increase table, including the raw table’s provider-reported uncertainty precision.
- Good for: Retrieving NOAA GML global annual atmospheric SF6 mole-fraction increase and raw-table uncertainty for an explicit bounded year range.; Recording one fixed raw-table response digest, retrieval time, HTTP metadata, and explicit missing-year or acquisition status.; Returning the raw table’s stated uncertainty text without interpreting a rounded 0.00 as exact zero uncertainty.
- Not for: Comparing NOAA’s rendered page against its raw table; this tool reads only the raw table.; Estimating SF6 emissions, identifying emitting sectors, attributing causes, or making a scientific conclusion.; Arbitrary URLs, station-level or monthly observations, article content, or unrestricted date ranges.; Treating a missing year as zero or the raw table’s rounded 0.00 uncertainty as no uncertainty.
- Use only if: The caller has decided that one public NOAA GML raw-table request is appropriate and passes --allow-public-network for this invocation.; The request contains exactly integer start_year and end_year values from 1998 through 2100, with no more than 50 inclusive years.; A proposed route explicitly includes this candidate ID and allowed_network_hosts contains exactly gml.noaa.gov; route planning is not execution authorization.
- Do not use when: Public network access is disallowed or the caller must not contact NOAA GML.; The task requires higher precision for uncertainty than NOAA’s raw table reports; consult the rendered NOAA page or a separately validated source.; The task needs an emissions inventory, source attribution, causal explanation, station data, or another gas species.; The requested range exceeds the tool’s bounds, or a precise historical snapshot is required and the live response is not archived.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object with schema_version toolbox-noaa-sf6-annual-growth-lookup-input-r0 and exactly integer start_year/end_year (1998–2100 inclusive; range at most 50 years) → Provider-reported annual SF6 atmospheric mole-fraction increase and raw-table uncertainty in ppt for each returned year, with explicit FOUND, PARTIAL_DATA, or NO_MATCH status, Fixed NOAA source URL, retrieval timestamp, HTTP status, ETag/Last-Modified when supplied, response SHA-256, and source caveats
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_noaa_sf6_annual_growth_lookup.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_noaa_source_surface_comparison_core.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `gml.noaa.gov` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: When explicitly invoked with --allow-public-network, sends exactly one HTTPS GET to the fixed NOAA GML SF6 raw-table URL; year bounds are applied locally and no caller URL or private identifier is transmitted., Writes a bounded selected-record JSON receipt to stdout and errors to stderr; does not retain the raw response or write repository files., Rejects redirects, non-text content, oversized or slow responses, malformed headers/tables, duplicate/out-of-order years, and invalid numeric rows as explicit outcomes..
- Dependencies: ignition/tools/toolbox_noaa_source_surface_comparison_core.py; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.10 standard library, ignition/tools/toolbox_noaa_source_surface_comparison_core.py.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · Exactly one fixed public HTTPS GET is attempted per explicitly opted-in invocation. Monetary cost, stable latency, long-run availability, and parser reliability are not measured.
- Alternatives: `research.lookup_noaa_global_n2o_annual_growth` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The N2O adapter reads a different NOAA gas table and reports ppb; it cannot provide SF6 values or their ppt units.; `research.lookup_noaa_global_ch4_annual_growth` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The CH4 adapter reads a different NOAA gas table and reports ppb; it cannot provide SF6 values.; `research.lookup_noaa_global_co2_annual_growth` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The CO2 adapter reads a different NOAA gas table and reports ppm; it cannot provide SF6 values..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Retrieve an SF6-specific annual-growth series and its exactly published raw-table precision with one fixed GET; CO2, CH4, and N2O tools cannot satisfy the distinct species and unit contract..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · Same-species NOAA page/raw values were compared for 2025, and the SF6 raw-table adapter was compared by function and output contract with existing NOAA CO2, CH4, and N2O adapters. Page and raw surfaces report the same 2025 growth (0.48 ppt), while the raw uncertainty is rounded to 0.00 ppt and the page reports 0.004 ppt. This is a precision limitation, not evidence that either surface is superior. Other-gas tools are not substitutes. No overall accuracy, reliability, latency, or monetary-cost ranking is claimed. Comparators: research.lookup_noaa_global_co2_annual_growth, research.lookup_noaa_global_ch4_annual_growth, research.lookup_noaa_global_n2o_annual_growth; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-noaa-sf6-annual-growth-lookup-real-task-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-noaa-sf6-annual-growth-lookup-c1-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-noaa-sf6-annual-growth-lookup-source-binding-r0.json, ignition/docs/operations/noaa-sf6-annual-growth-lookup.md, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_noaa_sf6_annual_growth_lookup.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_noaa_sf6_annual_growth_lookup_routing.py; findings: The candidate adds an SF6-specific species and fixed raw-table endpoint absent from the current candidate catalog; the CO2, CH4, and N2O tools cannot return the same gas or its ppt values.; One live matched observation returned 2025 growth 0.48 ppt from both NOAA page and raw table. The raw table’s 0.00 ppt uncertainty is rounded; the page reported 0.004 ppt. Neither tool treats the latter value as independent validation.; The candidate makes one fixed request for the raw-only task. Current metadata does not establish comparative source reliability, accuracy, long-run availability, latency, or monetary cost.; No incumbent is retired. The gas-specific tools coexist by distinct source, unit, and task contract; no new tool is router-selectable by default..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · Retain as a separate optional default-off SF6 raw-table reader. Existing CO2, CH4, and N2O tools remain for their distinct species and units. No incumbent is retired.
- Migration/rollback: No Current registry or default route migration is made. Explicit selection is required only for an SF6 raw-table request; page/raw comparison and higher precision uncertainty tasks remain counterindicated. / Remove this optional catalog entry, wrapper, schema, docs, fixtures, tests, route/source/C1 evidence, and real-task output from the isolated branch; regenerate identity cards and TOOLBOX.md; preserve source digests and the precision limitation evidence if useful..

### `research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata` · OpenAlex Work 元数据查询器 / OpenAlex Work metadata lookup

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.2.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1. OpenAlex states its metadata is CC0: https://help.openalex.org/data/how-its-built/. Public API usage is metered: https://help.openalex.org/api/authentication/ and https://help.openalex.org/access/example-costs/.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field grants no Owner, retraction, or scientific authority.` · source blob `3aaad5f911bd0f7f44aeb0c6e08389cf5de12058`.
- Can do: Retrieve a bounded exact-primary-DOI-matched OpenAlex Work projection, including provider-reported open-access and location metadata.
- Good for: Retrieving an exact-primary-DOI-matched OpenAlex Work ID, title, publication year, work type, and the API-reported is_retracted flag.; Returning OpenAlex-reported open_access, best_oa_location, and up to 25 sanitized location records with an explicit count and truncation flag.; Recording a bounded OpenAlex response digest, HTTP result, and server-reported rate-limit and usage fields without retaining the raw response.; Adding OpenAlex-specific work identity, location metadata, and retraction-signal output when a task explicitly requests those provider fields alongside another source.
- Not for: Retrieving abstracts, article full text, publisher pages, or arbitrary URLs; returned location URLs are never requested.; Establishing source authenticity, scientific truth, article-content support, URL availability, legal reuse rights, or complete OpenAlex coverage.; Treating OpenAlex is_retracted as an authoritative or complete retraction adjudication; the documented signal is sourced from Retraction Watch.; Treating no exact primary DOI match as proof that a work does not exist; DOI filters can match DOI values on work locations.; Selecting a best guess when multiple exact primary DOI matches are returned or updating repository source records.
- Use only if: The caller explicitly permits transmitting the supplied public DOI identifiers for this invocation and passes --allow-public-network.; The invocation uses only the fixed HTTPS host api.openalex.org and a JSON stdin object with one to five unique bare DOI identifiers.; A route proposal may include this candidate only when the task explicitly requires OpenAlex Work identity, location, or other provider-reported fields and allowed_network_hosts contains api.openalex.org.; The route proposal remains non-authoritative; actual invocation consent is checked separately by the CLI flag and task-specific decision.
- Do not use when: A DOI is confidential or must not be transmitted to a public scholarly metadata service.; Network access is unavailable, disallowed, or not explicitly approved for this invocation.; The task needs an authoritative or complete retraction decision, article-content review, URL access verification, reuse-rights determination, or scientific assessment.; The task requires an exhaustive search, an unbounded query, cursor paging, or full raw-response retention.
- Inputs → outputs: JSON stdin object with one to five unique bare DOI identifiers → input-ordered OpenAlex Work ID and selected bibliographic metadata rows including the provider-reported is_retracted signal, OpenAlex-reported open-access summary, best OA location, and up to 25 sanitized location records per matched Work; provider count and truncation state remain explicit, per-request response SHA-256, HTTP status, and public API usage fields
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_openalex_work_metadata.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `api.openalex.org` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: When explicitly invoked, sends one HTTPS GET to api.openalex.org/works. It transmits only the caller-supplied DOI values in a DOI filter; no email, API key, title, source ID, credential, arbitrary host, or other external action is sent., Selects id, doi, display_name, publication_year, type, is_retracted, open_access, locations, and best_oa_location; keeps the raw body in memory long enough to parse and hash, then emits no raw response or article content and writes no repository files., Projects no more than 25 location records per Work. It returns only bounded source fields and HTTPS URLs without embedded credentials or nonstandard ports; unsafe URL fields are omitted. It does not follow, resolve, or request any returned URL., Does not follow redirects or retry; uses a 5-second socket timeout, 7-second response-body budget, 8 KiB bounded reads, and a 256 KiB response cap. HTTP, rate-limit, malformed, oversized, ambiguous, non-exact, and network-error outcomes remain explicit., A prior v0.1 request and the successful v0.2 same-task request each reported one API credit; the successful v0.2 response had meta.cost=null. A separate earlier v0.2 invocation returned NETWORK_ERROR. These observations do not establish stable cost or latency; no API key or paid account was used..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library; no third-party runtime dependency.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · One bounded API request per invocation. One prior v0.1 and one successful v0.2 request each reported one API credit, while v0.2 meta.cost was null; a separate v0.2 request failed with NETWORK_ERROR. These single observations do not establish stable cost, latency, or paid-account cost.
- Alternatives: `research.verify_crossref_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Crossref compares caller-supplied expected title/year to Crossref fields; OpenAlex returns its Work ID and Retraction Watch-derived signal. One paired DOI matched on title/year but cannot establish provider quality or replacement value.; `research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The PubMed/PMC resolver maps DOI identifiers to NCBI IDs; this tool returns OpenAlex Work identity and selected OpenAlex metadata.; `ignition.scripts.external-research.openalex_client.fetch_by_doi` (EXISTING_BROADER_LIBRARY_FUNCTION_NOT_TOOLBOX_EQUIVALENT): The existing general client supports broader DOI Work metadata and abstract/search workflows. This wrapper adds an opt-in fixed-host contract, exact-primary-DOI matching, bounded location projection, response hashes, and explicit ambiguity. The implementations have distinct boundaries; no comparison establishes overall superiority or retirement.; `research.resolve_europe_pmc_fulltext_locations` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Europe PMC returns its own typed-identifier fullTextUrlList and availability codes; OpenAlex returns Work-level locations and an open_access summary. One same-DOI comparison observed eight OpenAlex location records and three Europe PMC entries, but these provider-specific sets are not comparable as coverage measures and establish no quality or replacement ranking.; `research.search_openalex_query` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The query adapter discovers OpenAlex Work IDs from public text; this adapter requires known DOI identifiers and returns exact-primary-DOI metadata/location fields..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Provide the optional router a constrained OpenAlex-only DOI lookup contract with exact-primary-DOI matching, selected metadata, response hash, and reported usage without exposing abstracts or arbitrary query URLs.; Return OpenAlex Work IDs and provider-reported location/open_access fields when those source-specific outputs are explicitly requested; Europe PMC remains a separate provider-specific fullTextUrlList and availability-code path, and the general OpenAlex client remains the wider search/abstract path..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · One same-DOI live location comparison between OpenAlex v0.2 and Europe PMC v0.2. OpenAlex returned eight Work-level location projections and an open_access summary; Europe PMC returned three fullTextUrlList entries with its availability codes and document styles. This supports distinct provider-specific outputs for this DOI only, not broad location coverage, comparative quality, latency, cost, URL validity, or legal reuse. One paired DOI does not establish completeness, broad reliability, quality, stable cost, or replacement.; The bounded OpenAlex topic-query candidate uses public text query-to-record discovery; this exact-DOI adapter requires caller-known identifiers. ### `research.match_crossref_citation` · Crossref 引文候选匹配器 / Crossref citation candidate matcher

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1; Crossref bibliographic metadata reuse basis is documented at https://www.crossref.org/documentation/retrieve-metadata/.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner, source-authentication, or scientific authority.` · source blob `99fcf198d676ad1f9d505879d155733d36714415`.
- Can do: Return up to two Crossref Works candidates for one caller-supplied bibliographic citation using Crossref query.bibliographic; never choose or verify a DOI.
- Good for: Finding a small set of Crossref Works candidate records from one public bibliographic citation when no DOI is supplied in the query.; Preserving Crossref provider row order, any returned relevance scores, bounded bibliographic fields, response digest, retrieval timestamp, and explicit HTTP/acquisition status.; A caller-reviewed citation lookup where the exact Crossref query.bibliographic contract is useful and at most two candidates are sufficient for triage.
- Not for: Declaring that a returned DOI is a correct match, selecting a winner automatically, or resolving citation ambiguity without human review.; Topic or systematic-review search, exhaustive recall, bulk harvesting, or high-frequency requests.; Retrieving abstracts, references, full text, publisher pages, arbitrary URLs, correction status, or scientific evidence.; Proving that a work does not exist from an empty result, provider failure, or incomplete metadata.
- Use only if: The caller supplies exactly one trimmed, public, non-sensitive citation of at most 2048 characters and 4096 UTF-8 bytes.; The caller separately approves transmitting this exact citation to Crossref for the current invocation and passes --allow-public-network.; The candidate is explicitly allowlisted, and the typed route profile names api.crossref.org in allowed_network_hosts.; No local source already supplies the required citation identifier and a two-row provider candidate response is sufficient.
- Do not use when: The citation or task context is confidential, patient-identifying, personal-health, or otherwise unsuitable for a public API.; The DOI is already known and the existing Crossref metadata verifier satisfies the task.; The task needs broad topic discovery, exhaustive search, source authentication, article-content assessment, or scientific review.; Network use has not been approved for this invocation or Crossref is rate-limiting requests.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object containing exactly one trimmed public citation string (maximum 2048 characters and 4096 UTF-8 bytes). → At most two provider-ordered Crossref Work candidates with optional DOI, title, year, type, publisher, container, volume, issue, page, and bounded author names., One-row request/response SHA-256 digests, retrieval timestamp, HTTP/acquisition outcome, bounded usage headers, and a claim ceiling; raw input and response text are omitted.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_SINGLE_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_citation_matcher.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `api.crossref.org` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Only when separately invoked with --allow-public-network, sends one HTTPS GET to api.crossref.org/v1/works with the caller citation in query.bibliographic; provider-side request logging is outside the wrapper., Sends no mailto, user email, API key, Authorization header, or other credential; follows no redirect, retries no request, and writes no repository files., Uses a 5-second socket timeout, an 8-second response-read budget checked between bounded reads, a 128 KiB response cap, and a small two-row projection; rate limiting and latency are not hidden..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library; no third-party runtime dependency..
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · One Crossref request is permitted per explicit invocation. No stable latency, bandwidth, availability, human-review, or monetary-cost profile has been measured.
- Alternatives: `research.search_crossref_query` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The existing adapter exposes Crossref general query for topic discovery and can accept citation text, but does not use the dedicated query.bibliographic field or expose the same candidate score contract. One same-task comparison is insufficient to replace general topic search.; `research.match_pubmed_citation` (COMPLEMENTARY_DIFFERENT_PROVIDER_AND_OUTPUT): The PubMed adapter accepts a structured journal/year/volume/page/author citation and reports a PMID; this tool accepts a single citation string and reports Crossref Work metadata candidates.; `research.verify_crossref_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The Crossref verifier checks a caller-known DOI; this candidate is for a citation query when the DOI is not supplied..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Use Crossref query.bibliographic for a caller-approved single citation lookup when the general Crossref query top rows do not surface the known expected record; the one observed task does not establish broad superiority or unique coverage..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · One public citation string was sent to Crossref twice: once through query.bibliographic and once through the existing general query field, both with rows=2. On this task the dedicated query returned the known historical DOI at provider rank 1, while the general query returned two different works. This is one interface-level observation; it does not estimate citation accuracy, recall, reliability, or broad superiority. Comparators: research.search_crossref_query; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_query_search.py; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-citation-matcher-c1-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-citation-matcher-real-task-evidence-r0.json; findings: For the shared title-and-year citation, query.bibliographic returned DOI 10.1007/s10489-024-05338-9 at rank 1, matching the previously recorded source-registry DOI; the general query top two did not contain that DOI.; The generic adapter remains useful for broad topic discovery, allows up to ten rows, and uses a different query contract; the specialized candidate is optional for citation-shaped lookup and does not replace topic search.; Neither the provider rank nor this one comparison authenticates a source, establishes citation correctness generally, or supports a scientific claim. Latency and billed cost remain unknown..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · The single same-task result shows a concrete task-scoped distinction from generic Crossref query search, while evidence is insufficient for broad superiority or replacement; preserve both routes by task and keep this candidate default-off.
- Migration/rollback: No Current operation or incumbent route changes. Call only after explicit selection for a public citation and review the provider candidates manually. / Remove this experimental card, adapter, tests, fixture, schemas, documentation, and dedicated evidence from the isolated candidate; regenerate identity projections. Preserve the C1 result and historical commit as evidence..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_citation_matcher.py --stdin --allow-public-network`
- Fallback: If transmission is not approved, preserve NOT_RUN and use a locally available citation source. On rate limit, network failure, no candidate, or ambiguity, retain the explicit status and request an authorized source or human bibliographic review; never infer nonexistence or choose a DOI automatically.
- Evidence: `REAL_TASK_OUTCOME` · source refs `ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_citation_matcher.py` (BOUNDED_FIXED_HOST_QUERY_BIBLIOGRAPHIC_ADAPTER), `ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-crossref-citation-matcher-input-r0.schema.json` (STRICT_CITATION_INPUT_CONTRACT), `ignition/schemas/operations/toolbox-crossref-citation-matcher-output-r0.schema.json` (BOUNDED_PROVIDER_RECEIPT_CONTRACT), `ignition/tests/fixtures/toolbox-crossref-citation-matcher/valid-input-r0.json` (PUBLIC_NON_SENSITIVE_CITATION_FIXTURE), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_crossref_citation_matcher.py` (MOCKED_NETWORK_NEGATIVE_CONTROLS_AND_DEFAULT_OFF_ROUTING), `ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_query_search.py` (SAME_TASK_GENERIC_QUERY_COMPARATOR), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-citation-matcher-real-task-evidence-r0.json` (LIVE_PUBLIC_CITATION_TASK_RECEIPT_SUMMARY), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-citation-matcher-c1-evidence-r0.json` (SAME_TASK_C1_COMPARISON), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-citation-matcher-route-evidence-r0.json` (DEFAULT_OFF_ROUTE_EXPERIMENT); Only api.crossref.org is allowed; redirects, non-JSON payloads, oversized responses, timeouts, HTTP errors, and malformed provider payloads are explicit.; Raw citation, abstracts, references, and provider response bodies are never included in the result; no files are written.; Route planning remains default-off; even a complete explicit-host proposal has execution_authorized=false..
- Route default: `false` · conditions: Remain excluded unless research.match_crossref_citation is explicitly allowlisted for a caller-reviewed citation-to-Crossref-candidate task.; The typed route profile must explicitly name api.crossref.org; that host list does not authenticate caller authority.; Every actual invocation separately requires task-specific approval to transmit the citation and the --allow-public-network flag.; The planner never grants permission to select a DOI or treat a candidate as verified..
- Claim ceiling: Reports at most two Crossref Works candidates and their provider-reported metadata for one supplied public citation at retrieval time. It does not verify a citation-to-DOI match, establish source authenticity, metadata completeness, article content, scientific support, or that no other work exists.

### `research.match_pubmed_citation` · PubMed 引文标识符匹配器 / PubMed Citation Matcher

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1. NCBI E-utilities usage terms apply; the adapter emits no article text and makes no reuse-right determination. https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/home/about/policies/ and https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/About/disclaimer.html.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `a8586fb4d3f81251f49bb19ab59217dc3ba66f19`.
- Can do: Map one caller-supplied structured public citation to the PMID reported by the fixed NCBI ECitMatch endpoint; return no article metadata or text.
- Good for: Returning a provider-reported PubMed PMID for one public citation supplied as journal title, year, volume, first page, and first author.; Recording whether the provider reports no match while keeping citation and response hashes for later review.; Using a structured citation when the caller has neither a DOI nor an existing PMID and needs only an identifier candidate.
- Not for: Searching arbitrary free text, retrieving article metadata, abstracts, full text, or publisher content.; Authenticating the citation, proving the returned record is bibliographically correct, or assessing relevance or scientific support.; Concluding that an article does not exist from NO_MATCH_REPORTED or a provider/network failure.; Using confidential, patient-identifying, or otherwise sensitive citation fields with a public service.
- Use only if: The caller supplies exactly one structured, public, non-sensitive citation and explicitly permits sending those five fields to NCBI.; The invocation separately passes --allow-public-network and permits eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov.; A DOI-to-identifier adapter or an already-known PMID is not available for the task; the candidate remains excluded unless explicitly allowlisted.
- Do not use when: The citation fields are confidential, patient-identifying, or otherwise inappropriate to transmit to a public provider.; Network use is unavailable or has not been separately authorized for this invocation.; The task requires title/abstract/full-text review, exhaustive or free-text search, bibliographic adjudication, or scientific assessment.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object with exactly journal_title, year, volume, first_page, and first_author; public non-sensitive citation → One provider-reported PubMed PMID for the structured citation, or an explicit NO_MATCH_REPORTED observation, with citation/response hashes and bounded status
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_SINGLE_FIXED_HOST_CITATION_LOOKUP` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_citation_matcher.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: When the caller separately permits the public lookup and passes --allow-public-network, sends exactly one HTTPS GET with the five structured citation fields and a fixed tool parameter to eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov; no email or API key is sent., Does not follow redirects, retry, access arbitrary URLs, write files, mutate the repository, or return a title, abstract, or full text. The caller-provided citation fields may be logged by the provider outside this wrapper., The response digest binds received bytes but not provider authenticity; no raw provider body is persisted..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · One public request was observed for the official example; no billed cost, latency distribution, service reliability, or analyst-time comparison was measured.
- Alternatives: `research.search_pubmed_query` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The query adapter accepts a caller-composed PubMed query and returns bounded ESummary metadata; it requires a caller contact email and is not an exact structured-citation input contract.; `research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The identifier resolver accepts DOI values; this matcher covers a structured citation when DOI and PMID are absent..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Match one structured public citation to a provider-reported PubMed identifier when no DOI or PMID is supplied and only the identifier is needed..
- Comparison evidence: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · An earlier source version and one direct ESearch query generated from the same public structured citation returned the same PMID. The final-source recheck ended in NETWORK_ERROR. The direct ESearch probe was not a full search_pubmed_query invocation, and neither attempt establishes bibliographic accuracy, equivalence, broad quality, reliability, or cost. Comparators: research.search_pubmed_query, research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_query_search.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-citation-matcher-real-task-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-citation-matcher-c1-evidence-r0.json; findings: An earlier source version and a one-request ESearch probe reported PMID 2014248 for the official example citation; the final-source public request ended in NETWORK_ERROR.; The low-cost comparison checks one historical returned identifier only; it does not test metadata accuracy, search recall, failure rates, source authenticity, user effort, reliability, or monetary cost.; The DOI resolver cannot consume this citation-only input. The generic query adapter keeps its broader query-to-metadata purpose and is not shown to be wholly replaced.; Keep replacement_candidates empty and preserve COMPARISON_UNKNOWN; no existing adapter is retired or deprecated..
- Deprecation/retirement: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · One same-citation example is insufficient to establish replacement or retirement; preserve this candidate as optional and keep the existing PubMed query and DOI resolver scopes.
- Migration/rollback: No Current registry or existing call path is changed. Select this adapter only when a structured citation-to-PMID result is explicitly required and the caller permits the fixed-host request. / Remove the optional card, adapter, schemas, fixture, tests, documentation, and its route/C1/task evidence from the isolated branch; regenerate identity projections; preserve this evidence and prior commits as history..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_citation_matcher.py --stdin --allow-public-network`
- Fallback: If public lookup is not permitted or fails, retain the exact acquisition or NOT_RUN status and ask for a DOI/PMID or an authorized manual path; never infer absence from failure or from no match.

### `research.merge_selected_index_search_results` · 多索引检索结果 DOI 聚组器 / Selected-index DOI result grouper

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.2.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field grants no provider, Owner, scientific, or publication authority.` · source blob `df1b03d64247ec72a68a34421e3f7f7ce356b29c`.
- Can do: Merge bounded Crossref, OpenAlex, or Europe PMC topic-search receipts offline. Group only exact normalized DOI matches and flag identical normalized titles with multiple distinct DOIs for human review without merging them.
- Good for: Grouping exact DOI matches across two or three already collected bounded Crossref, OpenAlex, or Europe PMC topic-search receipts.; Preserving provider-specific status, endpoint, query hash, response hash, count, retrieval time, and bounded source rows in an offline result.; Surfacing records with an identical normalized title and multiple distinct normalized DOIs for human review while leaving all records unmerged.
- Not for: Searching a provider, making network requests, retrieving abstracts or full text, or expanding a source result set.; Systematic-review completeness, provider ranking comparison, fuzzy title matching, citation correctness, or evidence synthesis.; Treating an exact DOI group as source authentication, a publication-status check, or scientific support.; Treating an exact-title/multiple-DOI review candidate as a duplicate, related version, or reason to merge records.
- Use only if: The task explicitly needs two or more provider topic-search result sets grouped or needs exact-title/different-DOI cases surfaced for review.; Every input is a bounded receipt from one of the supported fixed-endpoint adapters and preserves its query and response fingerprints.; A single provider adapter is insufficient for the requested cross-index view.
- Do not use when: One source is sufficient or the task is a known-DOI metadata comparison.; Inputs are raw, unbounded, malformed, from unsupported providers, or do not preserve source identity and hashes.; The task needs relevance ranking across providers, complete recall, fuzzy identity resolution, or scientific conclusions.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON object containing two or three complete bounded Crossref, OpenAlex, or Europe PMC topic-search receipts; each retains provider identity, endpoint, query/response hashes, bounded records, and counts. → Offline normalized source summaries and exact normalized DOI cross-index groups; same-title or same-year records are never merged., Exact normalized-title records with multiple distinct valid DOIs surfaced as review-only candidates; warnings never create identity groups.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `BOUNDED_OFFLINE_STDIN_PROCESS` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_merge_selected_index_search_results.py` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads bounded stdin JSON and writes a deterministic JSON summary to stdout or an error to stderr; no network requests, repository reads, file writes, or external actions..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library; no third-party runtime packages..
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · The tool performs only local validation and comparison over at most 30 provider rows. No matched latency, memory, analyst-time, or monetary benchmark was run.
- Alternatives: `research.search_crossref_query` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Retrieves one bounded Crossref topic result set; it does not merge already captured multi-provider receipts.; `research.search_openalex_query` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Retrieves one bounded OpenAlex topic result set; it does not merge already captured multi-provider receipts.; `research.search_europe_pmc_query` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Retrieves one bounded Europe PMC topic result set; it does not merge already captured multi-provider receipts.; `research.compare_publication_metadata_snapshots` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Compares selected metadata fields for a supplied known DOI pair; this tool groups matching DOI identifiers across topic-search result sets and reports title/DOI variants without resolving them..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Offline union and exact normalized DOI grouping of already captured bounded topic-search receipts from two or three selected provider indexes.; Preserve cross-index identifier overlap separately from each provider-specific ranking and metadata record without title-based identity inference.; Flag identical normalized titles with multiple distinct normalized DOIs as human-review candidates while leaving DOI-group results unchanged..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · One paired offline replay on the same ten-row captured Crossref/OpenAlex input, comparing the preserved 0.1.0 output with 0.2.0, plus contract comparison against the provider adapters and known-DOI comparator. No same-task human decision-quality, broad reliability, time, or cost benchmark. Comparators: research.search_crossref_query, research.search_openalex_query, research.search_europe_pmc_query, research.compare_publication_metadata_snapshots; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_query_search.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_openalex_query_search.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_europe_pmc_query_search.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_compare_publication_metadata.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_merge_selected_index_search_results.py; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-selected-index-search-result-merge-c1-evidence-r1.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-selected-index-search-result-merge-real-task-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-selected-index-search-result-merge-real-task-r1.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-selected-index-search-result-merge-input-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-selected-index-search-result-merge-output-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-selected-index-search-result-merge-output-r1.json; findings: The 0.2.0 same-input replay preserves the 0.1.0 exact DOI cluster result (zero groups and ten unclustered rows) and adds one review-only warning for four records sharing an identical normalized title but carrying four distinct DOI values.; The warning does not merge records or change unclustered counts. No replacement or retirement is supported..

### `research.pair_noaa_co2_ch4_growth_by_year` · NOAA 温室气体年增长配对器 / NOAA Greenhouse Gas Annual Growth Pairer

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.2.0` · license `Pairer wrapper BUSL-1.1; NOAA GML source-use page requests fair credit for its measurements and data carried by the supplied comparator outputs.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field grants no NOAA, Owner, emissions-accounting, or scientific authority.` · source blob `bedccaf15039a007e88bc6966c28a4d4a7b4a163`.
- Can do: Align already captured NOAA GML CO2 and CH4 page/raw comparison outputs by year, with an optional N2O comparator output. The 0.2.0 candidate preserves each gas status, original units, source receipts, response digests and capture-time span; it performs no network retrieval or cross-gas arithmetic.
- Good for: Creating a same-year table from already captured NOAA GML CO2 and CH4 comparator outputs, optionally including a captured N2O comparator output.; Retaining separate ppm/ppb values, per-gas page/raw comparison statuses, source receipts, retrieval times and response digests in one bounded output.; Offline review that needs explicit year-key alignment and must retain that captures were not simultaneous.
- Not for: Fetching current NOAA data, resolving source access, or using arbitrary URLs or caller-supplied file paths.; Converting, summing, weighting, dividing or ranking CO2, CH4 or N2O values, asserting that unlike gases are commensurate, or analyzing cross-gas co-movement.; Estimating emissions, attributing sources, making causal claims, authenticating NOAA, validating measurements, or establishing scientific truth.
- Use only if: The caller supplies complete version-0.1.0 CO2 and CH4 NOAA GML page/raw comparator outputs; an N2O comparator output is optional and accepted only in input schema r1.; Every supplied comparator output uses its exact fixed NOAA GML endpoints, complete source-consistent rows, valid UTC receipt metadata and SHA-256 digests, and the same inclusive year range; N2O is supported from 2001 onward.; The UTF-8 stdin envelope is no larger than 256 KiB and contains no unknown keys, caller-selected paths, or alternate URLs.
- Do not use when: Only one gas, a raw table value, or a fresh source retrieval is needed.; Any supplied output is unavailable, malformed, mismatched in year range, or unable to provide valid fixed-source receipts.; The requested result would combine unlike units, infer relative contribution, estimate emissions, attribute sources, or claim simultaneous measurement or causation.
- Inputs → outputs: Legacy UTF-8 JSON stdin envelope with schema_version toolbox-noaa-co2-ch4-growth-pair-input-r0, or schema r1 with no n2o_output, containing complete version-0.1.0 CO2 and CH4 comparator outputs from the fixed NOAA GML page/raw surfaces with the same inclusive year range., UTF-8 JSON stdin envelope with schema_version toolbox-noaa-co2-ch4-growth-pair-input-r1 containing complete version-0.1.0 CO2, CH4 and N2O comparator outputs from fixed NOAA GML page/raw surfaces; all request the same inclusive year range and N2O starts no earlier than 2001. → Same-year separate NOAA GML CO2 (ppm) and CH4 (ppb) source-surface comparison rows with per-gas statuses; no cross-gas arithmetic., Four fixed-source receipts, retrieval timestamps, response digests, upstream request counts, comparator-output digests and combined capture-time span., Same-year separate NOAA GML CO2 (ppm), CH4 (ppb) and N2O (ppb) source-surface comparison rows with per-gas statuses; no cross-gas arithmetic or simultaneity claim., Six fixed-source receipts, retrieval timestamps, response digests, upstream request counts, comparator-output digests and combined capture-time span.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_RUN_NO_NETWORK_NO_FILESYSTEM_ACCESS` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `none` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads at most 256 KiB of caller-supplied JSON from stdin and writes one JSON result to stdout or a bounded validation error to stderr., Does not read or write repository or caller files; does not use network, credentials, billing or external actions..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · Reads at most 256 KiB and aligns at most 50 rows per gas using the Python standard library; it makes no network requests or paid calls. CPU and latency have not been benchmarked.
- Alternatives: `research.compare_noaa_co2_source_surfaces` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Produces the CO2 page/raw result in ppm; it does not accept a CH4 comparator output or create a cross-gas year-key table.; `research.compare_noaa_ch4_source_surfaces` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Produces the CH4 page/raw result in ppb; it does not accept a CO2 comparator output or create a cross-gas year-key table..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Create a same-year table for captured CO2, CH4 and optional N2O comparator outputs while retaining unlike units, each gas’s status and the full capture-time span..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · Same captured 2023-2025 two-gas task compared prior 0.1.0 output with 0.2.0 after normalizing only schema_version and tool_version; then replayed the same CO2/CH4 captures with a third captured N2O comparator output. The 0.2.0 two-gas mode retained exact prior rows, metrics, provenance, claim limits and payload digest; the three-gas mode added the N2O rows and provenance. Comparators: research.pair_noaa_co2_ch4_growth_by_year; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_pair_noaa_co2_ch4_growth_by_year.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pair_noaa_co2_ch4_growth_by_year.py; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-noaa-co2-ch4-growth-pair-real-task-output-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-noaa-co2-ch4-growth-pair-v0-2-real-task-output-r1.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-noaa-co2-ch4-growth-pair-n2o-extension-real-task-output-r1.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-noaa-co2-ch4-growth-pair-v0-2-c1-evidence-r0.json; findings: The 0.2.0 candidate accepts the legacy two-gas r0 input and preserves its complete 2023-2025 output semantics; the captured prior 0.1.0 result matches after normalizing only the two version fields.; The same candidate aligns all three gases by year and retains six source receipts, three comparator digests, independent units and the combined capture-time span; 3 of 3 captured years had two-surface values for every gas.; The version extension supersedes 0.1.0 for both the existing two-gas task and the new three-gas task. CPU, analyst time, latency and broad scientific utility remain unmeasured..
- Deprecation/retirement: `ACTIVE` · 0.2.0 is the current isolated version of this tool ID and supersedes the 0.1.0 implementation with legacy two-gas input support plus optional N2O alignment. The 0.1.0 captures and comparison evidence remain preserved as history.
- Migration/rollback: Existing two-gas r0 stdin envelopes remain accepted without edits; new optional N2O alignment uses r1 and requires the third comparator output.; The tool remains experimental and default-off; Current registry, permissions, upstream comparators and automatic route activation are unchanged. / Restore the pre-0.2.0 candidate commit and regenerate identity cards and TOOLBOX.md.; Retain the 0.2.0 matched-task and three-gas output evidence as historical records; keep independent CO2, CH4 and N2O comparator captures..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_pair_noaa_co2_ch4_growth_by_year.py --stdin`
- Fallback: Keep the two source comparator outputs separate and report that pairing was not run. Do not fetch missing data, treat a missing year as zero, or construct cross-gas arithmetic.

### `research.preflight_g1_goal_intake` · Goal 提案只读预检器 / Goal proposal read-only preflight

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `6539d319463d24cb15b8fa637e1b083b39fce004`.
- Can do: Evaluate one caller-supplied V1 ResearchEpisodeRequest envelope against existing Steering value semantics and emit a bounded proposal-only report.
- Good for: Checking caller-supplied Intent, Goal, Completion Contract, version, parent, and objective-digest bindings in one closed ResearchEpisodeRequest proposal envelope.; Detecting malformed or stale/unbound lineage, policy expansion, budget changes, weakened stop conditions, and forbidden RUN_PASS-to-completion shortcuts.; Producing a bounded machine report while keeping Owner authentication, live Current lookup, canonical Goal mutation, completion, approval receipts, and dispatch outside the tool.
- Not for: Authenticating the person named in the input, Owner identity, trust roots, or current canonical Goal/Intent state.; Activating, editing, completing, approving, or dispatching a Goal or research task.; Interpreting a structurally valid proposal as truth, scientific validity, independent review, or G1 completion.; Writing report files; the advertised Toolbox wrapper accepts stdin and emits stdout only.
