### `research.probe_counterfactual_fragility` · 反事实脆弱性探针 / Counterfactual fragility probe

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `toolbox-wrapper-0.1.0; Function OS 0.2.1rc0 candidate` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `f4959e685433592f165e86cdbd2d6ec0a28c9e0c`.
- Can do: Run one bounded Function OS N1-N7 baseline and up to eight independent one-input edits, each bound to a caller-declared load-bearing assumption; report model-conditional stability, expected-direction mismatch, and retained failures.
- Good for: Compare one declared model baseline with up to eight independent one-input counterfactual edits tied to explicit load-bearing assumptions.; See whether the declared model output changes, whether the caller-predicted direction appears, and whether any case failed or remained unassessable.; Keep every probe conditional on the same unedited baseline, with explicit model-only claim limits and no source-evidence mutation.
- Not for: Establish actual-world evidence, causal identification, scientific truth, model validity, proof, or whether a real-world intervention is admissible.; Run arbitrary Python, untrusted code, stateful functions, effectful operations, multi-output models, or inputs outside Function OS bounds.; Replace evidence review, empirical testing, statistical sensitivity analysis, or domain expert judgment.
- Use only if: Python 3.10 or newer and the checked-in Function OS evaluator and v0.2 source tree are available and intact.; The caller supplies a single-output pure numeric or boolean FunctionSpec, exact baseline inputs, explicit input-to-assumption bindings, and one to eight finite scalar edits.; Each proposed edit changes exactly one declared input from the same baseline and the caller supplies its expected direction and an unverified admissibility note.; The UTF-8 JSON request is at most 64 KiB and the embedded FunctionSpec is at most 16 KiB.
- Do not use when: The question concerns whether a counterfactual is possible or admissible in the actual world, or whether the model itself represents reality.; The input or expression is adversarial and requires operating-system isolation or a security boundary.; A task requires multiple simultaneous input edits, external data, network access, persistence, registry writes, or scientific adjudication.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object conforming to toolbox-counterfactual-probe-input-r0: one bounded pure FunctionSpec, exact baseline inputs, declared assumption bindings, and 1 to 8 single-input probes. → One baseline plus each independent counterfactual execution status, output, N7 internal consistency result, artifact integrity result, and input/trace digests., Per-probe assumption binding, caller-declared expected direction, observed model direction, model-conditional stability, failure/disconfirmation status, and caller-declared admissibility note., Explicit execution count, source evidence unchanged, no external actions, and a model-only claim ceiling.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_REPOSITORY_SOURCE_AND_STDIN` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_counterfactual_probe.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_function_os_evaluator.py, ignition/function-os-candidate/v0.2/function_os/, ignition/function-os-candidate/v0.2/v0.2-scope-contract.json, ignition/function-os-candidate/v0.2/pyproject.toml` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Writes one bounded JSON result to stdout; model outputs and traces remain in process memory., Does not invoke N9 registry writes, alter source evidence, access the network, or take external actions., Disables Python bytecode writes before importing the Function OS evaluator..
- Dependencies: ignition/tools/toolbox_counterfactual_probe.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_function_os_evaluator.py, ignition/function-os-candidate/v0.2/function_os/, ignition/function-os-candidate/v0.2/v0.2-scope-contract.json, ignition/function-os-candidate/v0.2/pyproject.toml; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.10 standard library; no third-party runtime packages., Checked-in Function OS v0.2 Python source tree through the existing bounded N1-N7 evaluator..
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · One local baseline plus one evaluation per probe; no portable latency, memory, or cost estimate is established. The paired C1 timings are a single local microbenchmark.
- Alternatives: `function_os.run_bounded_symbolic_spec` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Runs one FunctionSpec/input pair. It can supply each individual evaluation, but does not accept an assumption-bound probe set or emit the paired baseline, independent-probe, stability, and failure-preservation report..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Run one baseline and multiple independent assumption-bound one-input probes in one request, preserving each result, expected-direction mismatch, and model-only claim ceiling..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · Same real task compared one wrapper invocation against repeated calls to the existing single-case Function OS runner. ### `research.resolve_europe_pmc_fulltext_locations` · Europe PMC 全文位置定位器 / Europe PMC Full-text Location Locator

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.2.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1; Europe PMC Web Services terms and applicable provider/data licensing terms apply. Provider-reported location and license metadata are not rights determinations. API documentation: https://europepmc.org/RestfulWebService.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `3e3e5f2c04721e8a7a3277404f4bb077f70d2efe`.
- Can do: Resolve up to five exact DOI, PMID, or PMCID values against Europe PMC core-search results and return provider-reported fullTextUrlList metadata without requesting article URLs or assessing access rights.
- Good for: A task already has one to five public DOI, PMID, or PMCID values and needs the provider-reported Europe PMC fullTextUrlList projection for exact identifier records.; Returning bounded source identifiers, URL metadata, response hashes, retrieval timestamps, and explicit no-match or ambiguous-match states without article content.; Checking whether this provider reports candidate locations before a separately authorized retrieval or rights review performed by another workflow.
- Not for: Topic-to-literature discovery when no DOI is known; use a suitable bounded topic search instead.; Downloading, parsing, summarizing, quoting, or checking the returned article URLs or article text.; Verifying that a location works, that open access is legally reusable, that a license is valid, or that a source is authentic.; Establishing article quality, retraction status, clinical advice, or scientific support for any claim.
- Use only if: The caller supplies one to five unique bare DOI, PMID, or PMCID values and exact Europe PMC location metadata is needed.; The candidate is explicitly selected for this task and the caller separately permits sending those public identifiers to www.ebi.ac.uk.; The actual CLI invocation includes --allow-public-network; routing remains a proposal and does not authorize network access.; The identifiers contain no confidential, patient-identifying, or otherwise sensitive information that should not be sent to a public service.
- Do not use when: No persistent identifier is known, the task needs general topic discovery, or a checked-out local source already satisfies the request.; Public network access or disclosure of the identifier to Europe PMC is not allowed.; The task requires the article itself, a verified working URL, a license determination, a rights decision, or scientific appraisal.; The caller requests unrestricted or exhaustive location discovery beyond the provider response bounded to five records per identifier.
- Inputs → outputs: Legacy UTF-8 JSON stdin object with exactly one key dois and an array of 1-5 unique bare DOI strings (each at most 128 characters), or typed UTF-8 JSON stdin object with exactly one key identifiers and an array of 1-5 unique {type,value} objects where type is DOI, PMID, or PMCID; request maximum 4096 bytes. → Input-ordered per-identifier acquisition and exact-match state, provider hit count, and at most five exact-identifier-matched provider records., Each row preserves requested_identifier type/value and keeps requested_doi populated only for legacy/typed DOI requests; records include provider source/id/PMID/PMCID/DOI, isOpenAccess claim, safe HTTPS fullTextUrlList fields, and omitted-unsafe count., Provider endpoint, UTC retrieval timestamp, query and response SHA-256 values, source-code SHA-256, top-level status, claim ceiling, and valid/acquired response counts; no title, abstract, raw response, or article content.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_europe_pmc_fulltext_locator.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `www.ebi.ac.uk` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: One explicit invocation sends one HTTPS GET per identifier, at most five requests total, to the fixed www.ebi.ac.uk Europe PMC Search endpoint: DOI:<value>, EXT_ID:<value> for PMID, or PMCID:<value>, with format=json, resultType=core, and pageSize=5. Caller-supplied identifiers may appear in provider-side access logs., Returned fullTextUrlList links are output metadata only: the adapter never requests them, follows redirects, downloads content, or checks live availability or license., Uses no retries, secrets, API key, billing account, file write, repository mutation, or external action; only a bounded JSON receipt is written to stdout., Each API request has a five-second socket timeout, each response body has a seven-second time budget and a 512 KiB cap, and no response contains more than five provider records..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No service availability or stable latency/cost comparison is established; one explicit invocation performs at most five bounded fixed-host API requests.
- Alternatives: `research.search_europe_pmc_query` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The existing lite topic/query adapter remains useful for discovering records from public text; same PMID evidence shows it can return the record identity but omits fullTextUrlList, while core results expose provider-reported locations.; `research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): OpenAlex returns bounded Work-level locations and an open_access summary for DOI inputs, with OpenAlex source/version/license fields; it does not provide Europe PMC's provider-specific fullTextUrlList and availability codes. One same-DOI comparison supports task-specific coexistence, not broad coverage, quality, cost, or replacement conclusions.; `research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The resolver remains useful when PMID/PMCID/DOI cross-reference mapping is itself required. For an already-known PMID or PMCID and a Europe PMC location task, the direct typed locator can skip that extra lookup stage; this does not establish the resolver is replaceable..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: For already-known public DOI, PMID, or PMCID values, retrieve exact Europe PMC provider-reported fullTextUrlList metadata while leaving returned article URLs untouched and preserving rights and availability uncertainty..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · One DOI same-provider core-versus-lite comparison showed that lite omitted fullTextUrlList while core returned three locations. A second live same-PMID comparison returned the same MED identity from both adapters; only core supplied fullTextUrlList. A separate same-DOI cross-provider comparison paired Europe PMC v0.2 and OpenAlex v0.2 and observed distinct provider-specific location schemas. The typed-identifier location use remains distinct; none of these samples establishes broad superiority, stable latency/cost, rights, provider completeness, or scientific quality. Comparators: research.search_europe_pmc_query, research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers, research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-europe-pmc-fulltext-locator-evidence-r0.json, ignition/tools/toolbox_europe_pmc_query_search.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_europe_pmc_query_search.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_openalex_work_metadata.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_openalex_work_metadata.py, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-openalex-locations-c1-evidence-r0.json; findings: For the same DOI, lite and core returned MED record id 19404397; the lite projection omitted fullTextUrlList, while core supplied three provider-reported locations. The provider response said isOpenAccess=Y but all location license fields were null; no permission inference follows.; For the same PMID 19404397, the existing lite query returned one exact MED record identity without fullTextUrlList; the typed core locator returned the same identity and three provider-reported locations using one request in each arm. A separate typed PMCID request for PMC2671458 returned that same exact record and three locations.; The topic-search adapter retains its discovery use case. The PubMed identifier resolver remains useful for cross-reference mapping, but a known PMID/PMCID location task can skip that extra step. No existing tool is removed, made Current, or enabled for default routing.; For the same DOI, OpenAlex v0.2 returned eight Work-level location records and an OpenAlex open_access summary; Europe PMC core returned three fullTextUrlList entries with Europe PMC availability codes. The different provider schemas and counts do not measure comparative completeness. Neither response validates returned URLs, licenses, or article content..

- Fallback: If Europe PMC is unavailable, returns no exact match, or lists no usable URL, preserve that status without inferring absence. Use another explicitly authorized provider or manual lookup only under the caller's separate network and rights instructions; do not fetch returned locations through this tool.

### `research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers` · PubMed/PMC DOI 标识符解析器 / PubMed/PMC DOI identifier resolver

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.1` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1; NCBI PMC ID Converter service terms apply, and no license for underlying record metadata is asserted. API documentation: https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/tools/idconv/.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `566c6d84149b11b0bbb7a9417094a146b8601f80`.
- Can do: Resolve caller-supplied bare DOIs to matching PMID and PMCID identifiers through the fixed PMC ID Converter API, normalizing NCBI's numeric JSON PMID representation to a bounded decimal string.
- Good for: Mapping caller-supplied DOI identifiers to PubMed PMID and PMC PMCID values when those identifiers are returned by NCBI's ID Converter.; Retaining per-DOI source response hashes, HTTP outcomes, and explicit no-match results.; Adding an NCBI/PMC identifier lookup alongside a separately requested Crossref DOI metadata comparison.; Normalizing a positive bounded integer PMID returned by NCBI while preserving the legacy decimal-string form.
- Not for: Retrieving article full text, abstracts, publisher pages, or arbitrary URLs.; Establishing that a paper is authentic, scientifically correct, retracted, or supports a claim.; Treating a missing PMID or PMCID as proof that a DOI or article does not exist.; Adding or correcting repository records or registering scientific sources.
- Use only if: The caller provides one to ten unique bare DOI identifiers and explicitly permits sending those DOI identifiers to the public API for this invocation.; The caller invokes the CLI with --allow-public-network.; The exact fixed host pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov is allowed by the actual invocation environment; route context alone is not authority.; The PMC ID Converter endpoint is reachable and returns a supported JSON response.
- Do not use when: A DOI or its presence in task context is confidential or must not be sent to a public service.; Network access is unavailable, disallowed, or not explicitly approved for this invocation.; The task requires article-content review, source-authenticity review, retraction adjudication, or scientific assessment.
- Inputs → outputs: JSON stdin object with one to ten unique bare DOI identifiers → input-ordered PubMed/PMC identifier resolution rows, per-response SHA-256 and explicit HTTP or network outcome summary
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: When explicitly invoked with --allow-public-network, sends at most ten HTTPS GET requests to the fixed pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/tools/idconv/api/v1/articles/ endpoint; only the DOI identifier and response-format parameter are sent., Uses no redirects, secrets, API key, billing, or arbitrary URL. Returns a compact JSON receipt on stdout and validation errors on stderr; does not create or modify repository files., Applies a five-second socket timeout, seven-second response-body budget checked between and after bounded 8 KiB reads, a 128 KiB response cap, and a serial 0.34-second inter-request delay; no retries are performed..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No comparable latency, bandwidth, service-availability, or compute-cost profile has been measured; the wrapper has a maximum of ten serial fixed-host lookups and bounded response handling. R1 recorded one process wall/child-CPU observation (622.937/71.600 ms) for one public request; billed cost, bandwidth and latency distribution remain UNKNOWN.
- Alternatives: `research.verify_crossref_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Crossref compares caller-supplied DOI title/year expectations with Crossref metadata; this tool maps DOI identifiers to NCBI PMID/PMCID values and does not verify title or year..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Resolve one to ten DOI identifiers to NCBI-returned PMID/PMCID values without requesting PubMed update relations.; Provide the first DOI-to-PMID/PMCID stage for the optional single-DOI update-audit composition..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · One live DOI pair exposed the NCBI numeric-PMID response form and demonstrated its use as the identifier stage in a raw-update-link workflow. The candidate remains separately useful for DOI-only identifier lookup. Comparators: research.verify_crossref_metadata, research.audit_pubmed_updates_from_doi; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_doi_update_audit.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-c1-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-real-task-r0.json, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py; findings: A live PMC ID Converter response encoded PMID as an integer; v0.1.0 dropped it. v0.1.1 normalizes bounded positive integers while retaining string inputs and rejecting booleans, zero, negatives, and over-limit values.; The resolver still covers DOI-to-PMID/PMCID lookup without a PubMed relation request; the composition proceeds to the second stage only when an exact PMID is returned.; One DOI comparison does not establish provider completeness, broad reliability, cost, or retirement of either adapter..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · Optional/default-off DOI identifier lookup remains useful independently; the same-task composition extends this capability but does not cover DOI-only lookup tasks.
- Migration/rollback: No automatic migration is inferred; preserve this exact input/output contract until a same-task comparison supports specialization or replacement.; Version 0.1.1 adds support for the observed positive integer PMID JSON form and preserves decimal-string inputs; downstream output remains a normalized string and the resolver remains optional/default-off.; Do not migrate DOI-only identifier lookup callers to the composition; they would incur an unnecessary second public request when a PMID is present. / Remove the candidate entry and wrapper from the isolated toolbox branch, regenerate the identity and human-directory projections, and preserve prior commits and evidence.; If integer normalization causes an incompatible consumer result, revert the bounded numeric branch in _normalized_identifier, restore the prior wrapper version and candidate card, rerun focused tests, and preserve the provider-response evidence.; If version 0.1.1 numeric normalization is incompatible, restore resolver 0.1.0 and its card while retaining the exact provider-shape evidence..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py --stdin --allow-public-network`
- Fallback: If live network use is not approved or fails, preserve the explicit NOT_RUN or acquisition failure and use the official PubMed/PMC interface manually only when the caller separately authorizes that lookup; never infer an absent record from an unavailable response.
- Evidence: `REAL_TASK_OUTCOME` · source refs `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py` (BOUNDED_FIXED_HOST_CLI), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py` (FOCUSED_MOCKED_NETWORK_AND_NEGATIVE_CONTROLS), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-real-task-route-evidence-r0.json` (REAL_TASK_ROUTE_AND_OUTCOME_EVIDENCE), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-real-task-route-evidence-r1.json` (R1_REPEAT_REAL_ROUTE_AND_MEASURED_SINGLE_RUN_OUTCOME), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-real-task-route-evidence-r2.json` (R2_EXACT_CURRENT_CANDIDATE_ROUTE_AND_MEASURED_LIVE_OUTCOME), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-c1-evidence-r0.json` (C1_MATCHED_COMPOSITION_COMPARISON_AND_INTEGER_ID_FINDING), `ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-doi-update-real-task-r0.json` (PRESERVED_PRE_FIX_NO_EXACT_PMID_OUTCOME), `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_doi_update_audit.py` (C1_COMPOSED_ROUTE_IMPLEMENTATION_COMPARATOR), `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py` (C1_EXACT_PMID_STAGE_IMPLEMENTATION_COMPARATOR); focused checks [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py](tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py); negative controls: Missing --allow-public-network makes no request and emits no result.; Reject URLs, malformed or duplicate DOI identifiers, duplicate JSON keys, and batches above ten before network access.; Only pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov is contacted; redirects, non-JSON, oversized responses, HTTP errors, timeout, malformed JSON, mismatched records, and invalid identifiers remain explicit outcomes.; A no-match response yields no invented PMID/PMCID; no repository files are written..
- Route default: `false` · conditions: Remain excluded unless the candidate ID is explicitly allowlisted and the typed route profile explicitly lists pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov in allowed_network_hosts.; A route proposal has execution_authorized=false and does not validate the caller authority behind that host list.; Every actual CLI invocation separately requires --allow-public-network and a task-specific decision to transmit the supplied DOI identifiers..
- Claim ceiling: DOI-to-PMID/PMCID identifier lookup from the live PMC ID Converter response only; response hashes bind received bytes, not source authenticity, article content, bibliographic accuracy, scientific truth, retraction status, or complete PubMed/PMC coverage.

### `research.search_arxiv_query` · arXiv 主题检索 / arXiv topic search

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1; arXiv descriptive metadata may be used under CC0 1.0 as stated by the provider. arXiv API terms and article-content copyright boundaries apply; this wrapper asserts no license over returned data.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner, source-authentication, or scientific authority.` · source blob `faf93d4195dac204630e4e2625b5807f7bbd17d4`.
- Can do: Search one caller-supplied public query in the arXiv corpus and return at most ten provider-reported metadata rows with source IDs and response evidence.
- Good for: Discovering up to ten arXiv metadata records for one public research topic when no arXiv ID is known and the checked-out source corpus does not already cover the task.; Returning arXiv identifiers and selected provider metadata for caller-directed follow-up in the arXiv corpus.; Retaining the query hash, response hash, retrieval time, hit count, result count, and bounded claim ceiling without echoing the query.
- Not for: Exhaustive or systematic-review search, guaranteed recall, or treating provider order as a scientific quality ranking.; Searching PubMed, Europe PMC, or arbitrary web sources; retrieving abstracts, paper files, or full text; or replacing known-ID lookup.; Assessing relevance, source authenticity, publication or correction status, claim support, scientific quality, or discovery.
- Use only if: The caller supplies one public, non-sensitive query of at most 512 characters and a result limit from one through ten.; Local source navigation and an existing source-cluster brief do not already satisfy the task.; The caller explicitly selects this experimental candidate, approves query transmission to arXiv, and invokes --allow-public-network.; The exact export.arxiv.org host is separately allowed by the typed route profile.; All arXiv API use is serialized with one connection at a time and at least three seconds between requests across the caller's controlled machines.
- Do not use when: The query or task context is confidential, patient-identifying, personal-health, or otherwise unsuitable for public-provider transmission.; Network access is not allowed, bulk harvesting or concurrent API requests are needed, or an exhaustive reproducible search is required.; A known arXiv ID, DOI, or PMID is already supplied and a source-specific exact-identifier operation is required.; The caller needs abstract/full-text review, scientific support, or evaluation of source truth.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object with exactly a public query string (1-512 characters) and integer max_results (1-10) → At most ten arXiv records with ID, entry URL, title, first ten author names/count, dates, categories, journal reference, and DOI when returned; abstracts and full text are omitted., Provider hit count, retrieval timestamp, source/response byte count, query/request/response SHA-256 values, explicit acquisition status, and bounded claim ceiling.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_arxiv_query_search.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `export.arxiv.org` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: One explicit invocation sends one HTTPS GET containing the public query to the fixed export.arxiv.org API; provider-side query logging is outside this wrapper's control., The adapter makes no retries or redirects, sends no credentials, and writes no files; it emits only bounded selected metadata and a JSON receipt., Callers must serialize arXiv API access and wait at least three seconds between requests across all machines they control; this CLI has no shared cross-process or cross-machine rate limiter..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No comparable latency, reliability, bandwidth, human review, or service-cost profile has been measured; the adapter makes at most one request and caps output.
- Alternatives: `research.lookup_arxiv_paper_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The existing arXiv adapter resolves one already-known modern arXiv ID. It cannot discover record IDs from a topic query.; `research.search_pubmed_query` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): PubMed search uses its own collection and ESearch/ESummary metadata contract. It may coexist when PubMed-indexed records are required; this candidate does not replace that source.; `research.search_europe_pmc_query` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Europe PMC search uses its own MED/PMC collection and source identifiers. The arXiv corpus and returned identifier contract differ.; `knowledge.navigate_raw_evidence` (LOCAL_FIRST_COMPLEMENT): The local navigator searches only the checked-out Knowledge corpus. Prefer it when the needed sources are already indexed; this candidate searches the external arXiv corpus.; `knowledge.cluster_source_brief` (LOCAL_FIRST_COMPLEMENT): The cluster brief reads a declared local article cluster and does not perform arbitrary external topic-to-record discovery.; `research.search_openalex_query` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): OpenAlex searches a distinct Works corpus and returns OpenAlex IDs. The same-task snapshot does not establish superior relevance, recall, or replacement coverage..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Discover a bounded set of arXiv records from a public topic query when no arXiv ID is known and local checked-out sources do not cover the topic..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · One same-task public snapshot compared five metadata rows from arXiv, Europe PMC, and OpenAlex within a session window. Provider queries, corpora, ranking fields, IDs, and timestamps differ; comparative relevance, recall, quality, latency, and cost remain unknown. Comparators: research.search_europe_pmc_query, research.lookup_arxiv_paper_metadata, research.search_openalex_query; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_europe_pmc_query_search.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_openalex_query_search.py; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-arxiv-query-search-c1-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-arxiv-query-search-real-task-evidence-r1.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-arxiv-query-search-source-binding-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-arxiv-query-search-c1-evidence-r1.json; findings: Both same-task providers returned five metadata rows for the public topic within seconds of one another; their provider hit counts, result orders, and query syntax differ, so this does not establish relative relevance or recall.; arXiv returns arXiv entry IDs while Europe PMC returns MED/PMC identifiers and provider publication fields. They address distinct source collections and can coexist when the task requires those scopes.; The known-ID arXiv lookup requires an ID and cannot replace topic discovery. PubMed remains a source-specific alternative whose same-task result was not measured.; No retirement or replacement is supported. Keep this experimental search default-off and route only for arXiv topic discovery.; The OpenAlex same-task observation adds a third provider-specific scope; no comparative ranking or retirement follows from five-row snapshots..

- Migration/rollback: No Current operation, existing query adapter, or exact-ID adapter is migrated. Use this candidate only for arXiv topic discovery; use the known-ID adapter after an arXiv identifier is available. / Remove the optional candidate, runtime code, focused tests, and unneeded projections from the isolated branch; regenerate the identity cards and human toolbox directory. Preserve source, task, comparison, and route evidence without rewriting it..

- Fallback: If the caller does not approve a public query or the API fails, preserve the explicit outcome and use local source navigation when it covers the task. For a known arXiv ID, select the exact-ID adapter. A manual arXiv web lookup requires a separate caller decision.

### `research.search_crossref_query` · Crossref 主题检索 / Crossref topic search

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1. Crossref states that almost none of its metadata is subject to copyright, while some abstracts may be; this adapter excludes abstracts and claims no license over returned metadata.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner, source-authentication, or scientific authority.` · source blob `f820f71d4ad8f14f36ac0f2845d38d17eba07aa8`.
- Can do: Search Crossref Works v1 for one caller-supplied public topic query and return a bounded DOI-oriented metadata projection.
- Good for: Discovering at most ten Crossref Works records for one public topic query when no DOI is known and the caller needs Crossref DOI or deposited bibliographic metadata.; Returning selected provider metadata including DOI, title, issued year when present, publisher, container title, author names, and provider-reported cited-by count.; Recording provider result count, retrieval time, response digest, request/query digests, HTTP outcome, and available rate-limit headers without returning raw query or response data.
- Not for: Systematic-review search, exhaustive recall, multi-page harvesting, bulk export, or automated high-frequency queries.; Treating provider order, cited-by counts, hit counts, DOI records, or metadata fields as human relevance, evidence strength, citation correctness, or scientific quality.; Retrieving abstracts, references, full text, publisher pages, arbitrary URLs, correction/retraction status, or legal reuse rights.
- Use only if: The caller supplies one public, non-sensitive query of at most 512 characters and 2048 UTF-8 bytes plus max_results from one through ten.; Checked-out local source navigation and an existing source-cluster brief do not already satisfy the requested source scope.; The caller explicitly selects this candidate and separately approves sending the query to Crossref for this invocation.; The exact api.crossref.org host is allowed by the typed route profile and the CLI receives --allow-public-network.; Public-pool list-query frequency is coordinated at no more than one request per second with one concurrent request across the caller's processes; stop on HTTP 429.
- Do not use when: The query or task context is confidential, patient-identifying, personal-health, or otherwise unsuitable for a public metadata provider.; A known DOI is supplied and the existing Crossref metadata verifier satisfies the task, or an existing local source already provides the requested records.; The task requires exhaustive coverage, abstract/full-text assessment, source authentication, authoritative correction/retraction adjudication, or scientific review.; Network use is not authorized, the API is unavailable, or the request would exceed the public-pool rate/concurrency boundary.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object with exactly a public query string (1-512 characters, at most 2048 UTF-8 bytes) and integer max_results (1-10) → At most ten Crossref Works metadata rows with DOI when present, title, issued publication year when present, type, publisher, container title, up to ten author names with count/truncation, and provider-reported cited-by count; abstracts, references, links, and URLs are omitted., Provider hit count, retrieval timestamp, response bytes, query/request/response/source-code SHA-256 values, HTTP/acquisition outcome, selected public-pool rate headers, and bounded claim ceiling.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_query_search.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `api.crossref.org` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: One explicit invocation sends one HTTPS GET to api.crossref.org/v1/works containing the caller's public query, result limit, and selected metadata fields; provider-side query logging is outside this wrapper., The wrapper sends no mailto, account email, API key, Authorization header, or caller credential; no payment account or billed charge was used or established., The request is fixed-host, v1-pinned, no-redirect, no-retry, with a five-second socket timeout, seven-second response budget, 512 KiB response cap, and one-page maximum of ten rows., The response body is parsed and hashed in memory; the receipt excludes the raw query, abstract, references, links, and raw response. No repository files are written..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library; no third-party runtime dependency..
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · Crossref public REST use requires no signup, and this adapter made one public-pool request; no stable latency, bandwidth, service-availability, human-review, or billed-cost profile has been measured.
- Alternatives: `research.verify_crossref_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The existing Crossref tool verifies caller-supplied DOI records and projects their references; it requires known DOI identifiers and does not discover records from a text query.; `research.search_openalex_query` (ALTERNATE_PROVIDER_DIFFERENT_PROVENANCE): OpenAlex searches its Works index and returns OpenAlex Work IDs and provider metadata. One same-query top-five snapshot had no DOI intersection, but it does not establish comparative relevance, recall, or superiority.; `research.search_arxiv_query` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): arXiv has its own preprint corpus, query syntax, identifiers, and metadata contract; use it when arXiv-specific source coverage is needed.; `research.search_europe_pmc_query` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Europe PMC covers its biomedical corpus and returns MED/PMC source identifiers; provider scope and output identifiers differ.; `research.search_pubmed_query` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): PubMed uses NCBI database syntax and requires caller-provided contact email; it was not re-run for this Crossref comparison.; `knowledge.cluster_source_brief` (LOCAL_FIRST_COMPLEMENT): Prefer the declared local article-cluster brief when it already covers the task; it does not perform arbitrary Crossref discovery.; `knowledge.navigate_raw_evidence` (LOCAL_FIRST_COMPLEMENT): Prefer local Knowledge navigation when the needed sources are indexed; it does not query Crossref..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Provide a default-off Crossref Works query route when a caller explicitly needs Crossref DOI records and deposited publisher/container metadata and no exact DOI is known; exclusive superiority over other providers remains unestablished..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · One same-exact-query, same-limit comparison of the top five Crossref and OpenAlex results at different timestamps. The DOI sets did not overlap; Crossref returned three same-title rows with distinct DOIs and one row without a reported year. No human relevance review, source validation, recall assessment, or outcome comparison was performed. Comparators: research.search_openalex_query; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_openalex_query_search.py; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-query-search-c1-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-query-search-real-task-evidence-r1.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-openalex-query-search-real-task-evidence-r1.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-query-search-source-binding-r0.json; findings: Crossref and OpenAlex completed the same public query and returned five records each; their top-five DOI sets shared zero exact DOIs in this one snapshot.; Crossref's top-five provider response includes repeated title text under distinct DOIs and a missing year. This observation does not adjudicate duplicate records or relevance.; Crossref source-specific DOI discovery and Crossref's existing known-DOI verification have different inputs and outputs; no exact-DOI incumbent is replaced.; No comparison establishes relevance, recall, completeness, metadata correctness, quality, latency, reliability, cost, or scientific value superiority. Existing source-specific query tools remain candidates for tasks requiring their own collections..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · The candidate exposes a default-off Crossref topic-to-DOI route. Its small same-query comparison shows provider-specific results but no superiority or full coverage; existing Crossref exact-DOI and other provider tools remain available for their distinct scopes.
- Migration/rollback: No Current operation or known-DOI verifier is migrated. Select this candidate only for explicit Crossref topic discovery; select an exact-identifier adapter when the DOI is known. / Remove the optional candidate, wrapper, focused tests, schemas, documentation, and unneeded route projections from the isolated branch; regenerate identity projections. Preserve initial failure, final-source, C1, and route evidence without rewriting it..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_query_search.py --stdin --allow-public-network`
- Fallback: Prefer local source navigation or the existing cluster brief when they cover the task. For a known DOI, use the corresponding exact-identifier adapter. If the query cannot be sent or Crossref fails, preserve NOT_RUN or the explicit acquisition outcome; do not silently substitute another provider.

### `research.search_europe_pmc_query` · Europe PMC 主题检索 / Europe PMC topic search

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1; Europe PMC Web Services terms and applicable provider/data licensing terms apply, and this wrapper asserts no license over returned metadata. API documentation: https://europepmc.org/RestfulWebService.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `bc2822fa0ac1d9519ce1c6cc6393d211d2c741b0`.
- Can do: Search one caller-supplied public query against Europe PMC and return at most ten provider-ranked lite metadata rows with source identifiers and response evidence.
- Good for: Discovering a small set of literature metadata records from a caller-supplied public scientific topic when no DOI or PMID is known.; Returning the provider-reported source and identifiers alongside selected title, author, journal and year fields for caller-directed follow-up.; Retaining the exact query hash, response hash, retrieval time, provider hit count and bounded returned count without echoing the query.
- Not for: Exhaustive systematic-review searches, guaranteed complete recall, or treating the provider result order as a scientific quality ranking.; Retrieving or evaluating abstracts, full text, clinical advice, source authenticity, retraction status, article quality or scientific support for a claim.; Replacing exact-identifier tasks: known DOI-to-PMID/PMCID resolution, known PMID Comments/Corrections inspection, Crossref/OpenAlex DOI metadata lookup, or arXiv-ID metadata lookup.
- Use only if: The caller supplies one public, non-confidential query of at most 512 characters and a result limit from one through ten.; The caller separately chooses this experimental candidate and explicitly invokes the CLI with --allow-public-network.; The caller's actual invocation context explicitly permits sending this query to www.ebi.ac.uk; a route profile's host list is not authority.; Checked-out local source navigation or an existing article-cluster brief does not already satisfy the task; the caller specifically needs external topic-to-record discovery.; A bounded metadata discovery result is sufficient for the task and provider-side query logging is acceptable.
- Do not use when: The query contains confidential, patient-identifying, personal-health or otherwise sensitive material that should not be sent to a public service.; Network use is not permitted, a public query is not appropriate, or an exhaustive independently reproducible search is required.; The caller already has exact identifiers and only needs DOI-to-PMID/PMCID mapping, PubMed update-link inspection, Crossref/OpenAlex metadata lookup, or arXiv-ID metadata lookup.; The checked-out raw-evidence navigator or declared article-cluster source brief already supplies the evidence needed for the task.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object with exactly a public query string (1-512 characters) and integer max_results (1-10) → at most ten Europe PMC provider-ranked lite publication metadata rows with source, provider ID, DOI/PMID/PMCID when present, title, author string, journal and publication year, query SHA-256, response SHA-256, retrieval timestamp, provider hit count, returned count, HTTP and acquisition status, and bounded claim ceiling
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_europe_pmc_query_search.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `www.ebi.ac.uk` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: One explicit invocation sends one HTTPS GET to the fixed www.ebi.ac.uk Europe PMC search endpoint, including the caller's URL-encoded query, format=json, resultType=lite and pageSize; provider-side access logging is outside this wrapper's control., Uses no redirects, retries, API key, secret, billing account, file write or repository mutation; returns selected metadata and a bounded JSON receipt on stdout., Applies a five-second socket timeout, seven-second response-body budget, 512 KiB response cap, one-request maximum and ten-record maximum..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No service availability or latency commitment and no comparable cost profile have been established; this wrapper performs at most one fixed-host request and bounds response handling.
- Alternatives: `research.verify_crossref_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Crossref compares caller-supplied exact DOI title/year expectations to one provider record; it cannot discover candidate records from a topic query.; `research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): OpenAlex requires caller-supplied DOI identifiers and returns a selected Work projection; this search discovers candidate records from text and does not replace exact DOI lookup.; `research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The identifier resolver requires caller-supplied DOI values and maps them to returned PMID/PMCID values; this search discovers candidate records from a text query and does not resolve or verify a caller-supplied DOI.; `research.inspect_pubmed_update_links` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The PubMed update-link reader requires exact known PMIDs and reports raw Comments/Corrections relations; this search discovers candidate metadata and does not adjudicate update status.; `research.lookup_arxiv_paper_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The arXiv adapter requires one exact modern arXiv ID and returns that provider record; it does not perform topic-to-record discovery.; `research.compare_publication_metadata_snapshots` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The local comparator consumes already-collected Crossref and OpenAlex results for one DOI and performs no retrieval; it cannot discover records from a query.; `knowledge.cluster_source_brief` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The local source brief requires an already-declared article-cluster ID and reads the checked-out asset-card corpus; it cannot discover external records from a public text query.; `knowledge.navigate_raw_evidence` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The local navigator searches the checked-out Knowledge index and returns bounded current raw source lines; it does not query Europe PMC or return provider publication metadata for arbitrary external topics.; `research.search_openalex_query` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): OpenAlex searches a distinct Works corpus and returns OpenAlex IDs. The same-task snapshot does not establish superior relevance, recall, or replacement coverage..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Find a bounded set of Europe PMC records from a public topic query when the checked-out source corpus is insufficient and no DOI, PMID or arXiv identifier is known, while preserving the returned source identifier for follow-up..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · One same-task public snapshot compared five metadata rows from arXiv, Europe PMC, and OpenAlex within a session window. Provider queries, corpora, ranking fields, IDs, and timestamps differ; comparative relevance, recall, quality, latency, and cost remain unknown. Comparators: research.verify_crossref_metadata, research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata, research.resolve_pubmed_identifiers, research.inspect_pubmed_update_links, research.lookup_arxiv_paper_metadata, research.compare_publication_metadata_snapshots, knowledge.cluster_source_brief, knowledge.navigate_raw_evidence, research.search_openalex_query; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_openalex_query_search.py; refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_metadata_verifier.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_crossref_metadata_verifier.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_openalex_work_metadata.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_openalex_work_metadata.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_arxiv_metadata_lookup.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_compare_publication_metadata.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_publication_metadata_comparison.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_identifier_resolver.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_pubmed_update_link_reader.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-europe-pmc-query-search-evidence-r0.json, ignition/tools/toolbox_cluster_source_brief.py, ignition/tests/test_cluster_source_brief_tool.py, ignition/tools/toolbox_raw_evidence_navigator.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_raw_evidence_navigator.py, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-arxiv-query-search-c1-evidence-r1.json; findings: The new tool accepts a public text query and returns bounded provider metadata. Crossref and OpenAlex adapters require known DOIs, the PubMed resolver requires a known DOI, the update reader requires a known PMID, and the arXiv adapter requires a known arXiv ID; each returns a different exact-identifier or record-level output.; The local Crossref/OpenAlex comparator consumes two prior adapter results for one DOI and performs no source retrieval. None of these declared contracts covers query-to-record discovery, and the search does not cover their known-identifier or pairwise-comparison outputs.; The route experiment selects only the query-search candidate for its declared query-to-metadata task and rejects the DOI resolver as an input-type mismatch, even when both candidates are explicitly offered.; No paired evidence establishes superior relevance, recall, latency, cost or scientific quality; those comparisons remain unknown and do not support retiring any existing tool.; The local cluster brief needs a declared cluster ID, while the raw-evidence navigator searches only the checked-out Knowledge index and returns source lines. These local tools remain preferred when their bounded corpus already satisfies the task; neither discovers external topic records.; The OpenAlex same-task observation adds a third provider-specific scope; no comparative ranking or retirement follows from five-row snapshots..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · Retain as a specialized experimental topic-to-record discovery operation; its query input and bounded metadata output do not cover known-identifier retrieval, local source navigation, or local paired-result comparison contracts.
- Migration/rollback: No existing tool or Current operation is migrated. When the caller has a known DOI, PMID or arXiv ID and needs its source-specific output, retain the corresponding exact-identifier adapter; when comparing already-collected Crossref/OpenAlex results, retain the local comparator. Use this candidate only for public-query discovery. / If later evidence supports retirement, remove this candidate from the available set and delete unneeded runtime code, then regenerate identity projections; preserve this historical query, response and route evidence without rewriting it..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_europe_pmc_query_search.py --stdin --allow-public-network`
- Fallback: If the API is unavailable or the query is unsuitable for public transmission, preserve the explicit failure or do not run it. Use Europe PMC's official interface manually only when the caller separately authorizes that lookup; for already-known DOI or PMID values use the corresponding specialized optional adapter when explicitly selected.

### `research.search_openalex_query` · OpenAlex 主题检索 / OpenAlex topic search

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1. OpenAlex states its metadata is CC0: https://help.openalex.org/data/how-its-built/. Public search requests consume provider usage credits; this wrapper claims no license over returned metadata.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner, source-authentication, or scientific authority.` · source blob `b042e84bee1ee37fcf42bea22f3617679ce71e86`.
- Can do: Expose the existing OpenAlex Works search through one bounded, default-off, metadata-only toolbox request for a caller-supplied public topic query.
- Good for: Discovering at most ten OpenAlex Works for one public topic query when no DOI, PMID, PMCID, or OpenAlex Work ID is known and local sources do not satisfy the task.; Returning bounded OpenAlex Work IDs and provider metadata for caller-directed follow-up without returning abstracts or full text.; Recording provider hit count, retrieval time, response digest, query/request digests, and rate-limit usage headers when supplied.
- Not for: Systematic-review search, exhaustive recall, full-dataset export, paging, semantic search, or provider reranking.; Treating provider relevance scores, result order, citation contribution, or hit counts as human relevance, evidence strength, or scientific quality.; Retrieving or reviewing abstracts/full text, authenticating sources, checking correction or retraction status, establishing legal reuse rights, or supporting scientific claims.
- Use only if: The caller supplies one public, non-sensitive query of at most 512 characters and 2048 UTF-8 bytes plus max_results from one through ten.; Checked-out local source navigation and an existing source-cluster brief do not already satisfy the requested source scope.; The caller explicitly selects this experimental candidate and approves transmission of the query to OpenAlex for this invocation.; The exact api.openalex.org host is allowed by the typed route profile and the CLI receives --allow-public-network.
- Do not use when: The query or task context is confidential, patient-identifying, personal-health, or otherwise unsuitable for public-provider transmission.; A known DOI/PMID/PMCID/OpenAlex Work ID is supplied and a corresponding exact-identifier adapter satisfies the task.; The task requires exhaustive coverage, abstract/full-text review, authoritative retraction or correction status, URL availability, reuse-rights determination, or scientific adjudication.; Network use is not authorized, the provider is unavailable, or a credentialed account is required.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object with exactly a public query string (1-512 characters, at most 2048 UTF-8 bytes) and integer max_results (1-10) → At most ten OpenAlex Works metadata rows with Work ID, DOI when present, title, publication year, type, up to ten author names with count/truncation, primary venue, and provider relevance score; abstracts and full text are omitted., Provider hit count, retrieval timestamp, source/response bytes, query/request/response SHA-256 values, HTTP/acquisition outcome, usage headers, and bounded claim ceiling.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_openalex_query_search.py, ignition/scripts/external-research/openalex_client.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `api.openalex.org` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: One explicit invocation sends one HTTPS GET to api.openalex.org/works containing the public query and result limit; provider-side query logging and usage-credit accounting are outside this wrapper., The wrapper sends no api_key, Authorization header, mailto/account email, or caller credentials; a public keyless request reported usage credits, while account-level monetary charge remains unknown., The request is fixed-host, no-redirect, no-retry, with a five-second socket timeout, seven-second response budget, 512 KiB response cap, and one-page maximum of ten rows., The response body is parsed and hashed in memory; the receipt excludes the raw query and response body, abstracts/full text, arbitrary URLs, and writes. No repository file or external action is created..
- Dependencies: ignition/scripts/external-research/openalex_client.py; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library; reuses ignition/scripts/external-research/openalex_client.py without third-party runtime dependencies..
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · The provider meters search usage credits. One final-source keyless request reported ten credits used, but no stable cost, paid-account charge, latency, reliability, or human-review profile is established.
- Alternatives: `research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): That adapter resolves caller-supplied exact DOI identifiers and returns selected Work/location metadata; this adapter discovers Works from a text query.; `research.search_crossref_query` (ALTERNATE_PROVIDER_DIFFERENT_PROVENANCE): Crossref searches its member-deposited DOI metadata with a separate query/index contract. One exact same-query top-five snapshot returned no shared DOI, but does not establish comparative relevance, recall, or superiority.; `research.search_arxiv_query` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): arXiv searches its own corpus and returns arXiv identifiers; the observed source scopes and result identifiers differ, with no quality or recall comparison.; `research.search_europe_pmc_query` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Europe PMC searches its biomedical corpus and returns MED/PMC identifiers and fields; it is a separate source scope.; `research.search_pubmed_query` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): PubMed is a distinct source-specific query route. Its same-task comparison was not run because the adapter requires caller-provided contact_email.; `knowledge.cluster_source_brief` (LOCAL_FIRST_COMPLEMENT): Prefer the declared local article-cluster brief when the needed works are already in the checked-out corpus; it does not perform arbitrary external topic discovery.; `knowledge.navigate_raw_evidence` (LOCAL_FIRST_COMPLEMENT): Prefer local Knowledge navigation when the needed sources are indexed; it does not query OpenAlex..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Provide a bounded, optional, no-placeholder-email OpenAlex Works topic-query route when a caller needs OpenAlex Work IDs from a public query and no exact identifier is known..

### `research.search_pubmed_query` · PubMed 限量检索器 / PubMed Bounded Query Search

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1. NCBI's scripting guidelines require the NCBI Disclaimer and Copyright notice to be evident to users; PubMed records can include third-party material, and this wrapper makes no reuse-right determination for returned metadata. See https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/home/about/policies/ and https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/About/disclaimer.html.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `b8b6c6053ac226baa4dd357228983a11ab0efa62`.
- Can do: Run a caller-specified PubMed ESearch query and retrieve a bounded ESummary metadata projection for the returned PMIDs.
- Good for: Discovering a bounded set of PubMed records when the task explicitly requires PubMed or its Entrez field-tag search syntax.; Returning provider-reported hit count, ordered PMIDs, and selected ESummary metadata for caller review.; Adding a PubMed-specific source alongside Europe PMC when the task needs separate provider observations.
- Not for: Exhaustive or independently reproducible systematic-review searching, recall claims, or scientific ranking.; Retrieving abstracts, full text, clinical advice, source content, or arbitrary URLs.; Deciding that a record is relevant, authentic, current, retracted, scientifically sound, or supportive of a claim.; Replacing known DOI/PMID lookup, PubMed update-link inspection, Europe PMC full-text location, or repository source navigation.
- Use only if: The caller supplies a public, non-sensitive PubMed/Entrez query of at most 512 characters, a result cap of one through ten, and a contact email suitable for NCBI.; The caller explicitly permits sending the query and contact email to eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov.; The caller explicitly selects this experimental candidate, permits the exact network host, and passes --allow-public-network for this invocation.; PubMed-specific source/indexing requirements matter and local checked-out source navigation does not already satisfy the bounded task.
- Do not use when: The query is confidential, patient-identifying, personal-health, or otherwise inappropriate for a public provider.; No contact email or explicit public-network permission is available.; High-volume or concurrent requests are needed and the caller cannot enforce NCBI's per-IP request-rate guidance across invocations.; The task requires exhaustive search, full-text retrieval, source authentication, scientific evaluation, or clinical decision-making.; The task already has an exact DOI or PMID and only needs the narrower corresponding identifier adapter.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin with a public PubMed/Entrez query (including optional PubMed field tags), max_results 1-10, and caller-supplied contact_email → At most ten PubMed ESummary metadata rows in ESearch result order, with PMID, DOI when unique, title, first ten author names, journal, and provider publication date, Provider hit count, ordered PubMed PMID list, per-endpoint HTTP status and response hashes, retrieval timestamp, partial-summary IDs, and bounded claim ceiling
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NCBI_QUERY_AND_SUMMARY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_pubmed_query_search.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Sends the non-sensitive query and caller-supplied contact_email to NCBI; provider-side access logging is outside this wrapper's control., Makes one ESearch HTTPS request and, only when PMIDs are returned, one ESummary HTTPS request; does not follow redirects or retry., Reads bounded stdin and writes one JSON receipt to stdout; writes no files and changes no repository state..
- Dependencies: none declared; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No comparable latency, provider-availability, analyst-time, or monetary cost measurement exists; no paid account or API key is used.
- Alternatives: `research.search_europe_pmc_query` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): Europe PMC performs a separate provider query and can include non-PubMed source IDs. One same-query top-five comparison found different returned IDs but cannot establish relative relevance, recall, quality, or cost..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: A bounded query task that explicitly requires the PubMed Entrez database or PubMed field-tag semantics and needs provider-ranked PMID plus ESummary metadata output..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · One same-query, same-limit live comparison against the existing Europe PMC query tool at the same recorded UTC timestamp. Both returned five records; the top-five PubMed PMID sets did not overlap. Search quality, recall, completeness, accuracy, latency, reliability, human review time, and cost remain unknown. Comparators: research.search_europe_pmc_query; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_europe_pmc_query_search.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_europe_pmc_query_search.py; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-query-search-real-task-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-pubmed-query-search-c1-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-europe-pmc-query-search-evidence-r0.json; findings: A matched public query returned five PubMed records and five Europe PMC records at one recorded timestamp; provider hit counts and rank order differed.; No exact PMID was shared in the two top-five result lists for this query. This does not show which list is more relevant or complete.; Keep both adapters for their distinct source and query contracts; do not retire either from one task..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · This optional PubMed-specific source adapter adds an explicit PubMed/Entrez query path. The Europe PMC adapter remains in use for its separate source. The matched one-query comparison does not support a replacement.

Select the PubMed adapter only when the task requires PubMed or its query syntax; retain Europe PMC for its distinct provider scope. / Remove this candidate card, adapter, schemas, focused tests, documentation, and its run/C1/route/source evidence; regenerate the identity projection and toolbox page. Retain historical evidence and prior commits..

- Fallback: If the query or contact email cannot be sent to NCBI, do not run the adapter. Report the unavailable scope or use an explicitly permitted alternative source adapter with its own host and limits; preserve the provider-specific source boundary.

### `research.validate_evidence_program_snapshot` · 科研证据计划快照校验器 / Evidence Program snapshot validator

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `toolbox-wrapper-0.1.0; upstream Task103 Evidence Program validator` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `b1abb127207333b53472b48be3fdc7d81b732f1c`.
- Can do: Run the existing standard-library Evidence Program validator against the fixed checked-out evidence-program tree and return a compact summary plus a source-tree digest.
- Good for: Check the fixed repository Evidence Program schemas, recorded preregistration-order metadata, source provenance fields, post-hoc threshold equality, and metric leakage constraints.; Get a compact check/failure count and SHA-256 digest for the entire fixed Evidence Program input tree.
- Not for: Running Crossref, OpenAlex, model, browser, search, or other network queries; the source-acquisition runner is a separate tool with different permissions.; Authenticating source origins, proving every file was committed as claimed, verifying scientific content, deciding truth, or recording independent/human acceptance.; Editing manifests, registrations, run records, thresholds, or the evidence-program tree.
- Use only if: The caller wants to validate the fixed `ignition/evidence-program/` snapshot in the currently checked-out Formal repository.; Python >=3.9 and the local Git CLI are available; Git is used only for repository-root discovery and preregistration ancestor checks.; The caller understands that the result validates recorded structure and consistency only, and that checkout-level Git metadata is read-only input.
- Do not use when: The task asks to fetch or refresh any evidence source; use a separately authorized acquisition workflow with network and output-path controls.; The task requires scientific adjudication, primary-source support review, authorship/authenticity proof, or epistemic acceptance.; The tool router must authorize an exact Git metadata read path; the current route planner cannot represent the containing checkout's `.git` metadata and will fail closed.
- Inputs → outputs: fixed checked-out ignition/evidence-program snapshot plus containing checkout Git metadata for read-only ancestry checks → compact validator status, check count, failure count, and bounded failure details, SHA-256 digest and file count for the complete checked-out Evidence Program tree, validator source SHA-256 and local Git root identity
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_TREE_AND_LOCAL_GIT_METADATA` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/evidence-program/, containing checkout Git metadata (read-only; `git rev-parse` and `git merge-base --is-ancestor` only)` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Writes one compact JSON summary to stdout; no repository files are created or changed., Runs the validator and fixed-argument local Git read commands as child processes; never invokes a shell or a remote Git operation..
- Dependencies: ignition/evidence-program/tools/validate_evidence_program.py, ignition/evidence-program/; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library and local Git CLI; no third-party runtime packages.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · The fixed tree has 31 files and 499 observed checks in the current run, but no calibrated latency or memory profile has been established.
- Alternatives: `ignition.evidence_program.validate_evidence_program.py` (EQUIVALENT_VALIDATION_DIFFERENT_INTERFACE): The upstream validator exposes a configurable root and a verbose per-check log; this wrapper fixes the repository root and summarizes the same validator run..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Validate only the fixed checked-out Evidence Program tree and emit a bounded machine-readable receipt that binds the tree and validator source hashes, without exposing a caller-selected root; use the direct validator when detailed per-check logs or another declared root are needed..
- Comparison evidence: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · One paired run on the same Formal candidate snapshot and Evidence Program tree compared the fixed-root JSON wrapper with its underlying direct validator; this is interface/receipt evidence, not an independent correctness or cost benchmark.
