### `research.verify_crossref_metadata` · Crossref 元数据核验器 / Crossref metadata verifier

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.3.0` · license `Wrapper BUSL-1.1; Crossref bibliographic metadata reuse basis documented at https://www.crossref.org/documentation/retrieve-metadata/.` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; Task 103 Crossref pilot supplies reused normalization helpers, not Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `3e8f0511edbe1e3ff1c80af29ff7ff19d8ed503c`.
- Can do: Compare caller-supplied DOI title/year with Crossref Work metadata and optionally project bounded update relations and deposited references from the same response.
- Good for: Comparing caller-supplied expected DOI title and year with the live Crossref metadata record.; Retaining per-record HTTP outcome, response SHA-256, observed fields, and explicit mismatch or network-failure status.; Checking public bibliographic metadata for a bounded batch without retrieving article content.; Inspecting Crossref-deposited update-to, updated-by, and typed related-item identifiers alongside title/year comparison without making a second request or following related identifiers.; Retrieving up to 25 Crossref-deposited reference citation entries in the same Work response for a known DOI, with explicit field status, provider count, omitted rows, invalid entries, and truncation.
- Not for: Retrieving article full text, abstracts, publisher pages, or arbitrary URLs.; Establishing that a paper is authentic, scientifically correct, or supports a claim.; Treating missing or stale Crossref fields as proof that a source does not exist.; Adding or correcting records in a repository registry.; Treating absent or empty provider relation fields as proof that no correction, update, or related item exists.; Establishing a complete or authoritative bibliography; Crossref reference deposits are optional and may be partial.; Resolving referenced works, following URLs, verifying citation correctness, or assessing scientific support.
- Use only if: The caller has explicitly approved transmission of the DOI identifiers in this invocation and passes --allow-public-network.; A route proposal may include this candidate only when allowed_network_hosts explicitly contains api.crossref.org; the proposal is not a permission grant.; The request contains one to ten unique bare DOI identifiers with caller-supplied expected title and year.; The public Crossref REST API is reachable from the declared environment.
- Do not use when: The DOI or associated task context is confidential or must not be sent to a public service.; Network access is unavailable, disallowed, or not explicitly approved for this invocation.; The task requires article-content review, source authenticity, current retraction adjudication, or scientific assessment.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin object with one to ten unique DOI, expected-title, expected-year records → input-ordered Crossref metadata comparison rows, per-response SHA-256 and explicit HTTP or network outcome summary, bounded update-to, updated-by, and typed relation identifier projections with reported/omitted counts and truncation state, bounded Crossref reference citation projection (up to 25 entries per DOI) with missing/empty/malformed status, provider count, omitted count, invalid-entry count, and truncation state
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_FIXED_HOST_NETWORK_QUERY` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_crossref_metadata_verifier.py, ignition/evidence-program/tools/run_crossref_verification.py` · writes `none` · network `true` hosts `api.crossref.org` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: When explicitly invoked with --allow-public-network, sends at most ten HTTPS GET requests to api.crossref.org with DOI identifiers only; no user title, source_id, credential, or arbitrary URL is transmitted., Writes the JSON result to stdout and validation errors to stderr; does not create or modify repository files., Follows no redirects; uses a 5-second socket inactivity timeout, checks a 7-second response-body budget between and after 8 KiB reads, and caps responses at 256 KiB; records timeout, size, HTTP, and malformed-response failures explicitly..
- Dependencies: ignition/evidence-program/tools/run_crossref_verification.py; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library, Task103 Crossref helper source in the same checkout.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · Crossref documents public REST access without signup. The wrapper permits at most ten serial requests, applies a 5-second socket timeout and a 7-second body-read budget with bounded chunks; no comparable latency, bandwidth, service-availability, or compute-cost profile has been measured. R1 recorded one process wall/child-CPU observation (1547.352/68.352 ms) for one public request; billed cost, bandwidth and latency distribution remain UNKNOWN.
- Alternatives: `research.validate_evidence_program_snapshot` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The local validator checks the Evidence Program structure and recorded provenance but does not retrieve current Crossref metadata.; `research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata` (ALTERNATE_PROVIDER_DIFFERENT_PROVENANCE): OpenAlex can return DOI-resolved title/year plus an OpenAlex Work ID and provider-reported retraction signal. It is a different metadata source and cannot establish the content or status of the Crossref record; one paired DOI comparison is not evidence that either provider replaces the other..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Project source-deposited Crossref reference citation metadata (including optional matched DOIs and bounded citation text) from the same known-DOI Work response.; The current OpenAlex adapter does not expose referenced-work lists, and the PubMed relation tools address update/correction links rather than bibliography entries; no general coverage comparison has been run..
- Comparison evidence: `COMPARISON_UNKNOWN` · One purposively selected real DOI produced a nonempty Crossref reference projection: 40 entries reported, 25 projected, 15 omitted, and truncation=true. This is a single-record observation only; it cannot estimate deposit prevalence, coverage, completeness, correctness, or cross-provider quality. Overall comparison remains COMPARISON_UNKNOWN. Comparators: research.lookup_openalex_work_metadata, research.inspect_pubmed_update_links; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-relations-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-pubmed-matched-relations-c1-evidence-r1.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-references-real-task-evidence-r0.json, ignition/data/operations/toolbox-crossref-references-route-evidence-r0.json; findings: Crossref documents that reference deposits are optional; absence or emptiness does not establish that a work has no references.; The existing OpenAlex candidate returns Work identity, OA, and location projections, while the PubMed relation reader returns Comments/Corrections fields; neither current adapter returns a Crossref-deposited bibliography projection.; This feature uses fields from the same Crossref Work response and does not add a request or permission. Any single DOI result is a positive or negative record-level observation only, not a coverage or quality comparison.; For the one purposively selected record, Crossref reported 40 references and the bounded adapter projected 25, omitted 15, and marked truncation; this does not establish general coverage or completeness..
- Deprecation/retirement: `ACTIVE` · The default-off Crossref candidate has a provider-specific reference projection and distinct update/relation fields. Existing candidates do not provide the same Crossref-deposited citation rows; no same-task replacement evidence supports retiring this adapter or another tool.
- Migration/rollback: Version 0.3.0 adds crossref_reference_metadata to each successful result row while preserving the existing request contract and prior metadata fields. Consumers may ignore the additive result. / Revert the v0.3.0 reference projection to v0.2.0, regenerate identity cards and ignition/TOOLBOX.md, and rerun focused tests. ### `research.verify_offline_source_snapshot_run` · 离线来源快照运行核验器 / Offline Source-Snapshot Run Verifier

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or scientific authority.` · source blob `12e99dee4b64446105f26b5cc8840d3fd78fad55`.
- Can do: Verify the exact regular-file set and per-file sizes and SHA-256 values declared by one saved E37 source-snapshot run receipt under a caller-selected external directory.
- Good for: Checking saved artifacts from the fixed E37 offline source-snapshot adapter against the unkeyed receipt in the same run directory.; Confirming receipt-listed regular-file sizes and SHA-256 values and detecting missing or unlisted files without printing artifact contents.; Reading an E37 run stored outside the current repository checkout when a caller supplies the canonical absolute directory path.
- Not for: Authenticating the receipt, capture origin, publisher, executor, prior run, or artifact provenance.; Proving semantic support, scientific truth, task correctness, or stable state after the check.; Checking FederatedResultReceipt handoffs under the repository-local ignition/ workspace contract.; Reading symlinks or special files, writing or repairing artifacts, using network/search/models, or authorizing execution or handoff.
- Use only if: The directory is a caller-selected canonical absolute path outside and not containing the repository checkout.; The run contains one ignition-source-binding-slot-run-v1 receipt from the fixed e37-source-snapshot-v1 adapter.; The caller accepts an unkeyed SHA-256 comparison as byte-consistency evidence only.
- Do not use when: Receipt authenticity, capture origin, publisher identity, execution history, or semantic/scientific review is required.; The run directory is inside or contains the repository, uses symlinks or non-regular files, or exceeds the declared artifact and byte limits.; The input is a FederatedResultReceipt or a different adapter/schema, or the request expects repair, network action, or authorization.
- Inputs → outputs: One canonical absolute filesystem path to a caller-selected external E37 run directory containing receipt.json and its receipt-listed files. → One JSON result with per-artifact byte and digest statuses, missing and unlisted paths, bounded read counts, explicit unkeyed-receipt limitations, and execution_authorized=false.
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_BOUNDED_CALLER_SELECTED_EXTERNAL_RUN_TREE` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_verify_offline_source_snapshot_run.py` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads the selected receipt and bounded regular-file contents using descriptor-relative no-follow opens; prints one bounded JSON result to stdout and writes no files..
- Dependencies: ignition/research_workflow/slot.py; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.10 standard library., POSIX O_NOFOLLOW, O_DIRECTORY, and dir_fd support; unsupported platforms fail closed..
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No CPU, memory, latency, or matched cost comparison has been measured; each artifact is limited to 64 MiB, total artifact reads to 128 MiB, and the receipt to 1 MiB.
- Alternatives: `collaboration.verify_handoff_artifact_files` (SPECIALIZED_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The existing verifier accepts a typed FederatedResultReceipt under a repository-local ignition/ workspace; it rejects the E37 receipt schema and cannot inspect a caller-selected external run directory..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: Check byte-level integrity and exact file membership for an E37 run directory outside the repository using that run’s receipt.; Detect post-run tampering, omission, or unlisted files while keeping artifact contents out of stdout and leaving the receipt explicitly unauthenticated..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · A low-cost same-input contract comparison supplied an E37 receipt to the existing Federation byte verifier; that verifier returned INPUT_INVALID with SOURCE_RECEIPT_INVALID. The new verifier accepted the fixed E37 schema and inspected its caller-selected external run tree. This establishes a schema/root boundary only; no comparative quality, broad reliability, authenticity, or cost advantage is established. Comparators: collaboration.verify_handoff_artifact_files; implementation refs: ignition/tools/toolbox_verify_handoff_artifact_files.py; refs: ignition/tests/test_toolbox_source_snapshot_run_verifier.py, ignition/docs/operations/toolbox-verify-offline-source-snapshot-run.md; findings: The existing verifier checks a different FederatedResultReceipt contract and rejects the E37 receipt; it remains appropriate for repository-local Federation handoffs.; The new verifier covers caller-selected external E37 run trees and exact file sets, so the tools remain specialized for different declared inputs and task boundaries.; No replacement or retirement is supported; measured performance, costs, provenance, receipt authenticity, and scientific validity remain unknown..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · The contract comparison establishes a distinct E37 external-run use case and does not support replacing or retiring the existing Federation receipt verifier.
- Migration/rollback: Keep collaboration.verify_handoff_artifact_files for FederatedResultReceipt inputs under ignition/; use this candidate only for fixed E37 receipt outputs in caller-selected external directories.; No Current registry operation is migrated and no permission or scientific status is inherited by adding this optional card. / Remove this optional card, verifier, tests, route evidence, and guide from the isolated branch, then regenerate identity projections; preserve historical comparison and implementation evidence..

- Good for: Produce the repository's deterministic typed component/connection/boundary IR as stdout JSON for a separately selected downstream consumer.; Inspect canonical architecture node and edge coverage together with the exact source-file digests and caller-supplied revision string.
- Not for: Rendering an image, invoking Archify, validating provider delivery, or claiming visual usefulness or visual review.; Changing canonical architecture files, repairing the separately deferred Task150 geometry delta, or publishing a derived artifact.; Establishing architecture truth, Current integration, provider admission, or resolution of the caller-supplied revision to the checked-out Git HEAD.
- Use only if: The canonical architecture, current system map, and Task149 adapter source are present in the checked-out Ignition repository and pass fixed-path safety checks.; The caller supplies an exact lowercase 40-character SHA string; the tool records that string but does not resolve or verify it against Git.; The request is at most 4 KiB and both fixed canonical JSON sources are at most 1 MiB.
- Do not use when: The user needs a rendered or visually reviewed system map, fresh provider evidence, or a Current provider integration.; The requested input requires arbitrary source paths or the caller needs the formal revision independently resolved.; The task requires the Task150 geometry repair or any aesthetic, architecture-truth, Owner, or scientific claim.

### `writing.preflight_cognitive_migration_revision` · 认知迁移编辑修订预检 / Cognitive-migration editorial revision preflight

- State: `EXPERIMENTAL_AVAILABLE` / `EXPERIMENTAL` · version `0.1.0` · license `BUSL-1.1` · maintainer `Ignition repository maintainers; this field does not grant Owner or publication authority.` · source blob `e7179d56964b3a3b0c29b29b5776ff6a5efe4d7f`.
- Can do: Build a bounded editorial proposal by reordering caller-supplied paragraphs verbatim, checking declared excerpt-to-paragraph associations, protecting evidence/counterargument/affected-person paragraphs, and reporting nonbinding structural diagnostics.
- Good for: Producing a local proposed order for explicitly selected source paragraphs without changing their wording.; Checking that caller-declared exact excerpt strings occur in their assigned paragraphs and echoing caller-supplied source references without inspecting those sources; keeping anchored, counterargument, and affected-person paragraphs in a proposal.; Reviewing caller-declared cognitive-move variety, concept-to-anchor mapping, paragraph lengths, and repeated surface patterns as nonbinding editorial prompts.
- Not for: Generating, paraphrasing, deleting or fact-checking prose; selecting an editorial direction on the author's behalf; or publishing or accepting a work.; Verifying that a source reference is real, that an excerpt is faithfully transcribed, that a factual claim is true, or that a claim stays within its ceiling.; Treating one paragraph-count range, cognitive-move set, surface pattern, readability signal, or proposal status as a universal writing rule or quality judgment.
- Use only if: The caller supplies one UTF-8 JSON request no larger than 1,000,000 bytes with exact source paragraphs, declared anchor excerpts and references, a claim ceiling, reader-shift fields, and the desired paragraph order.; The caller accepts that caller-declared evidence, counterargument, affected-person, and source-anchor paragraphs cannot be omitted from the generated proposal.; The result is reviewed by the author for factual support, scope, voice, and suitability before any use.
- Do not use when: The task requires fresh sources, source authentication, factual or causal adjudication, or independent claim-ceiling review.; The caller expects automatic paraphrase, paragraph selection, style imitation, publication approval, or a finished article.; The source draft or editorial decision is not authorized for local processing.
- Inputs → outputs: UTF-8 JSON stdin containing 1–80 identified paragraphs, declared exact anchors and source-reference strings, before/after reader-shift statements, a caller-supplied claim ceiling, and an ordered selection (maximum 1,000,000 bytes) → deterministic JSON review receipt with wrapper, source-paragraph, and proposal SHA-256, declared move and concept-anchor coverage, paragraph diagnostics, and explicit unverified-source boundaries, optional proposed text formed only by joining caller-selected paragraph text verbatim in caller order; withheld if protected content is omitted
- Permissions: `READ_ONLY_RUN` / `READ_ONLY_BOUNDED_STDIN_AND_SELF_HASH` · explicit Owner authorization required `false` · reads `ignition/tools/toolbox_cognitive_migration_revision.py` · writes `none` · network `false` hosts `none` · secrets `false` · billing `false` · external actions `false.
- Side effects: Reads one bounded JSON request from stdin and its own regular wrapper file for SHA-256, then writes a deterministic receipt to stdout; it creates no files and does not modify the repository., When protected content is omitted, returns a blocker and no proposal text; when safe unanchored paragraphs are omitted, reports their IDs for human review..
- Dependencies: ignition/tools/toolbox_cognitive_migration_revision.py; runtime dependencies: Python >=3.9 standard library.
- Cost: `UNKNOWN` · No comparable latency or memory profile has been measured; input is capped at 1,000,000 bytes and processing is local deterministic text/JSON work.
- Alternatives: `writing.apply_editorial_method` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The Current registered operation describes the broader writing method; this optional CLI only checks caller annotations and reorders existing paragraphs without applying generative or editorial judgment.; `writing.validate_publication_surface` (COMPLEMENTARY_NOT_EQUIVALENT): The Current registered operation covers publication-surface validation; this CLI produces a local editorial structure proposal and does not validate publication readiness..
- Replacement candidates: none established.
- Unique remaining use cases: For an already-written draft supplied as structured paragraphs, produce a caller-ordered verbatim reorder with excerpt-to-paragraph checks and fail-closed protection of declared evidence, counterargument, and affected-person paragraphs; this exact contract is absent from the two compared Current writing operations..
- Comparison evidence: `SPECIALIZED_NO_REPLACEMENT_ESTABLISHED` · Exact current operation input/output declarations plus one bounded synthetic CLI and route comparison; not a paired human writing-quality, cost, reliability, or real-task benchmark. Comparators: writing.apply_editorial_method, writing.validate_publication_surface; implementation refs: none recorded; refs: ignition/data/operations/ignition-operation-capability-registry-r1.json, ignition/packs/writing/manifest.json, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_cognitive_migration_revision.py, ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py; findings: writing.apply_editorial_method declares source-bounded L6 transformation and draft/provenance outputs; this candidate never rewrites prose and cannot cover those generation uses.; writing.validate_publication_surface declares publication-surface and provenance validation; this candidate neither renders nor validates a publication surface.; Exact-contract route trials selected each Current operation for its own request; paragraph reordering was INCOMPLETE by default and selected this candidate only after explicit allowlisting, with execution_authorized=false.; The synthetic CLI returned a verbatim reorder and retained designated counterargument and affected-person paragraphs; no paired real-task quality or cost advantage is established..
- Deprecation/retirement: `SPECIALIZED` · Keep as a default-off structural editing option; evidence does not support replacing either Current writing operation or promoting this candidate to ordinary default routing.
- Migration/rollback: Use only for explicit structured paragraph reordering; keep generative editing and publication-surface validation on their separately declared Current operations. / Remove this candidate entry and wrapper from the isolated toolbox branch, rebuild the identity-card and human-directory projections, and retain this comparison evidence and source refs..
- Invocation example: `python3 -B ignition/tools/toolbox_cognitive_migration_revision.py --stdin < request.json`
- Fallback: If the proposal is blocked or the tool is unavailable, preserve the full draft and reorder paragraphs manually; review all factual claims and source links against the original evidence.
- Evidence: `REAL_TOOL_TEST` · source refs `ignition/tools/toolbox_cognitive_migration_revision.py` (BOUNDED_VERBATIM_EDITORIAL_PROPOSAL_ENTRYPOINT), `ignition/docs/publication/zhiyuan-writing-method.md` (CURRENT_GENERATIVE_METHOD_BOUNDARY), `ignition/templates/publication/zhiyuan-writing-spec.md` (CURRENT_WRITING_SPEC_BOUNDARY), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_cognitive_migration_revision.py` (FOCUSED_SYNTHETIC_AND_NEGATIVE_CONTROLS), `ignition/data/operations/ignition-operation-capability-registry-r1.json` (BOUNDED_COMPARISON_EVIDENCE), `ignition/packs/writing/manifest.json` (BOUNDED_COMPARISON_EVIDENCE), `ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py` (BOUNDED_COMPARISON_EVIDENCE); focused checks [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_cognitive_migration_revision.py](tests/test_toolbox_cognitive_migration_revision.py), [ignition/tests/test_toolbox_routing.py](tests/test_toolbox_routing.py); negative controls: Omitting any declared evidence-anchor, counterargument, or affected-person paragraph withholds proposal text and reports every protected paragraph ID.; An anchor excerpt absent from its attached paragraph, a missing concept anchor, an unknown selected paragraph, duplicate JSON keys, invalid input, and oversized requests fail closed without a partial stdout receipt.; Repeated contrast/deepening patterns and an incomplete cognitive-move set are review advisories; phrases are not banned and declared move labels are never independently inferred from prose.; Repeated invocations on identical input produce identical JSON, and unsupported output-path options are rejected; no file, network, secret, billing, or external-action effect is exposed..

- Claim ceiling: Deterministic editorial structure proposal over caller-supplied paragraphs only.
